      "message": "chore: release 2026.10.1 (#13883)\n\n\nhttps://entire.io/gh/jdx/mise/trails/378\n\n\n### 🐛 Bug Fixes\n\n- **(aqua)** invalidate incompatible compiled registry caches by @jdx in\n[#13884](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13884)\n- **(brew-cask)** stop reinstalling pkg casks whose uninstall ids span\narchitectures by @jdx in\n[#13893](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13893)\n- **(completions)** complete `:task` shorthand for the current config\nroot by @pikeas in [#13882](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13882)\n- **(daemons)** start task daemons without a shell so they work on\nWindows by @JamBalaya56562 in\n[#13714](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13714)\n- **(daemons)** render env and vars in daemon commands at launch by @jdx\nin [#13894](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13894)\n- **(github)** never auto-select metadata assets as the tool by @jdx in\n[#13908](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13908)\n- **(lock)** prune unbound entries superseded by a bound entry by @jdx\nin [#13909](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13909)\n- **(packslip)** retry missing declared skills by @jdx in\n[#13885](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13885)\n- **(plugins)** update plugin repos whose remote is not named origin by\n@jdx in [#13914](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13914)\n- **(release)** back up alpine bump to GitHub and allow SSH push to\nGitLab by @jdx in [#13892](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13892)\n- **(rust)** upgrade stable and beta when rustup reports an update by\n@jdx in [#13898](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13898)\n- **(rust)** honor mise.lock for core:rust installs by @jdx in\n[#13915](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13915)\n- **(shim)** stop native shim copies recursing through mise x by\n@JamBalaya56562 in [#13681](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13681)\n- **(shim)** forward Node IPC through Windows shims by @jdx in\n[#13903](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13903)\n- **(skills)** show $HOME as ~ in skills sync output by @jdx in\n[#13910](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13910)\n- **(task)** stop running tasks when mise run --timeout expires by\n@Marukome0743 in [#13876](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13876)\n- **(task)** let tasks shut down after a single Ctrl-C by @jdx in\n[#13904](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13904)\n\n### 📚 Documentation\n\n- **(contributing)** make the restricted AI reply policy and instant ban\nunmissable by @jdx in [#13886](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13886)\n- replace the landing-page showreel's synthesized music with a Suno\ntrack by @jdx in [#13900](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13900)\n- stop the showreel's dark gradients from banding into rings by @jdx in\n[#13902](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13902)\n- tighten the landing-page video and smooth its soundtrack by @jdx in\n[#13916](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13916)\n- retime the mise run captions to the vocal by @jdx in\n[#13917](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13917)\n\n### 🧪 Testing\n\n- **(zig)** follow indexed master version by @jdx in\n[#13911](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13911)\n\n### Ci\n\n- **(release)** arm release PR after daily cutoff by @jdx in\n[#13905](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13905)\n- give windows-unit enough time to save its cache by @jdx in\n[#13874](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13874)\n- run trusted Windows jobs on a self-hosted runner by @jdx in\n[#13888](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13888)\n- build real Windows releases on a dedicated release runner by @jdx in\n[#13899](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13899)\n\n## 📦 Aqua Registry Updates\n\n### New Packages (2)\n\n-\n[`agentgateway/agentgateway/agctl`](https://github.com/agentgateway/agentgateway)\n-\n[`agentgateway/agentgateway/agentgateway`](https://github.com/agentgateway/agentgateway)",

**(daemons)** start task daemons without a shell so they work on\nWindows by @JamBalaya56562 in\n[#13714](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13714)\n- **(daemons)** render env and vars in daemon commands at launch by @jdx\nin [#13894](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13894)\n- **(github)** never auto-select metadata assets as the tool by @jdx in\n[#13908](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13908)\n- **(lock)** prune unbound entries superseded by a bound entry by @jdx\nin [#13909](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13909)\n- **(packslip)** retry missing declared skills by @jdx in\n[#13885](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13885)\n- **(plugins)** update plugin repos whose remote is not named origin by\n@jdx in [#13914](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13914)\n- **(release)** back up alpine bump to GitHub and allow SSH push to\nGitLab by @jdx in [#13892](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13892)\n- **(rust)** upgrade stable and beta when rustup reports an update by\n@jdx in [#13898](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13898)\n- **(rust)** honor mise.lock for core:rust installs by @jdx in\n[#13915](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13915)\n- **(shim)** stop native shim copies recursing through mise x by\n@JamBalaya56562 in [#13681](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13681)\n- **(shim)** forward Node IPC through Windows shims by @jdx in\n[#13903](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13903)\n- **(skills)** show $HOME as ~ in skills sync output by @jdx in\n[#13910](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13910)\n- **(task)** stop running tasks when mise run --timeout expires by\n@Marukome0743 in [#13876](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13876)\n- **(task)** let tasks shut down after a single Ctrl-C by @jdx in\n[#13904](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13904)\n\n### 📚 Documentation\n\n- **(contributing)** make the restricted AI reply policy and instant ban\nunmissable by @jdx in [#13886](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13886)\n- replace the landing-page showreel's synthesized music with a Suno\ntrack by @jdx in [#13900](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13900)\n- stop the showreel's dark gradients from banding into rings by @jdx in\n[#13902](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13902)\n- tighten the landing-page video and smooth its soundtrack by @jdx in\n[#13916](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13916)\n- retime the mise run captions to the vocal by @jdx in\n[#13917](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13917)\n\n### 🧪 Testing\n\n- **(zig)** follow indexed master version by @jdx in\n[#13911](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13911)\n\n### Ci\n\n- **(release)** arm release PR after daily cutoff by @jdx in\n[#13905](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13905)\n- give windows-unit enough time to save its cache by @jdx in\n[#13874](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13874)\n- run trusted Windows jobs on a self-hosted runner by @jdx in\n[#13888](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13888)\n- build real Windows releases on a dedicated release runner by @jdx in\n[#13899](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/13899)\n\n## 📦 Aqua Registry Updates\n\n### New Packages (2)\n\n-\n[`agentgateway/agentgateway/agctl`](https://github.com/agentgateway/agentgateway)\n-\n[`agentgateway/agentgateway/agentgateway`](https://github.com/agentgateway/agentgateway)",

      }

      "sha": "ded5c19c72591fdf80490d1dcc2994d2384b6202",
      "node_id": "C_kwDOIvux3toAKGRlZDVjMTljNzI1OTFmZGY4MDQ5MGQxZGNjMjk5NGQyMzg0YjYyMDI",
      "commit": {
        "author": {
          "name": "jdx",
          "email": "216188+jdx@users.noreply.github.com",

        "committer": {

          "date": "2026-10-03T12:25:45Z"
        },
        "message": "fix(activate): keep loading trusted configs when a project config is untrusted (#13919)\n\n\nhttps://entire.io/gh/jdx/mise/trails/401\n\n\n## Problem\n\nOpening a shell in a project whose \\`mise.toml\\` is not yet trusted made\n\\`mise hook-env\\` fail entirely, so tools and env from the trusted\nglobal config were not applied (e.g. \\`fish: Unknown command: foo\\`):\n\n\\`\\`\\`\nmise WARN ~/project/mise.toml is not trusted, run \\`mise trust\\` to\nenable it\n\\`\\`\\`\n\n## Fix\n\n\\`hook-env\\` now skips untrusted configs and keeps loading the rest,\nstill printing the same one-time warning. Explicit commands (\\`mise\nrun\\`, \\`mise x\\`, ...) behave as before. The warning marker is no\nlonger cleared while a config is skipped, so the warning still shows\nonce per directory.\n\nFixes #13918\n\n## Tests\n\n- New \\`e2e/cli/test_hook_env_untrusted_keeps_global\\`: global env is\nemitted, local env is not, warning is shown.\n- Existing trust-warning e2e tests still pass.\n\n*AI-assisted — Tool: Claude Code; model: anthropic/claude-sonnet-5-5;\nversion: unavailable.*\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\n\n\n---\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> **Medium Risk**\n> Changes shell activation and config-load trust handling on every\n`cd`/prompt, but scope is limited to `hook-env` and preserves strict\ntrust for explicit commands.\n> \n> **Overview**\n> **`mise hook-env` no longer aborts** when it hits an untrusted project\nconfig. It **skips** those files, still applies env/tools from\n**trusted** layers (e.g. global `~/.config/mise`), and prints the same\nstderr warning instead of exiting with failure.\n> \n> Config loading gains a **`hook-env`-only** mode\n(`skip_untrusted_configs`) that records skipped paths and continues;\n**`mise run` / `mise x` and other explicit commands are unchanged** and\nstill error on untrusted configs.\n> \n> Warning dedup is updated so the shell marker covers **all** skipped\nconfigs in the directory—adding another untrusted file (e.g.\n`mise.local.toml`) can warn again even if the first file was already\nreported.\n> \n> E2E: new **`test_hook_env_untrusted_keeps_global`**;\n**`test_local_settings_trust_controls`** now expects skip-with-warning,\nnot hard failure.\n> \n> Reviewed by [Cursor Bugbot](https://cursor.com/bugbot) for commit\ncb24afd24e0e12ed17d5ba1d7a5c130c7b01584e. Bugbot is set up for automated\ncode reviews on this repo. Configure\n[here](https://www.cursor.com/dashboard/bugbot).\n\n\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **Bug Fixes**\n* `mise hook-env` now continues applying environment variables from\ntrusted configuration when a project configuration is untrusted, instead\nof stopping with an error.\n* Untrusted configuration files are skipped, and a warning identifies\neach skipped file. Variables from skipped files are not applied, while\ntrusted configuration continues to load. Other configuration errors\nstill prevent loading.\n* Warnings are shown for each untrusted configuration encountered,\nincluding additional local configuration files.\n\n\n---------\n\nCo-authored-by: Claude Sonnet 5.5 ",

Bugbot is set up for automated\ncode reviews on this repo. Configure\n[here](https://www.cursor.com/dashboard/bugbot).\n\n\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **Bug Fixes**\n* `mise hook-env` now continues applying environment variables from\ntrusted configuration when a project configuration is untrusted, instead\nof stopping with an error.\n* Untrusted configuration files are skipped, and a warning identifies\neach skipped file.     {
      "sha": "e78ea41f00144f6b14867d8d4fecf4c422e47c33",
      "node_id": "C_kwDOIvux3toAKGU3OGVhNDFmMDAxNDRmNmIxNDg2N2Q4ZDRmZWNmNGM0MjJlNDdjMzM",
      "commit": {
        "author": {
          "name": "jdx",
          "email": "216188+jdx@users.noreply.github.com",
          "date": "2026-10-03T13:19:20Z"
        },
        "committer": {
          "name": "GitHub",
          "email": "noreply@github.com",
          "date": "2026-10-03T13:19:20Z"
        },
        "message": "feat(backend): add experimental spinel backend (#13922)\n\n\nhttps://entire.io/gh/jdx/mise/trails/404\n\n\nAdds an experimental `spinel:` backend that compiles a Ruby command-line\ntool from a GitHub repository into a native executable with\n[Spinel](https://github.com/matz/spinel), Matz's Ruby AOT compiler\n(requested in discussion 13301).\n\n```toml\n[tools.\"spinel:tobi/try\"]\nversion = \"1.10.1\"\nentrypoint = \"try.rb\"\nbin = \"try\"\ntag_prefix = \"v\"\n```\n\n```\n$ mise install && mise x -- try --help\ntry v1.10.1 - ephemeral workspace manager\n```\n\n`mise ls-remote spinel:tobi/try` lists git tags through `git ls-remote`,\nso it never touches the GitHub API. Options are `entrypoint`, `bin`,\n`tag_prefix`, `source_ref` and `spinel`. The backend needs the `spinel`\ncompiler on `PATH`, or its path in the `spinel` option. Spinel ships as\nsource, so mise does not install it yet. macOS and Linux only.\n\n## Status\n\nWe're doing this, but only because it's for Matz. It is gated behind\n`experimental`, and it may not survive. If it turns into a large\nmaintenance burden, or Spinel doesn't take off, it will be removed. The\ndocs page says the same.\n\nBased on\n[nateberkopec/mise-backend-spinel](https://github.com/nateberkopec/mise-backend-spinel);\nNate is credited as co-author.\n\n## Testing\n\nI built Spinel at the commit Nate's README pins, then ran `mise install`\nand `mise x -- try --help` against `tobi/try`. Unit tests cover tag\nparsing and option validation. Clippy with `-D warnings` is clean. `mise\nrun lint-fix` fails locally on the repo's existing `package.homepage`\nmanifest warnings, so lint did not run; CI will.\n\n*AI-assisted — Tool: Claude Code; model: anthropic/claude-sonnet-5-5;\nversion: 2.1.286.*\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\n\n\n---\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> **Medium Risk**\n> Install runs git and an external compiler on user-supplied repos;\ninputs are validated and the feature is experimental-only, but failed or\nmalicious source could still affect the build environment.\n> \n> **Overview**\n> Adds an **experimental** `spinel:` installation backend that builds a\nRuby CLI from a GitHub repo (`owner/repo`) into a standalone native\nbinary via the external Spinel AOT compiler, without bundling Ruby or\nSpinel in the output.\n> \n> Install flow shallow-fetches a tag or `source_ref` with `git`, runs\nthe configured `spinel` binary on `entrypoint`, and writes the result to\n`bin/`. Remote versions come from `git ls-remote` tags (optional\n`tag_prefix`), not the GitHub API. Tool options include `entrypoint`,\n`bin`, `tag_prefix`, `source_ref`, and `spinel` (install-time only).\n> \n> Wiring updates register `BackendType::Spinel`, hook it into backend\ndispatch and `mise edit`, extend the registry JSON schema, and add\ndocs/sidebar/`llms.txt` entries. Use requires `mise settings\nexperimental=true`; Windows is rejected at install time.\n> \n> Reviewed by [Cursor Bugbot](https://cursor.com/bugbot) for commit\n1fbcbc3c8d8eab0ecce48d1782e4a485959ac93c. Bugbot is set up for automated\ncode reviews on this repo. Configure\n[here](https://www.cursor.com/dashboard/bugbot).\n\n\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **New Features**\n* Added an experimental Spinel backend that compiles Ruby command-line\ntools from GitHub repositories into native executables.\n* Added Spinel to the backend list and documentation, with setup\nrequirements, configuration options, version selection from Git tags or\ncommit references, and platform limitations. Only a subset of Ruby is\nsupported, and Windows installation is unavailable.\n\n\n---------\n\nCo-authored-by: Nate Berkopec <845662+nateberkopec@users.noreply.github.com>\nCo-authored-by: Claude Sonnet 5.5 ",

        },
        "url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/git/commits/e78ea41f00144f6b14867d8d4fecf4c422e47c33",
        "comment_count": 0,
        "verification": {
          "verified": true,
          "reason": "valid",
          "signature": "-----BEGIN PGP SIGNATURE-----\n\nwsFcBAABCAAQBQJqwQDYCRC1aQ7uu5UhlAAADZsQAIyxU/1hYpwD8qEhZGEdFCsS\nWgFgZ4QglWRuGPPdCXMK0AL8fx4ZuuD8xEIMLCxYjHxYe9qy5SewUkWM38l9vwrg\nUztti/CY3d4xv1tWPdNmsVpikSoCtBl8Nx7I+qjDkRivPK+ErUTgo/z0leRPcevj\nKHkDMO/Sap42BkbakrWxSK13lYr3lWVAZQcjHKKuim6YoHX6P09BWhRnLZ3c9zJo\nlq4ESzYjQpa/EZk0t7KlgWcecKV50mzx1SSysPUvbmbx/jZ8hzT7P7RVztAFb5my\nE0jr7/A+a7fAmZ1EgWKWGf8uYB/+vXA9VhyocdTwf+0Y3BtOswTU3Mqcn7HU4dh1\n2x9VhdzJtS3zmpPgpCfCRFSnCoEAn3g+dtcHHZYR2bQX6hFFML21j/u66+CyreWO\nnvM5cCrHeDQ4n5vdM2T4WMVEy/fCWIOukP6p58tfl24LTRDHK3bVfByamsK2FbY+\nqegd7W3C7mf1zf1YDRdl8uIyY/YH9E/vtDF9H5egiO5k5WVdo0T001dI/PuyZBsi\nZwEWP8mmQDWOiAYXOpCj5UjgK1OsSVCT8cNnGzg4rf64Q6ifv0Vj00mOV8DAkzYj\n98QiAmBC/htcjpIdVVM2euiD1NcP77rUE+wWa05BleGm2xbx9ngeg5KGVheB1QC1\nAOOEHWuHQ+nMpJOq0HNC\n=kSNp\n-----END PGP SIGNATURE-----\n",
          "payload": "tree cdb4e4e9228eb93880f5f55fe08055b04584a35b\nparent ded5c19c72591fdf80490d1dcc2994d2384b6202\nauthor jdx <216188+jdx@users.noreply.github.com> 1791033560 -0500\ncommitter GitHub  1791033560 -0500\n\nfeat(backend): add experimental spinel backend (#13922)\n\n\nhttps://entire.io/gh/jdx/mise/trails/404\n\n\nAdds an experimental `spinel:` backend that compiles a Ruby command-line\ntool from a GitHub repository into a native executable with\n[Spinel](https://github.com/matz/spinel), Matz's Ruby AOT compiler\n(requested in discussion 13301).\n\n```toml\n[tools.\"spinel:tobi/try\"]\nversion = \"1.10.1\"\nentrypoint = \"try.rb\"\nbin = \"try\"\ntag_prefix = \"v\"\n```\n\n```\n$ mise install && mise x -- try --help\ntry v1.10.1 - ephemeral workspace manager\n```\n\n`mise ls-remote spinel:tobi/try` lists git tags through `git ls-remote`,\nso it never touches the GitHub API. Options are `entrypoint`, `bin`,\n`tag_prefix`, `source_ref` and `spinel`. The backend needs the `spinel`\ncompiler on `PATH`, or its path in the `spinel` option. Spinel ships as\nsource, so mise does not install it yet. macOS and Linux only.\n\n## Status\n\nWe're doing this, but only because it's for Matz. It is gated behind\n`experimental`, and it may not survive. If it turns into a large\nmaintenance burden, or Spinel doesn't take off, it will be removed. The\ndocs page says the same.\n\nBased on\n[nateberkopec/mise-backend-spinel](https://github.com/nateberkopec/mise-backend-spinel);\nNate is credited as co-author.\n\n## Testing\n\nI built Spinel at the commit Nate's README pins, then ran `mise install`\nand `mise x -- try --help` against `tobi/try`. Unit tests cover tag\nparsing and option validation. Clippy with `-D warnings` is clean. `mise\nrun lint-fix` fails locally on the repo's existing `package.homepage`\nmanifest warnings, so lint did not run; CI will.\n\n*AI-assisted — Tool: Claude Code; model: anthropic/claude-sonnet-5-5;\nversion: 2.1.286.*\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\n\n\n---\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> **Medium Risk**\n> Install runs git and an external compiler on user-supplied repos;\ninputs are validated and the feature is experimental-only, but failed or\nmalicious source could still affect the build environment.\n> \n> **Overview**\n> Adds an **experimental** `spinel:` installation backend that builds a\nRuby CLI from a GitHub repo (`owner/repo`) into a standalone native\nbinary via the external Spinel AOT compiler, without bundling Ruby or\nSpinel in the output.\n> \n> Install flow shallow-fetches a tag or `source_ref` with `git`, runs\nthe configured `spinel` binary on `entrypoint`, and writes the result to\n`bin/`. Remote versions come from `git ls-remote` tags (optional\n`tag_prefix`), not the GitHub API. Tool options include `entrypoint`,\n`bin`, `tag_prefix`, `source_ref`, and `spinel` (install-time only).\n> \n> Wiring updates register `BackendType::Spinel`, hook it into backend\ndispatch and `mise edit`, extend the registry JSON schema, and add\ndocs/sidebar/`llms.txt` entries. Use requires `mise settings\nexperimental=true`; Windows is rejected at install time.\n> \n> Reviewed by [Cursor Bugbot](https://cursor.com/bugbot) for commit\n1fbcbc3c8d8eab0ecce48d1782e4a485959ac93c. Bugbot is set up for automated\ncode reviews on this repo. Configure\n[here](https://www.cursor.com/dashboard/bugbot).\n\n\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **New Features**\n* Added an experimental Spinel backend that compiles Ruby command-line\ntools from GitHub repositories into native executables.\n* Added Spinel to the backend list and documentation, with setup\nrequirements, configuration options, version selection from Git tags or\ncommit references, and platform limitations. Only a subset of Ruby is\nsupported, and Windows installation is unavailable.\n\n\n---------\n\nCo-authored-by: Nate Berkopec <845662+nateberkopec@users.noreply.github.com>\nCo-authored-by: Claude Sonnet 5.5 ",
          "verified_at": "2026-10-03T13:19:21Z"
        }

      "commit": {
        "author": {
          "name": "jdx",
          "email": "216188+jdx@users.noreply.github.com",
          "date": "2026-10-03T13:19:41Z"

        "committer": {
          "name": "GitHub",
          "email": "noreply@github.com",
          "date": "2026-10-03T13:19:41Z"
        },
        "message": "chore: add hk pre-commit hook and drop redundant crate homepages (#13920)\n\n\nhttps://entire.io/gh/jdx/mise/trails/402\n\n\n`hk.pkl` defined only `fix` and `check` hooks, so `hk install` set up no\ngit hooks and lint ran only when invoked by hand. Contributors could\ncommit code that CI then rejected.\n\nThis adds a `pre-commit` hook (`fix = true`, `stash = \"git\"`) that runs\nthe same linters as `fix`.\n\nIt also removes `package.homepage` from the ten workspace crates where\nit duplicated `package.repository`. Recent cargo warns about the\nredundancy, and with `build.warnings = \"deny\"` that made `cargo-check`\n(and so `mise run lint`) fail locally. `aqua-registry` and\n`mise-brew-relocation` keep their distinct `homepage`.\n\n*AI-assisted — Tool: Claude Code; model: anthropic/claude-sonnet-5-5;\nversion: unavailable.*\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\n\n\n---\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> **Low Risk**\n> Developer workflow and package metadata only; no runtime CLI or\nsecurity behavior changes.\n> \n> **Overview**\n> Adds a **git `pre-commit` hook** in `hk.pkl` that runs the same linter\n`fix` steps as manual `hk run fix`, with `stash = \"git\"` so unstaged\nwork is preserved. **`hk install --mise`** now installs that hook so\nfixes run before commits instead of only when contributors remember\n`mise run lint-fix`.\n> \n> **Contributor docs** (`AGENTS.md`, `docs/contributing.md`) are updated\nto describe the hook and how it relates to `mise run lint` / `lint-fix`\n(including the Windows `lint-fix` limitation noted in `AGENTS.md`).\n> \n> Removes **`package.homepage`** from ten workspace crates where it\nduplicated `package.repository` (`https://github.com/jdx/mise`),\navoiding Cargo redundancy warnings that fail local `cargo-check` under\n`build.warnings = \"deny\"`. Crates with a distinct homepage (e.g.\n`aqua-registry`, `mise-brew-relocation`) are unchanged.\n> \n> Reviewed by [Cursor Bugbot](https://cursor.com/bugbot) for commit\n9132e59a9363d2e22dea66de04ea1447492cedeb. Bugbot is set up for automated\ncode reviews on this repo. Configure\n[here](https://www.cursor.com/dashboard/bugbot).\n\n\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **Chores**\n* Removed homepage links from package metadata; repository links remain\nwhere provided.\n* **Documentation**\n* Updated contribution guidance with instructions for installing the\npre-commit hook, which runs linters with automatic fixes before commits\nand preserves unstaged changes.\n  * Clarified how to run lint checks and apply fixes manually.\n\n\n---------\n\nCo-authored-by: Claude Sonnet 5.5 ",

    },
    {
      "sha": "ebd2cd359c63d2a9645f80d1bb71afceff1b49fe",
      "node_id": "C_kwDOIvux3toAKGViZDJjZDM1OWM2M2QyYTk2NDVmODBkMWJiNzFhZmNlZmYxYjQ5ZmU",
      "commit": {
        "author": {
          "name": "jdx",
          "email": "216188+jdx@users.noreply.github.com",
          "date": "2026-10-03T14:32:04Z"
        },
        "committer": {
          "name": "GitHub",
          "email": "noreply@github.com",
          "date": "2026-10-03T14:32:04Z"
        },
        "message": "fix(config): report a failed settings reload instead of aborting (#13925)\n\nSeveral commands rebuild settings partway through a run: `mise install`,\n`mise upgrade`, `mise use`, `mise uninstall`, `mise prune` and friends\nafter they change what is installed, and `mise ls-remote --prerelease` /\n`--no-versions-host` to apply the flag. That rebuild was lazy. The\ncached settings were dropped and the next `Settings::get()` loaded them\nagain, and if that load failed there was nothing to return the error to,\nso `Settings::get()` panicked. Release builds are compiled with `panic =\n\"abort\"`, so a settings problem ended the command with SIGABRT and a\ncore dump instead of an error message.\n\nThis is the crash in #13923: `mise install\ngithub:can1357/oh-my-pi@latest` for a tool that was already installed\naborted on the main thread, and the only clue left in the core was a\npanic location inside the settings loader. The one-off trigger there is\nstill unknown, but the abort is what turned it from an error into a\ncrash.\n\nA reload can only fail when one of its inputs changed since startup, so\na deliberate trigger is contrived. A `preinstall` hook that removes the\ndirectory `--cd` pointed at does it: re-applying `--cd` during the\nreload fails.\n\n`sub/mise.toml`:\n\n```toml\n[hooks]\npreinstall = \"rm -rf $PWD\"\n```\n\nBefore (2026.9.17 release build; paths shortened):\n\n```\n$ mise --cd sub install age@1.3.2\nmise ⇢ age@1.3.2  7ms · already installed\nmise ████████████████ 1/1 · installed 0 tools · 1 already installed in 25ms\n\nThe application panicked (crashed).\nMessage:  called `Result::unwrap()` on an `Err` value:\n   0: failed to set current directory to /tmp/demo/sub\n   1: No such file or directory (os error 2)\n$ echo $?\n134\n```\n\nAfter:\n\n```\n$ mise --cd sub install age@1.3.2\nmise ⇢ age@1.3.2  15ms · already installed\nmise ████████████████ 1/1 · installed 0 tools · 1 already installed in 52ms\nError:\n   0: failed to reload settings\n   1: failed to set current directory to /tmp/demo/sub\n   2: No such file or directory (os error 2)\n$ echo $?\n1\n```\n\n`Settings::reload()` and `Settings::override_with()` now build the new\nsettings first and only then swap them in, returning the error\notherwise; `Config::reset()` and the `ls-remote` and `test-tool` flags\npropagate it. The previous settings stay cached until the new ones are\nready, so readers on other threads never find the cache empty mid-reload\neither. A failed `override_with()` also puts the CLI layer back as it\nwas, so the override it could not apply is not picked up by a later\nreload, and `ls-remote` folds `--prerelease` and `--no-versions-host`\ninto one override so they rebuild settings once.\n\nValidation: a new unit test makes a reload fail (`MISE_YES=maybe` set\nafter the first load) and checks that `reload()` and `override_with()`\nreturn the error while `Settings::get()` keeps returning the previous\nsettings, and that the failed override is not applied once the\nenvironment is corrected. The existing reload tests and `cargo clippy\n--workspace --all-features --all-targets -- -D warnings` pass.\n\n*AI-assisted — Tool: Claude Code; model: anthropic/claude-fable-5-1;\nversion: unavailable.*\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\n\n\n\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **Bug Fixes**\n* Settings reloads and overrides now report errors instead of silently\ncontinuing. Commands that depend on a failed override stop and return\nthe error.\n* If new settings cannot be loaded, the previously loaded settings\nremain available rather than being replaced with invalid settings.\n\n\n\n---------\n\nCo-authored-by: Claude Fable 5.1 ",

          "payload": "tree b6c65ca3516410f8afda3785049dcc9a4617c666\nparent a8d9dcbdeec25acd812fc725376f56ec6aca5e7c\nauthor jdx <216188+jdx@users.noreply.github.com> 1791037924 -0500\ncommitter GitHub  1791037924 +0000\n\nfix(config): report a failed settings reload instead of aborting (#13925)\n\nSeveral commands rebuild settings partway through a run: `mise install`,\n`mise upgrade`, `mise use`, `mise uninstall`, `mise prune` and friends\nafter they change what is installed, and `mise ls-remote --prerelease` /\n`--no-versions-host` to apply the flag. That rebuild was lazy. The\ncached settings were dropped and the next `Settings::get()` loaded them\nagain, and if that load failed there was nothing to return the error to,\nso `Settings::get()` panicked. Release builds are compiled with `panic =\n\"abort\"`, so a settings problem ended the command with SIGABRT and a\ncore dump instead of an error message.\n\nThis is the crash in #13923: `mise install\ngithub:can1357/oh-my-pi@latest` for a tool that was already installed\naborted on the main thread, and the only clue left in the core was a\npanic location inside the settings loader. The one-off trigger there is\nstill unknown, but the abort is what turned it from an error into a\ncrash.\n\nA reload can only fail when one of its inputs changed since startup, so\na deliberate trigger is contrived. A `preinstall` hook that removes the\ndirectory `--cd` pointed at does it: re-applying `--cd` during the\nreload fails.\n\n`sub/mise.toml`:\n\n```toml\n[hooks]\npreinstall = \"rm -rf $PWD\"\n```\n\nBefore (2026.9.17 release build; paths shortened):\n\n```\n$ mise --cd sub install age@1.3.2\nmise ⇢ age@1.3.2  7ms · already installed\nmise ████████████████ 1/1 · installed 0 tools · 1 already installed in 25ms\n\nThe application panicked (crashed).\nMessage:  called `Result::unwrap()` on an `Err` value:\n   0: failed to set current directory to /tmp/demo/sub\n   1: No such file or directory (os error 2)\n$ echo $?\n134\n```\n\nAfter:\n\n```\n$ mise --cd sub install age@1.3.2\nmise ⇢ age@1.3.2  15ms · already installed\nmise ████████████████ 1/1 · installed 0 tools · 1 already installed in 52ms\nError:\n   0: failed to reload settings\n   1: failed to set current directory to /tmp/demo/sub\n   2: No such file or directory (os error 2)\n$ echo $?\n1\n```\n\n`Settings::reload()` and `Settings::override_with()` now build the new\nsettings first and only then swap them in, returning the error\notherwise; `Config::reset()` and the `ls-remote` and `test-tool` flags\npropagate it. The previous settings stay cached until the new ones are\nready, so readers on other threads never find the cache empty mid-reload\neither. A failed `override_with()` also puts the CLI layer back as it\nwas, so the override it could not apply is not picked up by a later\nreload, and `ls-remote` folds `--prerelease` and `--no-versions-host`\ninto one override so they rebuild settings once.\n\nValidation: a new unit test makes a reload fail (`MISE_YES=maybe` set\nafter the first load) and checks that `reload()` and `override_with()`\nreturn the error while `Settings::get()` keeps returning the previous\nsettings, and that the failed override is not applied once the\nenvironment is corrected. The existing reload tests and `cargo clippy\n--workspace --all-features --all-targets -- -D warnings` pass.\n\n*AI-assisted — Tool: Claude Code; model: anthropic/claude-fable-5-1;\nversion: unavailable.*\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\n\n\n\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **Bug Fixes**\n* Settings reloads and overrides now report errors instead of silently\ncontinuing. Commands that depend on a failed override stop and return\nthe error.\n* If new settings cannot be loaded, the previously loaded settings\nremain available rather than being replaced with invalid settings.\n\n\n\n---------\n\nCo-authored-by: Claude Fable 5.1 ",

      "sha": "648a3439e0e68b87f32a606a2767d98f66757d4d",

      "commit": {
        "author": {
          "name": "jdx",
          "email": "216188+jdx@users.noreply.github.com",
          "date": "2026-10-03T14:34:51Z"

        "committer": {

          "date": "2026-10-03T14:34:51Z"
        },
        "message": "docs: join the showreel films' cuts and play the kit's own brand cards (#13927)\n\n\nhttps://entire.io/gh/jdx/mise/trails/407\n\n\nThe landing page's two video editions (the full tour and the quick\noverview, from #13916) are cut from the source reel's scenes in a new\norder, and the seams showed. This makes the cuts land cleanly, replaces\nthe hand-drawn intro, outro and poster with the reel's own open, morph\nand end card, and re-cuts the music to the new picture. The tour is now\n4:58 and the overview 1:22.\n\n## What a viewer saw, and sees now\n\n- **Ghost scenes at three tickets.** The Environments, Tasks and\nDotfiles tickets \"took down\" the stage of the section the *source reel*\nplays before them (packslip, redact, daemons), none of which the tour\nplays, so those stages flashed on screen for about 0.3 s behind a 0.18 s\ndip to black. Each ticket now takes down the stage of the section the\nfilm actually plays before it (the registry's card, vars' card and\nterminal, skip's), so the bar line it starts from is the frame the film\narrives on. Tickets are also numbered in film order (ACT I–VI) instead\nof printing the source's I, III, IV, V, VI, VII.\n- **A card showing tools the tour had not introduced.** The switch ended\nby folding its folder diagram into packslip's station card (`jq`,\n`npm:prettier`), then the next scene wrote an empty card. The switch is\nnow cut at its storyboard `fold`, so it ends on both versions threaded\nto their folder cards.\n- **Dips to black at every other cut** are gone. A cut the source never\nmade now leaves on the kit's exit (fall 16 px and fade over 3/8 beat)\nand enters on its entrance (rise 24 px and fade in over 1/2 beat) over a\nstill stage, the same motion every window and card in the reel uses.\n- **Brand cards.** The 6 s intro (\"Dev tools. Environments. Tasks.\" over\na flat panel) and the 8 s outro (\"Install mise.\") were drawn outside the\nkit: no pass lamp, vignette or grain, pillar colours on headlines and a\nURL, type off the scale, and no chef. The films now play the source\nreel's open (the real `mise` help screen, the block chef lifting into\nthe chef with the bell, \"mise-en-place\" writing on; 10.75 s) and, in the\ntour, the morph (the card's three lit headers become the toque) into the\nend card (name, tagline, the install command typing, the platform line,\nmise.jdx.dev and the glint; 12 s). The overview goes straight to the end\ncard.\n- **Callouts.** The 64 px \"node --version → v26.10.0\" lines across the\ntop of the frame repeated values the scenes already lift onto the folder\nand station cards as 40 px chips, popped on and off in one frame, and in\nthe pitch stayed on `echo APP_ENV` for 13 s while `mise run ci`\nstreamed. They are removed.\n- **Poster.** The source reel's poster frame (the switch with both\nversions on their folders and its caption) replaces the flat \"Switch\nbetween projects.\" card.\n- **Music.** The overview's wind-down started 16 s before its end card;\nthe tour's loudest groove sat under the quiet \"breath\" and its outro\nunder the clone climax. Both films are re-cut on the song's own bar\nlines: the first groove drops as the open's card leaves and the first\ndemonstration begins, the song's break, build and loudest groove fall\nunder `mise use jq`, bootstrap and the clone's `[ci] api ready` climax,\nand the complete wind-down, outro and ring-out play under the morph and\nthe end card, ending a few frames before the picture. The tour now has\ntwo music joins instead of four.\n- **Pitched sound effects** (the switch's marimba runs, the slams, the\nlanes' pizzicato, the climax) were tuned to the chord the *source bed*\nplays at the source moment; under the films' re-cut song some landed on\nthe wrong chord (an F minor stab over C minor, for example). The films\nnow strike them on the chord the song actually plays under them, from a\nper-bar chord chart read off the track.\n- **Player.** On a 375 px phone the edition toggle no longer wraps the\nselected button onto its own row, chapter buttons are 44 px touch\ntargets, and the chapter labels match the tickets on screen\n(\"Everywhere\" rather than \"In CI\"; the opening chapter is \"mise\").\n\nChapter times (tour): mise 0:00, Project switching 0:10, Dev tools 0:58,\nEnvironments 1:32, Tasks 1:54, Dotfiles 2:32, Everywhere 3:12, A new\nmachine 3:36, Install mise 4:38.\n\n## How\n\n- `bible.ts` `Joins` and `kit/grey.ts` `G.prev`/`G.next`: the bar lines\na section starts from and ends on in the reel being drawn, which a film\nmay override; `ticketScene`, `ticketStage`, `sectionLit` and the\ndotfiles ticket read them instead of the timeline's. `Join.meta`\nrenumbers a ticket.\n- `compose.ts` `Reel.render(ctx, t, pw, ph, frame?)`: with a `frame`\npresence the scene is drawn on a layer of its own over the stage, so the\nstage, vignette and grain stay still while the scene rises or falls.\n- `edit.ts`: shots may be spans of a section (`OPEN_TO`, `SWITCH_TO`,\n`END_TO`); `film.ts` computes the joins and presences from the cuts and\nno longer draws anything itself.\n- `film-music.ts`: new arrangements, `SONG_CHART` and `filmChordAt`;\n`score/harmony.ts` `setFilmChart`, which `film-audio.ts` sets round its\nrender of the effects; every `chordAt` call passes its reel time.\n- Tests pin rules rather than numbers: the partial shots end where the\nstoryboard's events say (`fold`, `leave`), the music ends within a\nsecond before the film with its outro under the end card, every cut\neither arrives at rest (a join) or enters and leaves on the kit's\ncurves, and the Environments ticket draws the registry's card and not\npackslip's. `docs/public/showreel*-chapters.vtt` are regenerated.\nSTORYBOARD.md and BED.md describe the new edit and arrangement.\n\n## Validation\n\n`aube run typecheck:showreel` passes; `aube run test:showreel` runs 189\ntests, 188 pass and 1 is skipped (needs a capture run not on this\nmachine). Both editions were rendered as 30 fps drafts and inspected\nframe by frame at every join; the tour measures −16.8 LUFS integrated,\n−2.5 dBTP, the overview −18 LUFS, with both ring-outs dying out before\nthe picture ends. `aube run docs:build` passes with the drafts in place.\nThe production render (120/60 fps tour, overview, poster) runs in the\ndocs deploy; the render cache key already covers every changed file.\n\nNot changed here: `hk` and `npm:prettier` still appear in the tour's\nsecond half (lock, the clone's installs) without an introducing scene,\nsince restoring backends and packslip would take the tour past five\nminutes; `og:video` still points at the full tour.\n\n*AI-assisted — Tool: Claude Code; model: anthropic/claude-fable-5-1;\nversion: unavailable.*\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\n\n\n---\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> **Medium Risk**\n> Large docs/showreel render pipeline change (edits, compositing, audio\nchord mapping) affects published landing videos and chapter VTT;\nmitigated by expanded tests but still visual/audio QA dependent.\n> \n> **Overview**\n> **Landing-page showreel films** now splice source scenes with\n**joins** and kit motion instead of black dips and hand-drawn brand\ncards. Tour runs **4:58** (was 4:44); overview **1:22** (was 1:16).\n> \n> **Picture & transitions:** `edit.ts` trims **open**, **switch**\n(before packslip fold), and **end** to storyboard spans and adds\n**morph** before the end card on the tour. `film.ts` drops custom\nintro/outro, poster, and command callouts; it drives `filmJoins` and\n`framePresence` so tickets inherit the **previous film cut’s** stage\n(`Joins` / `G.prev`) and renumber **ACT I–VI**. Non-continuous cuts use\n`compose.ts` **Presence** layering (still stage, scene rises/falls).\nPoster uses the source reel’s `POSTER_TIME` frame.\n> \n> **Audio:** `film-music.ts` re-arranges the song on bar lines with\n`SONG_CHART` / `filmChordAt`; effects pitch via `setFilmChart` in\n`film-audio.ts` and `chordAt(..., t)`. Effect ramps respect `continuous`\ncuts. Docs/tests/VTT chapter times updated.\n> \n> **Player UI:** Edition toggles stay on one row on narrow screens;\nchapter buttons get **44px** min touch height and `nowrap`.\n> \n> Reviewed by [Cursor Bugbot](https://cursor.com/bugbot) for commit\ne28ee73862deee3abf6be5e3205b1d58fa33159d. Bugbot is set up for automated\ncode reviews on this repo. Configure\n[here](https://www.cursor.com/dashboard/bugbot).\n\n\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **New Features**\n* Updated the showreel’s Tour and Overview edits, with revised pacing,\nchapter timing, music, and transitions.\n* Improved edition and chapter button sizing and layout, including on\nnarrow screens.\n* **Documentation**\n* Updated the storyboard and music notes to reflect the revised edits\nand arrangements.\n\n\n---------\n\nCo-authored-by: Claude Fable 5.1 ",

          "payload": "tree 5d91a088750a6afc0696418bcc8b573d0355be57\nparent ebd2cd359c63d2a9645f80d1bb71afceff1b49fe\nauthor jdx <216188+jdx@users.noreply.github.com> 1791038091 -0500\ncommitter GitHub  1791038091 -0500\n\ndocs: join the showreel films' cuts and play the kit's own brand cards (#13927)\n\n\nhttps://entire.io/gh/jdx/mise/trails/407\n\n\nThe landing page's two video editions (the full tour and the quick\noverview, from #13916) are cut from the source reel's scenes in a new\norder, and the seams showed. This makes the cuts land cleanly, replaces\nthe hand-drawn intro, outro and poster with the reel's own open, morph\nand end card, and re-cuts the music to the new picture. The tour is now\n4:58 and the overview 1:22.\n\n## What a viewer saw, and sees now\n\n- **Ghost scenes at three tickets.** The Environments, Tasks and\nDotfiles tickets \"took down\" the stage of the section the *source reel*\nplays before them (packslip, redact, daemons), none of which the tour\nplays, so those stages flashed on screen for about 0.3 s behind a 0.18 s\ndip to black. Each ticket now takes down the stage of the section the\nfilm actually plays before it (the registry's card, vars' card and\nterminal, skip's), so the bar line it starts from is the frame the film\narrives on. Tickets are also numbered in film order (ACT I–VI) instead\nof printing the source's I, III, IV, V, VI, VII.\n- **A card showing tools the tour had not introduced.** The switch ended\nby folding its folder diagram into packslip's station card (`jq`,\n`npm:prettier`), then the next scene wrote an empty card. The switch is\nnow cut at its storyboard `fold`, so it ends on both versions threaded\nto their folder cards.\n- **Dips to black at every other cut** are gone. A cut the source never\nmade now leaves on the kit's exit (fall 16 px and fade over 3/8 beat)\nand enters on its entrance (rise 24 px and fade in over 1/2 beat) over a\nstill stage, the same motion every window and card in the reel uses.\n- **Brand cards.** The 6 s intro (\"Dev tools. Environments. Tasks.\" over\na flat panel) and the 8 s outro (\"Install mise.\") were drawn outside the\nkit: no pass lamp, vignette or grain, pillar colours on headlines and a\nURL, type off the scale, and no chef. The films now play the source\nreel's open (the real `mise` help screen, the block chef lifting into\nthe chef with the bell, \"mise-en-place\" writing on; 10.75 s) and, in the\ntour, the morph (the card's three lit headers become the toque) into the\nend card (name, tagline, the install command typing, the platform line,\nmise.jdx.dev and the glint; 12 s). The overview goes straight to the end\ncard.\n- **Callouts.** The 64 px \"node --version → v26.10.0\" lines across the\ntop of the frame repeated values the scenes already lift onto the folder\nand station cards as 40 px chips, popped on and off in one frame, and in\nthe pitch stayed on `echo APP_ENV` for 13 s while `mise run ci`\nstreamed. They are removed.\n- **Poster.** The source reel's poster frame (the switch with both\nversions on their folders and its caption) replaces the flat \"Switch\nbetween projects.\" card.\n- **Music.** The overview's wind-down started 16 s before its end card;\nthe tour's loudest groove sat under the quiet \"breath\" and its outro\nunder the clone climax. Both films are re-cut on the song's own bar\nlines: the first groove drops as the open's card leaves and the first\ndemonstration begins, the song's break, build and loudest groove fall\nunder `mise use jq`, bootstrap and the clone's `[ci] api ready` climax,\nand the complete wind-down, outro and ring-out play under the morph and\nthe end card, ending a few frames before the picture. The tour now has\ntwo music joins instead of four.\n- **Pitched sound effects** (the switch's marimba runs, the slams, the\nlanes' pizzicato, the climax) were tuned to the chord the *source bed*\nplays at the source moment; under the films' re-cut song some landed on\nthe wrong chord (an F minor stab over C minor, for example). The films\nnow strike them on the chord the song actually plays under them, from a\nper-bar chord chart read off the track.\n- **Player.** On a 375 px phone the edition toggle no longer wraps the\nselected button onto its own row, chapter buttons are 44 px touch\ntargets, and the chapter labels match the tickets on screen\n(\"Everywhere\" rather than \"In CI\"; the opening chapter is \"mise\").\n\nChapter times (tour): mise 0:00, Project switching 0:10, Dev tools 0:58,\nEnvironments 1:32, Tasks 1:54, Dotfiles 2:32, Everywhere 3:12, A new\nmachine 3:36, Install mise 4:38.\n\n## How\n\n- `bible.ts` `Joins` and `kit/grey.ts` `G.prev`/`G.next`: the bar lines\na section starts from and ends on in the reel being drawn, which a film\nmay override; `ticketScene`, `ticketStage`, `sectionLit` and the\ndotfiles ticket read them instead of the timeline's. `Join.meta`\nrenumbers a ticket.\n- `compose.ts` `Reel.render(ctx, t, pw, ph, frame?)`: with a `frame`\npresence the scene is drawn on a layer of its own over the stage, so the\nstage, vignette and grain stay still while the scene rises or falls.\n- `edit.ts`: shots may be spans of a section (`OPEN_TO`, `SWITCH_TO`,\n`END_TO`); `film.ts` computes the joins and presences from the cuts and\nno longer draws anything itself.\n- `film-music.ts`: new arrangements, `SONG_CHART` and `filmChordAt`;\n`score/harmony.ts` `setFilmChart`, which `film-audio.ts` sets round its\nrender of the effects; every `chordAt` call passes its reel time.\n- Tests pin rules rather than numbers: the partial shots end where the\nstoryboard's events say (`fold`, `leave`), the music ends within a\nsecond before the film with its outro under the end card, every cut\neither arrives at rest (a join) or enters and leaves on the kit's\ncurves, and the Environments ticket draws the registry's card and not\npackslip's. `docs/public/showreel*-chapters.vtt` are regenerated.\nSTORYBOARD.md and BED.md describe the new edit and arrangement.\n\n## Validation\n\n`aube run typecheck:showreel` passes; `aube run test:showreel` runs 189\ntests, 188 pass and 1 is skipped (needs a capture run not on this\nmachine). Both editions were rendered as 30 fps drafts and inspected\nframe by frame at every join; the tour measures −16.8 LUFS integrated,\n−2.5 dBTP, the overview −18 LUFS, with both ring-outs dying out before\nthe picture ends. `aube run docs:build` passes with the drafts in place.\nThe production render (120/60 fps tour, overview, poster) runs in the\ndocs deploy; the render cache key already covers every changed file.\n\nNot changed here: `hk` and `npm:prettier` still appear in the tour's\nsecond half (lock, the clone's installs) without an introducing scene,\nsince restoring backends and packslip would take the tour past five\nminutes; `og:video` still points at the full tour.\n\n*AI-assisted — Tool: Claude Code; model: anthropic/claude-fable-5-1;\nversion: unavailable.*\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\n\n\n---\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> **Medium Risk**\n> Large docs/showreel render pipeline change (edits, compositing, audio\nchord mapping) affects published landing videos and chapter VTT;\nmitigated by expanded tests but still visual/audio QA dependent.\n> \n> **Overview**\n> **Landing-page showreel films** now splice source scenes with\n**joins** and kit motion instead of black dips and hand-drawn brand\ncards. Tour runs **4:58** (was 4:44); overview **1:22** (was 1:16).\n> \n> **Picture & transitions:** `edit.ts` trims **open**, **switch**\n(before packslip fold), and **end** to storyboard spans and adds\n**morph** before the end card on the tour. `film.ts` drops custom\nintro/outro, poster, and command callouts; it drives `filmJoins` and\n`framePresence` so tickets inherit the **previous film cut’s** stage\n(`Joins` / `G.prev`) and renumber **ACT I–VI**. Non-continuous cuts use\n`compose.ts` **Presence** layering (still stage, scene rises/falls).\nPoster uses the source reel’s `POSTER_TIME` frame.\n> \n> **Audio:** `film-music.ts` re-arranges the song on bar lines with\n`SONG_CHART` / `filmChordAt`; effects pitch via `setFilmChart` in\n`film-audio.ts` and `chordAt(..., t)`. Effect ramps respect `continuous`\ncuts. Docs/tests/VTT chapter times updated.\n> \n> **Player UI:** Edition toggles stay on one row on narrow screens;\nchapter buttons get **44px** min touch height and `nowrap`.\n> \n> Reviewed by [Cursor Bugbot](https://cursor.com/bugbot) for commit\ne28ee73862deee3abf6be5e3205b1d58fa33159d. Bugbot is set up for automated\ncode reviews on this repo. Configure\n[here](https://www.cursor.com/dashboard/bugbot).\n\n\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **New Features**\n* Updated the showreel’s Tour and Overview edits, with revised pacing,\nchapter timing, music, and transitions.\n* Improved edition and chapter button sizing and layout, including on\nnarrow screens.\n* **Documentation**\n* Updated the storyboard and music notes to reflect the revised edits\nand arrangements.\n\n\n---------\n\nCo-authored-by: Claude Fable 5.1 ",
          "verified_at": "2026-10-03T14:34:52Z"
        }
      },

      "sha": "ef2783203ab020992ff79941c3b7203cb4c5b0a8",
      "node_id": "C_kwDOIvux3toAKGVmMjc4MzIwM2FiMDIwOTkyZmY3OTk0MWMzYjcyMDNjYjRjNWIwYTg",
      "commit": {
        "author": {
          "name": "jdx",
          "email": "216188+jdx@users.noreply.github.com",
          "date": "2026-10-03T14:47:11Z"

          "email": "noreply@github.com",
          "date": "2026-10-03T14:47:11Z"
        },
        "message": "feat(schema): type tool options per backend and core tool (#13924)\n\n\nhttps://entire.io/gh/jdx/mise/trails/405\n\n\nEditors validate and autocomplete `mise.toml` from `schema/mise.json`,\nbut a `[tools]` entry only ever got the generic options, so\n`asset_pattern`, `features`, `release_type` and friends were untyped and\ninvisible. This makes the schema offer each backend's own options, and\nthe core tools' options, only for tools of that kind.\n\n```toml\n[tools]\n\"github:cli/cli\" = { version = \"2\", asset_pattern = 5 }        # flagged: must be a string\n\"aqua:o/r\"       = { version = \"1\", libc = \"uclibc\" }          # flagged: glibc, gnu or musl\njava             = { version = \"21\", release_type = \"stable\" } # flagged: ga or ea\n\"cargo:ripgrep\"  = { version = \"latest\", features = [\"pcre2\"] } # completes and validates\n```\n\n## What changes\n\n- **Typed options per backend.** A `patternProperties` entry on `tools`\nmatches the backend prefix (`^github:`, `^(pypi|pipx):`, ...) and points\nat a definition that extends the generic tool. Covered: github, gitlab,\nforgejo, ubi, http, s3, aqua, cargo, npm, pypi/pipx, gem, go, conda,\nspm, packslip, and spinel from #13922. Every other key still gets the\ngeneric definition, so an unrelated `cargo:foo = { entrypoint = 5 }` is\nunaffected.\n- **Core tools.** `python` (`patch_sysconfig`), `java` (`release_type`),\n`rust` (`profile`, `components`, `targets`, `mr_boxington`) and core\n`dotnet` (`runtime`), including `core:` and bracketed spellings.\n- **Task tool tables.** `[tasks.*.tools]` uses the same typed\ndefinitions.\n- **Per-platform overrides.** `platforms.-` (and the\n`platform` alias) is modelled for every tool, and typed for the options\neach backend actually reads per platform (for example `github`\n`asset_pattern` and `url`, `http` `url` and `checksum`).\n- **`prerelease`** is added as a generic option, because every backend\nreads it. `lazy_bins` now accepts a string as well as an array, matching\nthe parser.\n- **Booleans** accept `true`/`false`, the strings `\"true\"`/`\"false\"`,\nand `1`/`0`, because mise coerces them. This avoids flagging configs\nthat work today.\n- **`experimental_monorepo_root`** is accepted as a deprecated key. mise\nstill honors it with a warning, but the root schema rejected it.\n\nOption definitions live once in `tool__options`, and platform\nentries `$ref` into them, which keeps the diff to one definition per\noption. `schema/mise-task.json` is regenerated by `render:schema`.\n\nThe option lists come from the backend source (what each backend\nactually reads), not only the docs. Options the docs miss, such as the\n`format` option on `github`, `http` and `s3`, are included.\n\n## Out of scope\n\nLeft for follow-ups: per-preset daemon `options` and `ports`, the\nundocumented `[bootstrap]` `secrets`, `groups`, `users` and `compose`\nsections, and typed options in `mise-registry-tool.json`.\n\n## Testing\n\n- `ajv compile --strict-types --strict-tuples` passes on both schemas,\nthe same flags `hk` runs.\n- `e2e/config/test_schema_ajv` gains valid and invalid cases for each\nfamily above, plus task tool tables. `e2e/config/test_schema_tombi`\nchecks that tombi accepts the valid config and rejects a wrong-typed\n`asset_pattern` and a bad `release_type`. The existing schema e2e tests\nstill pass.\n\nThis builds on #13922, which adds the `spinel` backend.\n\n*AI-assisted — Tool: Claude Code; model: anthropic/claude-sonnet-5-5;\nversion: 2.1.286.*\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\n\n\n---\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> **Medium Risk**\n> Large schema-only change that may surface new editor validation errors\non existing configs; runtime behavior is unchanged but misaligned schema\nrules could confuse users.\n> \n> **Overview**\n> **`mise.json` and `mise-task.json` now validate `[tools]` (and\ntask-level tool tables) with backend- and core-specific option shapes**,\ninstead of only generic tool fields. Tool keys are matched by prefix\n(`github:`, `cargo:`, `python`, etc.) and validated against dedicated\ndefs that layer shared options with backend options and typed\n`platforms` / `platform` overrides.\n> \n> Editors get stricter checks on enums, types, and platform key patterns\n(e.g. wrong `asset_pattern` type, invalid `java.release_type`, bad\n`lazy_bins` paths). **`lazy_bins`** accepts a single name or a list;\n**`prerelease`** is documented on generic tools; boolean-like options\naccept bool, `\"true\"`/`\"false\"`, and `0`/`1` where mise coerces them.\n**`experimental_monorepo_root`** is allowed again as a deprecated root\nkey.\n> \n> **E2E** adds AJV and Tombi fixtures for valid/invalid backend and core\noptions, task tools, and the legacy monorepo key.\n> \n> Reviewed by [Cursor Bugbot](https://cursor.com/bugbot) for commit\ne632596ca84b3d31ce66525192df024fa043eb21. Bugbot is set up for automated\ncode reviews on this repo. Configure\n[here](https://www.cursor.com/dashboard/bugbot).\n\n\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **New Features**\n* Configuration validation recognizes backend-specific options for\nsupported tools, including platform overrides and prerelease settings.\n* `lazy_bins` accepts a single slash-free value or a list of slash-free\nvalues.\n* `experimental_monorepo_root` is accepted as a deprecated alias for\n`monorepo_root`.\n* Supported boolean-like tool settings accept booleans,\n`\"true\"`/`\"false\"` strings, or `0`/`1`.\n* **Bug Fixes**\n* Global and task-level tool declarations validate supported backend\noptions and reject invalid values.\n* **Tests**\n  * Added coverage for valid and invalid backend-specific options.\n\n\n---------\n\nCo-authored-by: Claude Sonnet 5.5 ",

Options the docs miss, such as the\n`format` option on `github`, `http` and `s3`, are included.\n\n## Out of scope\n\nLeft for follow-ups: per-preset daemon `options` and `ports`, the\nundocumented `[bootstrap]` `secrets`, `groups`, `users` and `compose`\nsections, and typed options in `mise-registry-tool.json`.\n\n## Testing\n\n- `ajv compile --strict-types --strict-tuples` passes on both schemas,\nthe same flags `hk` runs.\n- `e2e/config/test_schema_ajv` gains valid and invalid cases for each\nfamily above, plus task tool tables. `e2e/config/test_schema_tombi`\nchecks that tombi accepts the valid config and rejects a wrong-typed\n`asset_pattern` and a bad `release_type`. The existing schema e2e tests\nstill pass.\n\nThis builds on #13922, which adds the `spinel` backend.\n\n*AI-assisted — Tool: Claude Code; model: anthropic/claude-sonnet-5-5;\nversion: 2.1.286.*\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\n\n\n---\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> **Medium Risk**\n> Large schema-only change that may surface new editor validation errors\non existing configs; runtime behavior is unchanged but misaligned schema\nrules could confuse users.\n> \n> **Overview**\n> **`mise.json` and `mise-task.json` now validate `[tools]` (and\ntask-level tool tables) with backend- and core-specific option shapes**,\ninstead of only generic tool fields. Tool keys are matched by prefix\n(`github:`, `cargo:`, `python`, etc.) and validated against dedicated\ndefs that layer shared options with backend options and typed\n`platforms` / `platform` overrides.\n> \n> Editors get stricter checks on enums, types, and platform key patterns\n(e.g. wrong `asset_pattern` type, invalid `java.release_type`, bad\n`lazy_bins` paths). **`lazy_bins`** accepts a single name or a list;\n**`prerelease`** is documented on generic tools; boolean-like options\naccept bool, `\"true\"`/`\"false\"`, and `0`/`1` where mise coerces them.\n**`experimental_monorepo_root`** is allowed again as a deprecated root\nkey.\n> \n> **E2E** adds AJV and Tombi fixtures for valid/invalid backend and core\noptions, task tools, and the legacy monorepo key.\n> \n> Reviewed by [Cursor Bugbot](https://cursor.com/bugbot) for commit\ne632596ca84b3d31ce66525192df024fa043eb21. Bugbot is set up for automated\ncode reviews on this repo. Configure\n[here](https://www.cursor.com/dashboard/bugbot).\n\n\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **New Features**\n* Configuration validation recognizes backend-specific options for\nsupported tools, including platform overrides and prerelease settings.\n* `lazy_bins` accepts a single slash-free value or a list of slash-free\nvalues.\n* `experimental_monorepo_root` is accepted as a deprecated alias for\n`monorepo_root`.\n* Supported boolean-like tool settings accept booleans,\n`\"true\"`/`\"false\"` strings, or `0`/`1`.\n* **Bug Fixes**\n* Global and task-level tool declarations validate supported backend\noptions and reject invalid values.\n* **Tests**\n  * Added coverage for valid and invalid backend-specific options.\n\n\n---------\n\nCo-authored-by: Claude Sonnet 5.5 ",
          "verified_at": "2026-10-03T14:47:12Z"
        }
      },

    {
      "sha": "3f0b40f0697f35374db98d5a2f298657ce9e66a0",
      "node_id": "C_kwDOIvux3toAKDNmMGI0MGYwNjk3ZjM1Mzc0ZGI5OGQ1YTJmMjk4NjU3Y2U5ZTY2YTA",
      "commit": {
        "author": {
          "name": "Jam Balaya",
          "email": "jambalaya.pyoncafe@outlook.jp",
          "date": "2026-10-03T15:19:33Z"
        },
        "committer": {
          "name": "GitHub",
          "email": "noreply@github.com",
          "date": "2026-10-03T15:19:33Z"
        },
        "message": "fix(exec): keep double quotes in a positional cmd /c command on Windows (#13887)\n\nThis reopens #13755, which the draft limit closed; that pull request has\nthe earlier review discussion.\n\nOn Windows, a command passed to `cmd /c` as an argument lost its double\nquotes: `mise exec -- cmd /c 'echo \"a b\"'` printed `\\\"a b\\\"`. mise hands\nthe command to cmd with the standard argument quoting, which escapes\neach `\"` as `\\\"`, and cmd does not understand that escape.\n\nThis is how pitchfork runs a daemon with `mise = true`: `mise x -- cmd\n/C `. So on Windows any such daemon whose `run` contains a double\nquote failed to start, for example one that quotes a program path with a\nspace:\n\n```toml\n[daemons.api]\nrun = '\"C:\\Program Files\\api\\api.exe\" --title \"a b\"'\nmise = true\n```\n\nBefore, pitchfork reported the daemon as `errored`, and its log showed\ncmd rejecting `'\\\"C:\\Program Files\\api\\api.exe\\\"'` as an unknown\ncommand. Now it starts. pitchfork documents this as a known limitation\nof `mise = true`, which this change removes on the mise side.\n\nmise now passes the command to cmd verbatim, the way it already does for\n`mise exec -c` (#9355), when all of these hold:\n\n- the program is `cmd`\n- the command is the one argument right after `/c` or `/k`, with only\nswitches before it\n- the command contains a double quote\n\nAnything else is unchanged. A command without a double quote already\nreaches cmd intact; a path with a space, such as `cmd /c \"C:\\Program\nFiles\\app.exe\"`, relies on cmd keeping the two quotes the standard\nquoting adds and would break if passed verbatim. Several arguments after\n`/c`, as in `cmd /c \"echo one\" two`, remain separate arguments.\n\n## Validation\n\nOn Windows 11 with a debug build:\n\n- `e2e-win/exec_inner_quotes.Tests.ps1` has three new cases. `mise exec\n-- cmd /c 'echo \"hello world\"'` prints `\"hello world\"`; it printed\n`\\\"hello world\\\"` with mise 2026.9.15. A `.cmd` in a directory with a\nspace, run as `\"probe.cmd\" \"a b\"`, now starts and receives `a b`;\nwith mise 2026.9.15 it did not start. A command without quotes still\nprints as before. The existing cases pass with and without this change.\n- pitchfork 2.28.0 with `general.mise_bin` pointing at the build: a\n`mise = true` daemon whose `run` quotes a mise path containing a space\nwent from `errored` to `running`, and its task ran. A `mise = true`\ndaemon without quotes runs as before.\n- `test_positional_cmd_command` covers the conditions above, on every\nplatform.\n- `cargo clippy -p mise -p mise-util --all-targets -- -D warnings` on\nWindows reports nothing in the changed files. CI now runs clippy on\nWindows too (#13757).\n\n*AI-assisted — Tool: Claude Code; model: anthropic/claude-opus-5-5;\nversion: unavailable.*\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\n\n\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **Bug Fixes**\n* On Windows, `mise exec -- cmd /c` preserves inner quotes in command\nstrings, while unquoted commands continue to run normally.\n* Quoted `.cmd` paths containing spaces pass quoted arguments as a\nsingle value.\n* Windows command exit codes are preserved, and commands terminated by a\nsignal return an error.\n* Commands with additional switches before `/c` or `/k` retain their\nquoted command string when it is supplied as a single argument.\n\n\nCo-authored-by: Claude Opus 5.5 ",

A command without a double quote already\nreaches cmd intact; a path with a space, such as `cmd /c \"C:\\Program\nFiles\\app.exe\"`, relies on cmd keeping the two quotes the standard\nquoting adds and would break if passed verbatim. Several arguments after\n`/c`, as in `cmd /c \"echo one\" two`, remain separate arguments.\n\n## Validation\n\nOn Windows 11 with a debug build:\n\n- `e2e-win/exec_inner_quotes.Tests.ps1` has three new cases. `mise exec\n-- cmd /c 'echo \"hello world\"'` prints `\"hello world\"`; it printed\n`\\\"hello world\\\"` with mise 2026.9.15. A `.cmd` in a directory with a\nspace, run as `\"probe.cmd\" \"a b\"`, now starts and receives `a b`;\nwith mise 2026.9.15 it did not start. A command without quotes still\nprints as before. The existing cases pass with and without this change.\n- pitchfork 2.28.0 with `general.mise_bin` pointing at the build: a\n`mise = true` daemon whose `run` quotes a mise path containing a space\nwent from `errored` to `running`, and its task ran. A `mise = true`\ndaemon without quotes runs as before.\n- `test_positional_cmd_command` covers the conditions above, on every\nplatform.\n- `cargo clippy -p mise -p mise-util --all-targets -- -D warnings` on\nWindows reports nothing in the changed files. CI now runs clippy on\nWindows too (#13757).\n\n*AI-assisted — Tool: Claude Code; model: anthropic/claude-opus-5-5;\nversion: unavailable.*\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\n\n\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **Bug Fixes**\n* On Windows, `mise exec -- cmd /c` preserves inner quotes in command\nstrings, while unquoted commands continue to run normally.\n* Quoted `.cmd` paths containing spaces pass quoted arguments as a\nsingle value.\n* Windows command exit codes are preserved, and commands terminated by a\nsignal return an error.\n* Commands with additional switches before `/c` or `/k` retain their\nquoted command string when it is supplied as a single argument.\n\n\nCo-authored-by: Claude Opus 5.5 ",

          "sha": "ef2783203ab020992ff79941c3b7203cb4c5b0a8",

    {
      "sha": "dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "node_id": "C_kwDOIvux3toAKGRmZTc0YTkwYjQxNjAzNjI1ZWU2YWFiZWNiNDJmMTRhMWY1ZWIwZjY",
      "commit": {
        "author": {
          "name": "Alexey Zhokhov",
          "email": "alexey@zhokhov.com",
          "date": "2026-10-03T15:37:36Z"
        },
        "committer": {
          "name": "GitHub",
          "email": "noreply@github.com",
          "date": "2026-10-03T15:37:36Z"
        },
        "message": "fix(config): skip miserc discovery with --no-config (#13926)\n\n## Problem\n\nA malformed `.miserc.toml` anywhere in the discovery chain fails even\nconfig-free invocations: `run_inner` calls `miserc::init()?`\nunconditionally (`src/cli/mod.rs`) before no-config is honored anywhere,\nand TOML errors propagate via `?`. So `mise --no-config version` run\nfrom a directory with a broken repo-level (or global/system) miserc\nexits non-zero instead of ignoring config.\n\n## Fix\n\nGuard the four miserc entry points on the pre-existing\n`Settings::no_config()` (`MISE_NO_CONFIG` env or pre-`--` argv\n`--no-config`):\n\n- `load_miserc_settings()` → `Ok(default)`\n- `load_global_miserc_settings()` → default\n- `get()` → default (folded into the existing `is_package_query()` early\nreturn, mirroring that precedent)\n- `get_global_ignored_config_paths()` → `None`\n\nNo new API; `no_config()` already exists. No-config now means no\nearly-config.\n\n## Semantic edge (call-out for reviewers)\n\n- `get_global_ignored_config_paths() → None` disables ignored-paths\nfiltering (consumed via `env.rs`) under no-config. Consistent with \"no\nconfig\", but changes `prune`-family verdicts for no-config invocations.\n- `no_config()` argv detection requires `ARGS` populated — true at the\n`run_inner` call site (`ARGS` is written just above the\n`miserc::init()?` call), so both `MISE_NO_CONFIG=1` and `--no-config`\nforms work.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- New `e2e/config/test_no_config_skips_miserc`: malformed project +\nglobal + system miserc; `MISE_NO_CONFIG=1` and `--no-config` both\nsucceed on `version`/`exec` from root and nested cwd; control without\nthe flag still fails with `Invalid TOML in config file`. Passes.\n- Existing `e2e/config/test_miserc`, `test_miserc_local`,\n`test_no_config` all still pass.\n- `cargo clippy --all-features` clean, `cargo fmt --all -- --check`\nclean, `shfmt`/`shellcheck` clean on the new test.\n\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **Bug Fixes**\n* `mise version` and `mise exec` now work with `--no-config` or\n`MISE_NO_CONFIG=1` when project, user, or system `miserc.toml` files are\nmalformed.\n* These commands also work from nested project directories when\nconfiguration is disabled.\n* Without either no-config option, malformed configuration may still\ncause commands to fail.\n\n\n---------\n\nSigned-off-by: Alexey Zhokhov ",
        "tree": {
          "sha": "73da24792e41be3d45bd94af9bf384c34e340248",
          "url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/git/trees/73da24792e41be3d45bd94af9bf384c34e340248"
        },
        "url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/git/commits/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
        "comment_count": 0,
        "verification": {
          "verified": true,
          "reason": "valid",
          "signature": "-----BEGIN PGP SIGNATURE-----\n\nwsFcBAABCAAQBQJqwSFACRC1aQ7uu5UhlAAAoagQADsQmT8wn5S5lFGrsLP8VwRS\n9M3ZWow4VVtM3qpj78mp0ahowLDGpUwkAjEhwEeQCKtEz29XLPKeL69hSWFlglr8\n7qn4XV6WAYUcNFlmQCBkyTtzhHmYLQvDNCHz3dXJF00IsNUeuGyG8Rz4LKClgd0B\nJoAJfp0lqPQ9U+61PUH294APpEIVZbOwH3kYq0V5cFQ1Fsdiw2tGgMeQT9BbSXPP\no8E30/balNmHZnRuFJSV/yxLHLcL8dUN0vI6EeqVQHht6TD2igW6DizFGNpc9Dj4\nrt2xP7IZUuWo1GSjr1IaHZi/nArTPZ9Og+EEmItrCYnDL7psAz7lI34YtkopdaZa\nLMWzgILsY4KfqtGE/QiWSUkJDR08rt4IGS76hs8qRL9gCoisw4ip26Xj4Fcv8hLY\nqciaJ2Y+Gw8/HwpoZNZ9KCOWzCP94MhkKvluVp7vKGL4QtObZcpPHn7wDlE06tPc\nOPUdVPQh4d92+n1n0uC9YCMx3BKV0j7O4uhOUGMM8Kcn6PIteHwEgvQKMEN+lpFN\nt5+Jufs0yHdi6YCQbrm0LD7ZrxZQpMLuUXVoFklAwVHyTanK5mtNE8Vqjt2dceKq\nkpOTvz6GvtouJ4yHCv2yubMQl+jEumxK2oxKNjrDW+/h11Cpkwj8ai0RJOhtEj/v\naBx8LIvvkh9RUE4e7gJt\n=kXWE\n-----END PGP SIGNATURE-----\n",
          "payload": "tree 73da24792e41be3d45bd94af9bf384c34e340248\nparent 3f0b40f0697f35374db98d5a2f298657ce9e66a0\nauthor Alexey Zhokhov  1791041856 +0700\ncommitter GitHub  1791041856 +0000\n\nfix(config): skip miserc discovery with --no-config (#13926)\n\n## Problem\n\nA malformed `.miserc.toml` anywhere in the discovery chain fails even\nconfig-free invocations: `run_inner` calls `miserc::init()?`\nunconditionally (`src/cli/mod.rs`) before no-config is honored anywhere,\nand TOML errors propagate via `?`. So `mise --no-config version` run\nfrom a directory with a broken repo-level (or global/system) miserc\nexits non-zero instead of ignoring config.\n\n## Fix\n\nGuard the four miserc entry points on the pre-existing\n`Settings::no_config()` (`MISE_NO_CONFIG` env or pre-`--` argv\n`--no-config`):\n\n- `load_miserc_settings()` → `Ok(default)`\n- `load_global_miserc_settings()` → default\n- `get()` → default (folded into the existing `is_package_query()` early\nreturn, mirroring that precedent)\n- `get_global_ignored_config_paths()` → `None`\n\nNo new API; `no_config()` already exists. No-config now means no\nearly-config.\n\n## Semantic edge (call-out for reviewers)\n\n- `get_global_ignored_config_paths() → None` disables ignored-paths\nfiltering (consumed via `env.rs`) under no-config. Consistent with \"no\nconfig\", but changes `prune`-family verdicts for no-config invocations.\n- `no_config()` argv detection requires `ARGS` populated — true at the\n`run_inner` call site (`ARGS` is written just above the\n`miserc::init()?` call), so both `MISE_NO_CONFIG=1` and `--no-config`\nforms work.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- New `e2e/config/test_no_config_skips_miserc`: malformed project +\nglobal + system miserc; `MISE_NO_CONFIG=1` and `--no-config` both\nsucceed on `version`/`exec` from root and nested cwd; control without\nthe flag still fails with `Invalid TOML in config file`. Passes.\n- Existing `e2e/config/test_miserc`, `test_miserc_local`,\n`test_no_config` all still pass.\n- `cargo clippy --all-features` clean, `cargo fmt --all -- --check`\nclean, `shfmt`/`shellcheck` clean on the new test.\n\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **Bug Fixes**\n* `mise version` and `mise exec` now work with `--no-config` or\n`MISE_NO_CONFIG=1` when project, user, or system `miserc.toml` files are\nmalformed.\n* These commands also work from nested project directories when\nconfiguration is disabled.\n* Without either no-config option, malformed configuration may still\ncause commands to fail.\n\n\n---------\n\nSigned-off-by: Alexey Zhokhov ",
          "verified_at": "2026-10-03T15:37:37Z"
        }
      },

      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/AGENTS.md",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/AGENTS.md",

      "patch": "@@ -193,7 +193,7 @@ Communique generates release notes from PR titles and descriptions, so write the\n 2. Use `mise run test:e2e [test_filename]...` for running specific e2e tests\n 3. Never run e2e tests by executing them directly — always use the mise task\n \n-`hk.pkl` currently defines `check` and `fix` steps only (no git `pre-commit` hook). `hk install --mise` may print that nothing is installed; that is expected. Use `mise run lint` / `mise run lint-fix` (which run hk) instead.\n+`hk.pkl` defines `check` and `fix` steps and a git `pre-commit` hook that runs the same linters with auto-fix (`stash = \"git\"` preserves unstaged work). `hk install --mise` installs that hook. You can still run `mise run lint` / `mise run lint-fix` by hand; on Linux and macOS they run hk, but on Windows `lint-fix` runs only Clippy, Prettier, and `cargo fmt`, so the other hk steps do not run there.\n \n ### hk Agent Workflow\n \n@@ -318,7 +318,7 @@ The install script:\n - keeps `GITHUB_TOKEN`, `MISE_GITHUB_TOKEN`, and `GH_TOKEN` in sync via one `sync_github_tokens` helper (prefer any already-set token; fall back to `gh auth token` only when all three are empty)\n - runs `MISE_SAFE=1 /usr/local/bin/mise install` with the just-built binary so checkout-controlled hooks/templates/`[env]` and tool-level `postinstall` / `install_env` cannot run with those tokens, then `mise trust` for later agent commands\n - runs `mise run build` so `target/` is warm for the `mbx`-wrapped cargo (`[wrappers.cargo]` in `mise.toml`) that agents build with, then points `/usr/local/bin/mise` at `target/debug/mise`. Every build runs with `GITHUB_TOKEN`, `MISE_GITHUB_TOKEN`, `GH_TOKEN`, and `GITHUB_API_TOKEN` unset\n-- runs `hk install --mise` (`hk.pkl` has no git hook, so this may report that nothing is installed)\n+- runs `hk install --mise` (installs the git `pre-commit` hook defined in `hk.pkl`)\n - persists mise shims and token sync in one `/etc/profile.d/mise-dev-env.sh` (shims first, then `sync_github_tokens`) and rewrites the Cloud Agent block in `/etc/bash.bashrc` so non-login interactive bash picks it up after a snapshot. Fish/zsh only get this from login shells (`profile.d`), not from bashrc\n - exposes the mise-installed `node` / `npm` / `npx` / `hk` / `gh` binaries on `/usr/local/bin` (isolated e2e PATH includes that directory, not the agent's shims). Links freeze the version from install time — re-run `.cursor/install.sh` after upgrading those tools\n "

      "sha": "728f758cea96879e7ff66dc143a0ca038139a346",
      "filename": "crates/mise-util/src/path.rs",
      "status": "modified",
      "additions": 14,
      "deletions": 0,
      "changes": 14,
      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/crates%2Fmise-util%2Fsrc%2Fpath.rs",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/crates%2Fmise-util%2Fsrc%2Fpath.rs",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/crates%2Fmise-util%2Fsrc%2Fpath.rs?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -340,6 +340,20 @@ pub fn cmd_verbatim_command(\n     Some(c)\n }\n \n+/// The switches and command of a positional `cmd /c ` (as in\n+/// `mise exec -- cmd /c `) to hand cmd verbatim, when the command is the\n+/// one argument after `/c` or `/k` and contains a `\"`. std would escape that\n+/// quote as `\\\"`, which cmd does not understand. Without one, std's quoting\n+/// already reaches cmd intact, so those commands are left alone.\n+pub fn positional_cmd_command(args: &[String]) -> Option<(&[String], &str)> {\n+    let (command, switches) = args.split_last()?;\n+    let runs = switches\n+        .last()\n+        .is_some_and(|s| s.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"/c\") || s.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"/k\"));\n+    (runs && switches.iter().all(|s| s.starts_with('/')) && command.contains('\"'))\n+        .then_some((switches, command.as_str()))\n+}\n+\n /// Split a configured shell *command string* (program + args) into argv,\n /// honoring host conventions.\n ///"
    },
    {
      "sha": "13e3041f602a1e2b9feb3e13522a8621b37cad19",
      "filename": "crates/mise-util/src/path/tests/display_shell.rs",
      "status": "modified",
      "additions": 30,
      "deletions": 0,
      "changes": 30,
      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/crates%2Fmise-util%2Fsrc%2Fpath%2Ftests%2Fdisplay_shell.rs",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/crates%2Fmise-util%2Fsrc%2Fpath%2Ftests%2Fdisplay_shell.rs",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/crates%2Fmise-util%2Fsrc%2Fpath%2Ftests%2Fdisplay_shell.rs?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -302,6 +302,36 @@ fn test_cmd_verbatim_args() {\n     );\n }\n \n+#[test]\n+fn test_positional_cmd_command() {\n+    // How pitchfork runs a `mise = true` daemon: `mise x -- cmd /C `.\n+    let args = sv(&[\"/C\", r#\"echo init && \"C:\\a b\\mise.exe\" run \"dev\"\"#]);\n+    assert_eq!(\n+        positional_cmd_command(&args),\n+        Some((&args[..1], args[1].as_str()))\n+    );\n+    // Other switches may come first, and `/k` runs a command too.\n+    let args = sv(&[\"/d\", \"/s\", \"/k\", r#\"echo \"a b\"\"#]);\n+    assert_eq!(\n+        positional_cmd_command(&args),\n+        Some((&args[..3], args[3].as_str()))\n+    );\n+    // Without a `\"`, std's quoting already reaches cmd intact: a path with a\n+    // space works because cmd keeps the only two quotes std adds.\n+    assert_eq!(\n+        positional_cmd_command(&sv(&[\"/c\", r\"C:\\Program Files\\app.exe\"])),\n+        None\n+    );\n+    // Several arguments after `/c` stay arguments, not one command.\n+    assert_eq!(\n+        positional_cmd_command(&sv(&[\"/c\", r#\"echo \"one\"\"#, \"two\"])),\n+        None\n+    );\n+    assert_eq!(positional_cmd_command(&sv(&[\"x\", \"/c\", r#\"\"a\"\"#])), None);\n+    assert_eq!(positional_cmd_command(&sv(&[r#\"\"a\"\"#])), None);\n+    assert_eq!(positional_cmd_command(&[]), None);\n+}\n+\n #[test]\n fn test_quote_arg_for_cmd_body() {\n     // No special chars -> returned as-is (no quotes added)."
    },
    {
