    {
      "sha": "47cbbf43171353b33ff2b753e96cfb38719ce2cb",
      "filename": "docs/.vitepress/theme/HomeShowreel.vue",
      "status": "modified",
      "additions": 18,
      "deletions": 2,
      "changes": 20,
      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2FHomeShowreel.vue",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2FHomeShowreel.vue",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2FHomeShowreel.vue?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -229,17 +229,30 @@ onUnmounted(() => window.removeEventListener(\"hashchange\", focusOnArrival));\n \n \n \n+/* Two buttons side by side at every width: on a phone they share the row\n+   rather than wrapping the selected one onto a line of its own. Inside a\n+   button the runtime may drop under the label where the row is too narrow\n+   for both (a 320 px phone has 272 px between the gutters). */\n .home-showreel-editions {\n   display: flex;\n   gap: 8px;\n   margin-bottom: 18px;\n-  flex-wrap: wrap;\n }\n .home-showreel-editions button {\n-  padding: 10px 16px;\n+  flex: 1 1 0;\n+  min-width: 0;\n+  min-height: 44px;\n+  padding: 10px 12px;\n   border: 1px solid var(--vp-c-border);\n   border-radius: 8px;\n   font-weight: 600;\n+  line-height: 1.3;\n+}\n+@media (min-width: 641px) {\n+  .home-showreel-editions button {\n+    flex: 0 0 auto;\n+    padding: 10px 16px;\n+  }\n }\n .home-showreel-editions button[aria-pressed=\"true\"] {\n   background: var(--vp-c-brand-soft);\n@@ -256,11 +269,14 @@ onUnmounted(() => window.removeEventListener(\"hashchange\", focusOnArrival));\n   gap: 8px 18px;\n   margin-top: 20px;\n }\n+/* Each chapter a 44 px touch target, on one line. */\n .home-showreel-chapters button {\n   color: var(--vp-c-text-1);\n   font-size: 14px;\n+  min-height: 44px;\n   padding: 6px 0;\n   text-align: left;\n+  white-space: nowrap;\n }\n .home-showreel-chapters button span {\n   color: var(--vp-c-brand-1);"
    },
    {
      "sha": "f9c7b1b3b772f6fcf5e8d0ecf25a1c5dbb6eb37d",
      "filename": "docs/.vitepress/theme/showreel/STORYBOARD.md",
      "status": "modified",
      "additions": 31,
      "deletions": 18,
      "changes": 49,
      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2Fshowreel%2FSTORYBOARD.md",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2Fshowreel%2FSTORYBOARD.md",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2Fshowreel%2FSTORYBOARD.md?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -7,25 +7,38 @@ scenes below. The source scene clock remains intact for the pacing, capture and\n sound-design checks; all delivered picture, audio, chapter and runtime metadata\n uses the edit clock.\n \n-- **Full tour: 4:44.** A six-second opening with tools, env vars and tasks in a\n-  project config leads straight to project switching. The project workflow,\n-  installing jq, registry, environments, task dependencies and skipping,\n-  dotfile history, lockfile/CI, bootstrap and fresh-clone demos follow at their\n-  recorded reading pace. Backend, packslip, redaction, argument and daemon\n-  walkthroughs are omitted. The eight-second install card replaces the chef\n-  morph and extended ending.\n-- **Quick overview: 1:16.** Opening, project switching, installing jq, task\n-  dependencies and install card. Both editions use the same source captures.\n-- **Presentation.** Brand cards use large Space Grotesk type, the three pillar\n-  colours and a simple ruled layout. The poster uses two large project/version\n-  cards; the player supplies the selected runtime. Large command/result\n-  callouts in the switching and project-workflow scenes quote the current\n-  capture's rows. They contain no retyped version numbers.\n+- **Full tour: 4:58.** The open through its name card and its leave (10.75 s)\n+  leads straight to project switching, cut before its fold into packslip's\n+  card (19.5 s). The\n+  project workflow, installing jq, registry, environments, task dependencies\n+  and skipping, dotfile history, lockfile/CI, bootstrap and fresh-clone demos\n+  follow at their recorded reading pace, then the morph and the end card\n+  (12 s: its last move at +9 s and a hold under the music's ending). Backend,\n+  packslip, redaction, argument and daemon walkthroughs are omitted.\n+- **Quick overview: 1:22.** The open, project switching, installing jq, task\n+  dependencies and the end card. Both editions use the same source captures.\n+- **Joins (`film.ts`).** Where the film puts two sections side by side that the\n+  source did not, the scenes are joined rather than dipped to black. A ticket\n+  takes down the stage of the section the film plays before it (`bible.ts`\n+  `Joins`, `kit/grey.ts` `G.prev`): the Environments ticket takes the\n+  registry's card, the Tasks ticket vars' card and terminal, the Dotfiles\n+  ticket skip's. Any other cut leaves on the kit's exit (fall 16 px and fade\n+  over 3/8 beat) and enters on its entrance (rise 24 px and fade in over 1/2\n+  beat) over the still stage (`compose.ts` `Reel.render`'s `frame`). The\n+  source's own bar lines carry straight on. Tickets are numbered in the\n+  film's order (ACT I to VI in the tour).\n+- **Presentation.** Nothing is drawn that the source does not draw: the brand\n+  cards are the open and the end card, the poster is the source's poster\n+  frame (`reel.ts` `POSTER_TIME`), and the type, colours and motion are the\n+  kit's (ART.md).\n - **Audio.** Only sound effects follow the picture edit. `film-music.ts`\n-  arranges the original song independently, removing long breakdowns and drum\n-  breaks while preserving its complete final chorus, wind-down and ring-out.\n-  Both buses pass through one master; retained accents duck the music lightly.\n-  The source reel's breath fader and music jumps are never copied into a film.\n+  arranges the original song on the film clock, cut on its own bar lines: the\n+  first groove drops as the open's card leaves, the song's break, build and\n+  loudest groove fall under `mise use jq`, bootstrap and the clone's climax,\n+  and its complete wind-down, outro and ring-out play under the morph and the\n+  end card, ending a few frames before the film. Both buses pass through one\n+  master; retained accents duck the music lightly. The source reel's breath\n+  fader and bed arrangement are never copied into a film.\n - **Player.** Both editions have visible chapter buttons and their own WebVTT\n   track. Switching editions resets playback; a chapter starts at its displayed\n   time, including before the first metadata load. Fullscreen remains available."
    },

      "sha": "bf29dd525ca7c4a7ab154634d9c2154e6d14ef24",
      "filename": "docs/.vitepress/theme/showreel/bible.ts",
      "status": "modified",
      "additions": 21,
      "deletions": 1,
      "changes": 22,
      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2Fshowreel%2Fbible.ts",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2Fshowreel%2Fbible.ts",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2Fshowreel%2Fbible.ts?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -8,6 +8,7 @@\n // social card and the hero terminal), and the facts are left open for the\n // capture run's versions file.\n \n+import type { BoundaryId } from \"./handoff\";\n import type { Pillar, SectionId } from \"./timeline\";\n import type { Caption } from \"./type\";\n \n@@ -150,12 +151,31 @@ export const TERM = {\n  */\n export type ReelFacts = Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>;\n \n+/**\n+ * The bar lines a section meets in a film that plays the sections out of\n+ * the source order (film.ts): `in`, the bar line it starts from, whose\n+ * persistent layers it inherits; `out`, the one it ends on. A ticket takes\n+ * down the stage of the section the film plays before it, not the source's,\n+ * so a scene reads its bar lines from kit/grey.ts `G.prev` and `G.next`,\n+ * which take these over the timeline's own (handoff.ts handoffIn,\n+ * handoffOut). Absent, a section keeps its own bar lines.\n+ */\n+export interface Join {\n+  in?: BoundaryId;\n+  out?: BoundaryId;\n+  /** A ticket's meta line (\"ACT II\"): the act's number in the film's order. */\n+  meta?: string;\n+}\n+export type Joins = Partial<Record<SectionId, Join>>;\n+\n export interface SceneEnv {\n   W: number;\n   H: number;\n   /** Global time in seconds. */\n   t: number;\n   facts: ReelFacts | null;\n+  /** A film's joins (Join); none in the source reel. */\n+  joins?: Joins;\n }\n \n export interface Scene {\n@@ -180,7 +200,7 @@ export interface Scene {\n    * facts as `draw`, so a scene whose picture depends on them needs no\n    * state carried over from the frame it last drew.\n    */\n-  lit?(lt: number, env: Pick<SceneEnv, \"facts\">): LitRect | null;\n+  lit?(lt: number, env: Pick<SceneEnv, \"facts\" | \"joins\">): LitRect | null;\n }\n \n /**"
    },
    {
      "sha": "d4d59c9d0c7e306303063d58a4ba0245b6d731e2",
      "filename": "docs/.vitepress/theme/showreel/compose.ts",
      "status": "modified",
      "additions": 83,
      "deletions": 10,
      "changes": 93,
      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2Fshowreel%2Fcompose.ts",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2Fshowreel%2Fcompose.ts",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2Fshowreel%2Fcompose.ts?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -9,7 +9,11 @@\n // passes take the stage's numbers (kit/style.ts STAGE_FX: the vignette at\n // 0.42, the grain). Each scene paints the stage itself, lamp and all\n // (fx.ts drawStage, from kit/grey.ts greyScene), as the bar lines' frames do\n-// (handoff.ts).\n+// (handoff.ts). A film (film.ts) that cuts between sections the source\n+// never put side by side hands the scenes its joins (bible.ts Joins) and\n+// may ask for a frame with the scene standing off its mark (Presence): it\n+// is then drawn on a layer of its own over the stage, so the stage, the\n+// vignette and the grain stay still while the scene enters or leaves.\n \n import {\n   actOf,\n@@ -18,27 +22,37 @@ import {\n   CHAPTERS,\n   DURATION,\n   H,\n+  type Joins,\n   PALETTE,\n   type ReelFacts,\n   type Scene,\n+  type SceneEnv,\n   sec,\n   W,\n } from \"./bible\";\n import { rgba } from \"./color\";\n-import { grain, vignette } from \"./fx\";\n+import { drawStage, grain, makeCanvas, vignette } from \"./fx\";\n+import type { Presence } from \"./kit/motion\";\n import { STAGE_FX } from \"./kit/style\";\n import { clamp } from \"./math\";\n import { drawCaptions, font, MONO, timeCaptions } from \"./type\";\n \n export interface Reel {\n   duration: number;\n   chapters: Chapter[];\n-  /** Draw the frame at `t` seconds into a canvas `pw` × `ph` device pixels. */\n+  /**\n+   * Draw the frame at `t` seconds into a canvas `pw` × `ph` device pixels.\n+   * With `frame`, the scene stands off its mark as the presence says (its\n+   * opacity and how far it is below its place, logical px), on a layer over\n+   * the still stage: how a film enters and leaves a scene it cuts to\n+   * (film.ts).\n+   */\n   render(\n     ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,\n     t: number,\n     pw: number,\n     ph: number,\n+    frame?: Presence,\n   ): void;\n }\n \n@@ -51,6 +65,12 @@ export interface ReelOptions {\n    * frames only; the published render never sets it.\n    */\n   burnIn?: boolean;\n+  /**\n+   * The bar lines a film gives the sections it plays out of the source\n+   * order (bible.ts Joins): a ticket then takes down the stage of the\n+   * section the film plays before it. None for the source reel.\n+   */\n+  joins?: Joins;\n }\n \n /** `m:ss.fff`, the slate's timecode. */\n@@ -79,6 +99,23 @@ function drawSlate(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, s: Scene, t: number): void {\n   ctx.restore();\n }\n \n+/** The layer a scene is drawn on when it stands off its mark (Reel.render's `frame`), one per canvas size. */\n+let layer: { canvas: HTMLCanvasElement; ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D } | null =\n+  null;\n+function sceneLayer(pw: number, ph: number): CanvasRenderingContext2D {\n+  if (!layer || layer.canvas.width !== pw || layer.canvas.height !== ph) {\n+    const canvas = makeCanvas(pw, ph);\n+    const ctx = canvas.getContext(\"2d\", { alpha: false });\n+    if (!ctx) throw new Error(\"no 2d context for the scene layer\");\n+    layer = { canvas, ctx };\n+  }\n+  return layer.ctx;\n+}\n+\n+/** Whether a presence leaves the scene exactly on its mark. */\n+const onMark = (p: Presence | undefined): boolean =>\n+  !p || (p.alpha >= 1 && p.dy === 0);\n+\n /**\n  * A reel of `scenes`, in timeline order. Given every section's scene it is\n  * the whole reel; given one, it draws that section on the reel's clock, so\n@@ -101,7 +138,7 @@ export function composeReel(\n   return {\n     duration: DURATION,\n     chapters: CHAPTERS,\n-    render(ctx, time, pw, ph) {\n+    render(ctx, time, pw, ph, frame) {\n       const t = clamp(time, 0, DURATION - 1e-6);\n       ctx.setTransform(pw / W, 0, 0, ph / H, 0, 0);\n       ctx.globalAlpha = 1;\n@@ -110,21 +147,57 @@ export function composeReel(\n       ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);\n       const s = sceneAt(t);\n       const lt = t - s.start;\n-      ctx.save();\n-      s.draw(ctx, lt, { W, H, t, facts });\n-      ctx.restore();\n+      const env: SceneEnv = { W, H, t, facts, joins: options.joins };\n+      // The scene off its mark (a film's entrance or exit): the stage\n+      // stays, and the scene's own frame, lamp and all, rises or falls over\n+      // it at the presence's opacity. The lamp is a soft gradient, so its\n+      // copy moving a few px under the fade is not seen.\n+      const off = onMark(frame) ? null : frame!;\n+      if (!off) {\n+        ctx.save();\n+        s.draw(ctx, lt, env);\n+        ctx.restore();\n+      } else {\n+        const lc = sceneLayer(pw, ph);\n+        lc.setTransform(pw / W, 0, 0, ph / H, 0, 0);\n+        lc.globalAlpha = 1;\n+        lc.globalCompositeOperation = \"source-over\";\n+        lc.save();\n+        s.draw(lc, lt, env);\n+        lc.restore();\n+        lc.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);\n+        drawStage(ctx, W, H, t);\n+        if (off.alpha > 0) {\n+          ctx.save();\n+          ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(off.alpha);\n+          ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, (off.dy * ph) / H);\n+          ctx.drawImage(lc.canvas, 0, 0);\n+          ctx.restore();\n+          ctx.setTransform(pw / W, 0, 0, ph / H, 0, 0);\n+        }\n+      }\n       if (!options.raw) {\n-        // Darker toward the corners, but not over a terminal's window, a lit screen.\n+        // Darker toward the corners, but not over a terminal's window, a lit\n+        // screen (standing where the scene stands).\n+        const lit = s.lit?.(lt, env) ?? null;\n         vignette(\n           ctx,\n           W,\n           H,\n           STAGE_FX.vignette.strength,\n-          s.lit?.(lt, { facts }) ?? null,\n+          off && lit\n+            ? { ...lit, y: lit.y + off.dy, alpha: lit.alpha * clamp(off.alpha) }\n+            : lit,\n         );\n         // Over the vignette, so a caption reads the same at the frame's edge;\n-        // under the grain, so it sits in the picture.\n+        // under the grain, so it sits in the picture. With the scene.\n+        ctx.save();\n+        if (off) {\n+          ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(off.alpha);\n+          ctx.translate(0, off.dy);\n+        }\n         drawCaptions(ctx, t, captions);\n+        ctx.restore();\n         grain(ctx, W, H, t, STAGE_FX.grain.amount, STAGE_FX.grain.fps);\n       }\n       if (options.burnIn) drawSlate(ctx, s, t);"
    },
    {
      "sha": "f09df665fe70ce570b6030a1fb8fbb979b33036b",
      "filename": "docs/.vitepress/theme/showreel/edit.ts",

      "patch": "@@ -1,29 +1,66 @@\n-// Editorial cuts share the recorded scenes at their original reading pace.\n-// The source clock stays intact for scene/sound checks; the delivered films\n-// have their own clock. Picture, effects and chapters use this same edit;\n-// music is arranged independently in film-music.ts.\n-import { chaptersVtt, sec, type Chapter, type SectionId } from \"./timeline.ts\";\n+// The landing-page films: editorial cuts of the source reel's sections,\n+// each played at its recorded reading pace on its own source clock. The\n+// source clock stays intact for the pacing, capture and sound-design checks;\n+// the delivered picture, effects and chapters use the film clock built\n+// here, and the music is arranged on it independently (film-music.ts).\n+//\n+// A shot is a whole section, or part of one where the source's own\n+// transition into a neighbour the film does not keep would otherwise play\n+// (the open's hand-over to the pitch's terminal, the switch's fold into\n+// packslip's card, the end card's long hold under the source bed's ending).\n+// Where the film puts two sections side by side that the source did not,\n+// film.ts joins them: a ticket takes down the stage of the section the film\n+// plays before it (bible.ts Joins), and any other cut leaves and enters on\n+// the kit's own exit and entrance (kit/motion.ts).\n+import { chaptersVtt, sec, type Chapter, type SectionId } from \"./timeline\";\n \n export type Edition = \"tour\" | \"overview\";\n export interface Cut {\n   id: SectionId;\n+  /** Film seconds. */\n   start: number;\n   end: number;\n+  /** Source (reel) seconds. */\n   from: number;\n   to: number;\n-  brand?: \"intro\" | \"outro\";\n }\n \n-type Shot =\n-  | SectionId\n-  | { id: SectionId; from: number; to: number; brand: \"intro\" | \"outro\" };\n-const INTRO: Shot = { id: \"open\", from: 0, to: 6, brand: \"intro\" };\n-// Replace the extended source ending with a concise install card.\n-const OUTRO: Shot = { id: \"end\", from: 6, to: 14, brand: \"outro\" };\n+/** A whole section, or a span of one in its own local seconds. */\n+type Shot = SectionId | { id: SectionId; from?: number; to?: number };\n+\n+/**\n+ * The open through the name card's hold and its leave (STORYBOARD.md Act\n+ * 0: `leave` 10.11–10.61, the chef an eighth behind the card), on the frame\n+ * grid, with a breath of empty stage before the next scene enters: the\n+ * pitch's terminal, which rises in at 11.11 in the source, belongs to the\n+ * pitch's own shot.\n+ */\n+export const OPEN_TO = 10.75;\n+/**\n+ * The switch to the end of its threads (STORYBOARD.md: `threads` 18.25–19,\n+ * then `fold` from 19.5, api/ growing into the card packslip carries on\n+ * with). The films play no packslip, so they cut before the fold; the\n+ * film's exit takes the diagram down instead (test/edit.test.ts holds this\n+ * to the storyboard's `fold`).\n+ */\n+export const SWITCH_TO = 19.5;\n+/**\n+ * The end card through its last move (+9 s, kit/namecard.ts END_CUES) and a\n+ * hold under the music's ending (film-music.ts), rather than the source's\n+ * 14 s under the bed's own.\n+ */\n+export const END_TO = 12;\n+\n+const OPEN: Shot = { id: \"open\", to: OPEN_TO };\n+const SWITCH: Shot = { id: \"switch\", to: SWITCH_TO };\n+const END: Shot = { id: \"end\", to: END_TO };\n+\n const SHOTS: Record<Edition, readonly Shot[]> = {\n+  // The brand's open, project switching first, then the project workflow\n+  // and the acts in the source's order, the morph and the end card.\n   tour: [\n-    INTRO,\n-    \"switch\",\n+    OPEN,\n+    SWITCH,\n     \"pitch\",\n     \"tools\",\n     \"use\",\n@@ -41,74 +78,80 @@ const SHOTS: Record<Edition, readonly Shot[]> = {\n     \"bootstrap\",\n     \"breath\",\n     \"clone\",\n-    OUTRO,\n+    \"morph\",\n+    END,\n   ],\n-  overview: [INTRO, \"switch\", \"use\", \"depends\", OUTRO],\n+  // The morph needs the clone's card before it; the overview goes straight\n+  // to the end card.\n+  overview: [OPEN, SWITCH, \"use\", \"depends\", END],\n };\n \n-/** Map retained scenes to film time without shortening their reading holds. */\n+/** Map the shots onto film time, each at its source pace. */\n export function cuts(edition: Edition): Cut[] {\n   let at = 0;\n   return SHOTS[edition].map((shot) => {\n     const id = typeof shot === \"string\" ? shot : shot.id;\n     const s = sec(id);\n-    const from = s.start + (typeof shot === \"string\" ? 0 : shot.from);\n-    const to = typeof shot === \"string\" ? s.end : s.start + shot.to;\n+    const from = s.start + (typeof shot === \"string\" ? 0 : (shot.from ?? 0));\n+    const to =\n+      typeof shot === \"string\" || shot.to === undefined\n+        ? s.end\n+        : s.start + shot.to;\n     const start = at;\n     at += to - from;\n-    return {\n-      id,\n-      start,\n-      end: at,\n-      from,\n-      to,\n-      ...(typeof shot === \"string\" ? {} : { brand: shot.brand }),\n-    };\n+    return { id, start, end: at, from, to };\n   });\n }\n \n-/** Runtime of the retained shots, in delivered-film seconds. */\n+/**\n+ * Whether cut `b` carries straight on from cut `a` on the source clock:\n+ * the source's own bar line between them, which the handoff holds (no\n+ * join, no exit or entrance).\n+ */\n+export const continuous = (a: Cut, b: Cut): boolean => a.to === b.from;\n+\n+/** Runtime of the film, in film seconds. */\n export const filmDuration = (edition: Edition) => cuts(edition).at(-1)!.end;\n /** Locate a film shot; hold the final shot after the film ends. */\n export function cutAt(edition: Edition, time: number): Cut {\n   const list = cuts(edition);\n   return list.find((cut) => time < cut.end) ?? list[list.length - 1];\n }\n \n-/** Group edited shots into contiguous chapters, including the brand cards. */\n+/**\n+ * The player's chapter labels, by act: the acts' own names (timeline.ts\n+ * ACTS, the tickets' titles), but for the switching chapter, which the\n+ * source has no act for, and the end card.\n+ */\n+const LABELS = {\n+  mise: \"mise\",\n+  install: \"Install mise\",\n+  versions: \"Project switching\",\n+  \"dev-tools\": \"Dev tools\",\n+  environments: \"Environments\",\n+  tasks: \"Tasks\",\n+  dotfiles: \"Dotfiles\",\n+  everywhere: \"Everywhere\",\n+  \"new-machine\": \"A new machine\",\n+} as const;\n+\n+/** Group the cuts into contiguous chapters, one per act the film visits. */\n export function filmChapters(edition: Edition): Chapter[] {\n   const chapters: Chapter[] = [];\n   for (const cut of cuts(edition)) {\n-    const s = sec(cut.id);\n-    const id =\n-      cut.brand === \"intro\"\n-        ? \"mise\"\n-        : cut.brand === \"outro\"\n-          ? \"install\"\n-          : s.act;\n+    const id = sec(cut.id).act;\n     const last = chapters.at(-1);\n     if (last?.id === id) {\n       last.end = cut.end;\n-      if (!cut.brand) last.sections.push(cut.id);\n+      last.sections.push(cut.id);\n     } else {\n-      const labels = {\n-        mise: \"Overview\",\n-        install: \"Install mise\",\n-        versions: \"Project switching\",\n-        \"dev-tools\": \"Dev tools\",\n-        environments: \"Environments\",\n-        tasks: \"Tasks\",\n-        dotfiles: \"Dotfiles\",\n-        everywhere: \"In CI\",\n-        \"new-machine\": \"A new machine\",\n-      };\n       chapters.push({\n         id,\n         numeral: \"\",\n-        label: labels[id],\n+        label: LABELS[id],\n         start: cut.start,\n         end: cut.end,\n-        sections: cut.brand ? [] : [cut.id],\n+        sections: [cut.id],\n       });\n     }\n   }"

      "patch": "@@ -1,13 +1,27 @@\n // Cut only the effects with the picture. Arrange the original song on its\n-// own musical clock, then mix both through one master. The old source\n-// reel's fader and music edits never enter the delivered films.\n+// own musical clock, then mix both through one master. The source reel's\n+// section fader and its bed's arrangement never enter the delivered films.\n+// The pitched effects are struck on the chord the film's music plays under\n+// them (film-music.ts SONG_CHART, score/harmony.ts setFilmChart), not on the\n+// bed's chord at the source moment.\n import { playFilmMix, playScore } from \"./audio\";\n import type { ReelFacts } from \"./bible\";\n-import { cuts, filmDuration, type Edition } from \"./edit\";\n-import { musicChannel } from \"./film-music\";\n+import { continuous, cuts, filmDuration, type Edition } from \"./edit\";\n+import { filmChordAt, musicChannel } from \"./film-music\";\n+import { type Chord, setFilmChart } from \"./score/harmony\";\n import { type Dip, LATENCY } from \"./score/mix\";\n import { DURATION } from \"./timeline\";\n \n+/**\n+ * The chord the film's music plays under reel second `t`: the song's at the\n+ * film moment the picture's cut maps `t` to, or null where the film does\n+ * not play that moment (a cut section) or its music has ended.\n+ */\n+export function reelChord(edition: Edition, t: number): Chord | null {\n+  const cut = cuts(edition).find((c) => t >= c.from && t < c.to);\n+  return cut ? filmChordAt(edition, cut.start + (t - cut.from)) : null;\n+}\n+\n /** Render edited effects and independently arranged music at the supplied rate. */\n export async function filmSoundtrack(\n   facts: ReelFacts | null,\n@@ -21,17 +35,26 @@ export async function filmSoundtrack(\n     Math.ceil(rate * (DURATION + preRoll)),\n     rate,\n   );\n-  const sourceAccents = playScore(\n-    sourceContext,\n-    sourceContext.destination,\n-    0,\n-    preRoll,\n-    facts,\n-    null,\n-    true,\n-    false,\n-  );\n-  const source = await sourceContext.startRendering();\n+  // The parts read their chords while they are wired in, which the offline\n+  // render does as it goes, so the film's chart stays set until it is done.\n+  setFilmChart((t) => reelChord(edition, t));\n+  let source: AudioBuffer;\n+  let sourceAccents: readonly Dip[];\n+  try {\n+    sourceAccents = playScore(\n+      sourceContext,\n+      sourceContext.destination,\n+      0,\n+      preRoll,\n+      facts,\n+      null,\n+      true,\n+      false,\n+    );\n+    source = await sourceContext.startRendering();\n+  } finally {\n+    setFilmChart(null);\n+  }\n   const length = Math.round(filmDuration(edition) * rate);\n   const effects = new AudioBuffer({\n     numberOfChannels: 2,\n@@ -47,15 +70,14 @@ export async function filmSoundtrack(\n   const list = cuts(edition);\n   for (let k = 0; k < list.length; k++) {\n     const cut = list[k];\n-    if (cut.brand) continue;\n     const offset = Math.round((preRoll - LATENCY + cut.from) * rate);\n     const at = Math.round(cut.start * rate);\n     const n = Math.round((cut.end - cut.start) * rate);\n-    const incoming = !k || list[k - 1].to !== cut.from || !!list[k - 1].brand;\n-    const outgoing =\n-      k === list.length - 1 ||\n-      list[k + 1].from !== cut.to ||\n-      !!list[k + 1].brand;\n+    // A cut the source did not make gets a short ramp either side, so no\n+    // effect's tail or attack is cut mid-sample; the source's own bar lines\n+    // carry straight on.\n+    const incoming = !k || !continuous(list[k - 1], cut);\n+    const outgoing = k === list.length - 1 || !continuous(cut, list[k + 1]);\n     const ramp = Math.round(rate * 0.008);\n     for (let channel = 0; channel < 2; channel++) {\n       const from = source.getChannelData(channel);"

      "patch": "@@ -1,6 +1,10 @@\n-// The original song, independently arranged for each film. Picture cuts\n-// never move the music playhead or inherit the source reel's quiet breath.\n+// The original song, arranged for each film on the film's own clock. The\n+// picture's cuts never move the music: the song is cut on its own bar\n+// lines (120 BPM, a bar every 2 s), each join a crossfade on a downbeat,\n+// and its complete ending (the wind-down, the outro and the ring-out) plays\n+// under the end card, so the music stops where the film does.\n import { filmDuration, type Edition } from \"./edit\";\n+import { CH, type Chord } from \"./score/harmony\";\n \n export const MUSIC = {\n   file: \"docs/.vitepress/theme/showreel/score/music.opus\",\n@@ -9,53 +13,170 @@ export const MUSIC = {\n   duration: 359.615,\n } as const;\n \n+/** A bar of the song, seconds (120 BPM, 4/4). */\n+export const BAR = 2;\n+/** The song's last bar line plus its ring-out, in bars: where every arrangement ends. */\n+export const SONG_END = MUSIC.duration / BAR;\n+\n export interface MusicCut {\n+  /** Song seconds. */\n   from: number;\n   to: number;\n+  /** Film seconds. */\n   start: number;\n   end: number;\n }\n \n-/** All joins land on the original 120 BPM downbeats, without time stretching. */\n+/**\n+ * The song's form, in bars (score/BED.md): intro 0–15 (no drums), groove\n+ * 16–43, break 44–47, groove 48–75, riser 76–79, breakdown 80–95 (no drums),\n+ * groove 96–109, break 110–111, groove 112–135, build 136–143, groove\n+ * 144–155, break 156–159, the loudest groove 160–167, wind-down 168–171,\n+ * outro 172–178, which rings out.\n+ *\n+ * Each arrangement starts in the intro's last bars, so the first groove\n+ * drops as the open's name card leaves and the first demonstration begins\n+ * (film 10 s, edit.ts OPEN_TO), and ends on the song's own ending, timed so\n+ * the ring-out dies out a few frames before the film ends: the sum of the\n+ * cuts is the film's length (edit.ts) less the ring-out's tail.\n+ *\n+ * Tour (298 s): bars 11–79 straight through (the first two grooves, the\n+ * break under `mise use jq`'s install, the riser ending in depends), then\n+ * 96–151 (the third and fourth grooves, with the build under bootstrap),\n+ * skipping only the drumless breakdown and the last phrase of the fifth\n+ * groove, then 156 to the end: the break under the clone command typing,\n+ * the loudest groove dropping on its install rows and carrying the climax\n+ * (`[ci] api ready`), the wind-down under the morph's lift, the outro's\n+ * soft kick on the toque's drop, and the ring-out under the end card.\n+ *\n+ * Overview (82 s): bars 11–39 (the intro's tail and six phrases of the\n+ * first groove, under the open, the switch and `mise use jq`), then 168 to\n+ * the end: the wind-down under depends' lanes and the outro under the end\n+ * card.\n+ */\n+const BARS: Record<Edition, readonly (readonly [from: number, to: number])[]> =\n+  {\n+    tour: [\n+      [11, 80],\n+      [96, 152],\n+      [156, SONG_END],\n+    ],\n+    overview: [\n+      [11, 40],\n+      [168, SONG_END],\n+    ],\n+  };\n+\n+/** All joins land on the song's downbeats, without time stretching. */\n export function musicCuts(edition: Edition): MusicCut[] {\n-  // Tour: four intro bars, grooves/builds, then the complete final chorus\n-  // and wind-down. Remove the song's long breakdown and its drum breaks.\n-  // Overview: one groove followed by the complete wind-down and outro.\n-  const bars =\n-    edition === \"tour\"\n-      ? [\n-          [12, 44],\n-          [48, 80],\n-          [96, 110],\n-          [112, 156],\n-          [160, MUSIC.duration / 2],\n-        ]\n-      : [\n-          [16, 42],\n-          [168, MUSIC.duration / 2],\n-        ];\n   let start = 0;\n-  return bars.map(([a, b]) => {\n-    const from = a * 2;\n-    const to = b * 2;\n+  return BARS[edition].map(([a, b]) => {\n+    const from = a * BAR;\n+    const to = b * BAR;\n     const cut = { from, to, start, end: start + to - from };\n     start = cut.end;\n     return cut;\n   });\n }\n \n+/** Where the music ends on the film clock: the end of its ring-out. */\n+export const musicEnd = (edition: Edition): number =>\n+  musicCuts(edition).at(-1)!.end;\n+\n+/**\n+ * The song's chords, a bar line at a time (score/harmony.ts CH): read off\n+ * the track's chroma, bar by bar. The grooves vamp round F minor, C minor\n+ * and B♭ (four bars each, F minor eight at a time); the breaks, riser,\n+ * build and the loudest groove sit on F minor; the outro moves C minor,\n+ * B♭, F minor and rings out on F minor. Through bars 112–135 the F's third\n+ * is unclear (score/BED.md), so the score's F there leaves it out (F5), as\n+ * the source's parts do. The breakdown (80–95), which no film plays, is\n+ * given to the nearest bar line of its wandering changes.\n+ */\n+export const SONG_CHART: readonly (readonly [bar: number, chord: Chord])[] = [\n+  [0, CH.Fm],\n+  [4, CH.Cm],\n+  [7, CH.Bb],\n+  [12, CH.Fm],\n+  [15, CH.Cm],\n+  [16, CH.Fm],\n+  [20, CH.Cm],\n+  [24, CH.Bb],\n+  [28, CH.Fm],\n+  [36, CH.Cm],\n+  [40, CH.Bb],\n+  [44, CH.Fm],\n+  [48, CH.Fm],\n+  [52, CH.Cm],\n+  [56, CH.Bb],\n+  [60, CH.Fm],\n+  [68, CH.Cm],\n+  [72, CH.Bb],\n+  [76, CH.Fm],\n+  [80, CH.Fm],\n+  [84, CH.Cm],\n+  [88, CH.Bb],\n+  [92, CH.Fm],\n+  [96, CH.Fm],\n+  [100, CH.Cm],\n+  [104, CH.Bb],\n+  [108, CH.Fm],\n+  [112, CH.F5],\n+  [116, CH.Cm],\n+  [120, CH.Bb],\n+  [124, CH.F5],\n+  [132, CH.Cm],\n+  [136, CH.Bb],\n+  [140, CH.Fm],\n+  [144, CH.Fm],\n+  [148, CH.Cm],\n+  [152, CH.Fm],\n+  [156, CH.Fm],\n+  [160, CH.Fm],\n+  [168, CH.Fm],\n+  [172, CH.Cm],\n+  [173, CH.Bb],\n+  [176, CH.Fm],\n+];\n+\n+/** The chord the song plays at song second `t`. */\n+export function songChordAt(t: number): Chord {\n+  const bar = t / BAR;\n+  let c = SONG_CHART[0][1];\n+  for (const [at, ch] of SONG_CHART) if (at <= bar + 1e-6) c = ch;\n+  return c;\n+}\n+\n+/**\n+ * The chord under film second `time` of `edition`: the song's at the bar\n+ * the arrangement plays there, or null after the music has ended.\n+ */\n+export function filmChordAt(edition: Edition, time: number): Chord | null {\n+  const cut = musicCuts(edition).find((c) => time >= c.start && time < c.end);\n+  return cut ? songChordAt(cut.from + time - cut.start) : null;\n+}\n+\n+/**\n+ * The crossfade at a join, seconds: a splice long enough to carry no click,\n+ * short enough that the outgoing tail does not notch the incoming downbeat.\n+ */\n+const JOIN = 0.05;\n+\n /** Crossfade preceding tails; incoming downbeats stay at full level. */\n export function musicChannel(\n   source: Float32Array,\n   edition: Edition,\n   rate: number,\n ): Float32Array<ArrayBuffer> {\n   const output = new Float32Array(Math.round(filmDuration(edition) * rate));\n-  const ramp = Math.round(0.15 * rate);\n+  const ramp = Math.round(JOIN * rate);\n   for (const cut of musicCuts(edition)) {\n     const at = Math.round(cut.start * rate);\n     const from = Math.round(cut.from * rate);\n-    const length = Math.round((cut.to - cut.from) * rate);\n+    const length = Math.min(\n+      Math.round((cut.to - cut.from) * rate),\n+      output.length - at,\n+    );\n     output.set(source.subarray(from, from + length), at);\n     if (at) {\n       for (let i = 0; i < ramp; i++) {"

      "patch": "@@ -1,275 +1,122 @@\n-// The landing-page films: an editorial cut of the recorded scenes, with\n-// new, concise brand cards and large callouts drawn from those same takes.\n-import type { ReelFacts } from \"./bible\";\n-import { capture, fileOf, frameAt, reelData, rowText } from \"./captures\";\n-import type { Reel } from \"./compose\";\n-import { cutAt, cuts, filmChapters, filmDuration, type Edition } from \"./edit\";\n-import { clipTime } from \"./kit/grey\";\n-import { createReel } from \"./reel\";\n-import { pacing as pitchPacing } from \"./scenes/pitch\";\n-import { pacing as switchPacing } from \"./scenes/switch\";\n-import { sec, W, H } from \"./bible\";\n-import { DISPLAY, font, MONO } from \"./type\";\n+// The landing-page films: the source reel's scenes, played on the film\n+// clock edit.ts lays out, each at its recorded pace. Where the film cuts\n+// between two sections the source never put side by side, the scenes are\n+// joined here rather than dipped to black: a ticket takes down the stage of\n+// the section the film plays before it (bible.ts Joins, kit/grey.ts G.prev),\n+// so the bar line it starts from is the frame the film arrives on; any\n+// other cut leaves on the kit's exit (fall 16 px and fade over 3/8 beat)\n+// and enters on its entrance (rise 24 px and fade in over 1/2 beat), over\n+// the still stage (compose.ts Reel.render's `frame`). The source's own bar\n+// lines carry straight on. The tickets are numbered in the order the film\n+// plays them.\n+import { BEAT, type Join, type Joins, type ReelFacts, sec } from \"./bible\";\n+import type { Reel, ReelOptions } from \"./compose\";\n+import {\n+  continuous,\n+  type Cut,\n+  cuts,\n+  type Edition,\n+  filmChapters,\n+  filmDuration,\n+} from \"./edit\";\n+import { boundaryOut } from \"./kit/grey\";\n+import { enter, exit, type Presence } from \"./kit/motion\";\n+import { DUR } from \"./kit/style\";\n+import { createReel, POSTER_TIME } from \"./reel\";\n \n-const COLORS = {\n-  bg: \"#171417\",\n-  paper: \"#f4eee3\",\n-  muted: \"#c2b6a4\",\n-  tools: \"#ed9fbc\",\n-  env: \"#e1bd87\",\n-  tasks: \"#adce9c\",\n-  panel: \"#211d21\",\n-};\n-const fade = (t: number, at = 0, duration = 0.35) =>\n-  Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (t - at) / duration));\n+const ROMAN = [\"I\", \"II\", \"III\", \"IV\", \"V\", \"VI\", \"VII\", \"VIII\", \"IX\"];\n \n-/** Draw a line of type, scaling long recorded strings to the safe width. */\n-function text(\n-  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,\n-  value: string,\n-  x: number,\n-  y: number,\n-  size: number,\n-  color: string = COLORS.paper,\n-  mono = false,\n-  width = 1600,\n-) {\n-  ctx.fillStyle = color;\n-  ctx.font = font(size, mono ? 500 : 600, mono ? MONO : DISPLAY);\n-  // Long captured strings keep their text and fit inside the safe area.\n-  const measured = ctx.measureText(value).width;\n-  if (measured > width)\n-    ctx.font = font(\n-      (size * width) / measured,\n-      mono ? 500 : 600,\n-      mono ? MONO : DISPLAY,\n-    );\n-  ctx.fillText(value, x, y);\n-}\n-\n-/** Paint the shared brand-card background, rule, name and site address. */\n-function stage(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D) {\n-  ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.bg;\n-  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);\n-  ctx.strokeStyle = \"#41353d\";\n-  ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n-  ctx.beginPath();\n-  ctx.moveTo(120, 155);\n-  ctx.lineTo(1800, 155);\n-  ctx.stroke();\n-  text(ctx, \"mise\", 120, 112, 58);\n-  text(ctx, \"mise.jdx.dev\", 1370, 112, 36, COLORS.muted);\n-}\n-\n-/** Reveal the three pillars beside an excerpt of the captured project config. */\n-function intro(\n-  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,\n-  t: number,\n-  facts: ReelFacts | null,\n-) {\n-  stage(ctx);\n-  const lines = [\"Dev tools.\", \"Environments.\", \"Tasks.\"];\n-  const colors = [COLORS.tools, COLORS.env, COLORS.tasks];\n-  lines.forEach((line, i) => {\n-    ctx.save();\n-    const a = fade(t, i * 0.35);\n-    ctx.globalAlpha = a;\n-    text(ctx, line, 120, 360 + i * 146 + 18 * (1 - a), 126, colors[i]);\n-    ctx.restore();\n+/**\n+ * The joins a film's cuts need (bible.ts Joins). A ticket that follows a\n+ * section the source did not put before it inherits that section's own\n+ * last bar line, the frame its last moment holds (handoff.ts), when the\n+ * film plays the section to its end; the ticket takes that stage down. Each\n+ * ticket's act is numbered in film order.\n+ */\n+export function filmJoins(edition: Edition): Joins {\n+  const joins: Joins = {};\n+  const list = cuts(edition);\n+  let acts = 0;\n+  list.forEach((cut, k) => {\n+    if (!sec(cut.id).ticket) return;\n+    const join: Join = { meta: `ACT ${ROMAN[acts++]}` };\n+    const prev = list[k - 1];\n+    if (prev && !continuous(prev, cut)) {\n+      const out = boundaryOut(prev.id);\n+      if (out && prev.to === sec(prev.id).end) join.in = out;\n+    }\n+    joins[cut.id] = join;\n   });\n-  ctx.save();\n-  ctx.globalAlpha = fade(t, 1.1);\n-  ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.panel;\n-  ctx.fillRect(1110, 250, 690, 580);\n-  text(ctx, \"mise.toml\", 1160, 330, 48, COLORS.paper, true, 590);\n-  const file = fileOf(reelData(facts), \"C2\", \"work/api/mise.toml\") ?? switchPacing(facts)\n-      : id === \"pitch\"\n-        ? pitchPacing(facts)\n-        : null;\n-  if (!plan) return null;\n-  const d = reelData(facts);\n-  const captureId = id === \"switch\" ? \"C7\" : \"C2\";\n-  const take = capture(d, captureId);\n-  const plays = plan.takes[captureId];\n-  if (!take || !plays?.length || localTime < plays[0].at) return null;\n-  const { ct } = clipTime(plays, localTime);\n-  const frame = take.frames[frameAt(take, ct)];\n-  const rows = frame.rows.map((r) => rowText(take, r).trimEnd());\n-  for (let i = rows.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n-    const command = rows[i].match(\n-      /^.*\\$ (node --version|echo APP_ENV=\\$APP_ENV)$/,\n-    )?.[1];\n-    if (!command) continue;\n-    const result = rows[i + 1]?.trim();\n-    // No partial commands, elapsed times, install summaries or invented output.\n-    if (result && /^(v\\S+|APP_ENV=\\S+)$/.test(result))\n-      return `${command}  →  ${result}`;\n-  }\n-  return null;\n+/**\n+ * How the scene of cut `k` stands at film time `time`: entering over its\n+ * first half beat after a cut the source did not make, leaving over the\n+ * 3/8 beat before the next; exactly on its mark otherwise, and on both\n+ * sides of every join a ticket makes.\n+ */\n+export function framePresence(\n+  list: readonly Cut[],\n+  k: number,\n+  time: number,\n+  joins: Joins,\n+): Presence {\n+  const cut = list[k];\n+  const b = (time - cut.start) / BEAT;\n+  const len = (cut.end - cut.start) / BEAT;\n+  const comes = arrives(list, k, joins);\n+  const goes = k + 1 < list.length && arrives(list, k + 1, joins);\n+  const i = comes ? enter(b, 0) : { alpha: 1, dy: 0 };\n+  const e = goes ? exit(b, len - DUR.exit) : { alpha: 1, dy: 0 };\n+  return { alpha: i.alpha * e.alpha, dy: i.dy + e.dy };\n }\n \n /** Render an edition on film time while replaying scenes on their source clock. */\n export function createFilm(\n   facts: ReelFacts | null,\n   edition: Edition = \"tour\",\n-  options = {},\n+  options: ReelOptions = {},\n ): Reel {\n-  const source = createReel(facts, options);\n+  const joins = filmJoins(edition);\n+  const source = createReel(facts, { ...options, joins });\n   const list = cuts(edition);\n   return {\n     duration: filmDuration(edition),\n     chapters: filmChapters(edition),\n     render(ctx, time, pw, ph) {\n-      const cut = cutAt(edition, time);\n+      let k = list.findIndex((cut) => time < cut.end);\n+      if (k < 0) k = list.length - 1;\n+      const cut = list[k];\n       const local = Math.max(\n         0,\n         Math.min(cut.end - cut.start - 1e-6, time - cut.start),\n       );\n-      const sourceTime = cut.from + local;\n-      if (!cut.brand) source.render(ctx, sourceTime, pw, ph);\n-      ctx.save();\n-      ctx.setTransform(pw / W, 0, 0, ph / H, 0, 0);\n-      if (cut.brand === \"intro\") intro(ctx, local, facts);\n-      else if (cut.brand === \"outro\") outro(ctx, local);\n-      else {\n-        const callout = commandCallout(\n-          cut.id,\n-          sourceTime - sec(cut.id).start,\n-          facts,\n-        );\n-        if (callout) text(ctx, callout, 160, 92, 64, COLORS.paper, true);\n-      }\n-      const index = list.findIndex((c) => c.start === cut.start);\n-      const previous = list[index - 1];\n-      const next = list[index + 1];\n-      // Continuous source neighbours keep their carefully matched handoff.\n-      // Editorial jumps dip briefly, concealing inherited source-stage props.\n-      const incoming =\n-        previous && previous.to !== cut.from ?       "patch": "@@ -10,6 +10,11 @@\n // `eval \"$(mise activate zsh)\"`) slides in 60 px from the right (ART.md §8\n // \"Enter\"), and the empty checkpoint rail under it and the terminal at\n // `~ $` for track rise in after it, a sixteenth apart, at rest by 1.85.\n+//\n+// The stage it takes down is the bar line it starts from in the reel being\n+// drawn (kit/grey.ts G.prev): daemons|dotfiles in the source reel; in a\n+// film that plays skip before it (film.ts), skip|args, whose card and\n+// terminal leave the same way.\n \n import { greyScene, keep, rest, stagePresence } from \"../kit/grey\";\n import { restOf } from \"../kit/rest\";\n@@ -31,36 +36,40 @@ export const scene = greyScene(\n   (g) => {\n     const { ctx, b } = g;\n     const out = EASE.glide(progress(OUT, OUT + OUT_DUR, b));\n-    const down = clearPresence(\"daemons|dotfiles\", b);\n-    restOf(\"daemons|dotfiles\").forEach((l, i) => {\n-      // The card slides out right; anything else (the terminal) falls.\n-      const card = l.kind === \"card\";\n-      const a = card ? 1 - out : down[i].alpha;\n-      if (a <= 0) return;\n-      keep(g, () => {\n-        ctx.save();\n-        if (card) ctx.translate(CARD_ART.swap * out, 0);\n-        else ctx.translate(0, down[i].dy);\n-        rest(g, \"daemons|dotfiles\", a, [i]);\n-        ctx.restore();\n+    const { prev, next } = g;\n+    if (prev) {\n+      const down = clearPresence(prev, b);\n+      restOf(prev).forEach((l, i) => {\n+        // The card slides out right; anything else (the terminal) falls.\n+        const card = l.kind === \"card\";\n+        const a = card ? 1 - out : down[i].alpha;\n+        if (a <= 0) return;\n+        keep(g, () => {\n+          ctx.save();\n+          if (card) ctx.translate(CARD_ART.swap * out, 0);\n+          else ctx.translate(0, down[i].dy);\n+          rest(g, prev, a, [i]);\n+          ctx.restore();\n+        });\n       });\n-    });\n+    }\n     // track's stage on the ticket's own entrance curve: the file card from\n     // the right, the rail and the terminal rising.\n-    stagePresence(\"dotfiles|track\", \"enter\", b).forEach((p, i) => {\n-      if (p.alpha <= 0) return;\n-      const k = p.dy / MOTION.riseIn;\n-      keep(g, () => {\n-        ctx.save();\n-        if (i === 0) ctx.translate(MOTION.slideIn * k, 0);\n-        else ctx.translate(0, p.dy);\n-        rest(g, \"dotfiles|track\", p.alpha, [i]);\n-        ctx.restore();\n+    if (next)\n+      stagePresence(next, \"enter\", b).forEach((p, i) => {\n+        if (p.alpha <= 0) return;\n+        const k = p.dy / MOTION.riseIn;\n+        keep(g, () => {\n+          ctx.save();\n+          if (i === 0) ctx.translate(MOTION.slideIn * k, 0);\n+          else ctx.translate(0, p.dy);\n+          rest(g, next, p.alpha, [i]);\n+          ctx.restore();\n+        });\n       });\n-    });\n-    drawTicketFor(ctx, \"dotfiles\", b);\n+    drawTicketFor(ctx, \"dotfiles\", b, { meta: g.join?.meta });\n   },\n-  { lit: (b) => ticketLit(\"dotfiles\", b) },\n+  { lit: (b, _d, joins) => ticketLit(\"dotfiles\", b, joins) },\n );\n \n /** The score's cues: the printer's steps, the tear, the lift-off. */"
    },

      "sha": "9305b62b50ca72617de982ee2a7b3d366082b9dc",

      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2Fshowreel%2Fscenes%2Fg1-brand%2Ftickets.ts",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2Fshowreel%2Fscenes%2Fg1-brand%2Ftickets.ts?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -19,11 +19,18 @@\n // print, tear and lift), from the ticket's own constants, so the printer\n // ticks, the tear's F and the lift's air land where the paper does.\n \n-import type { LitRect, SectionId } from \"../../bible\";\n-import { type BoundaryId, handoffIn, handoffOut } from \"../../handoff\";\n-import { type G, greyScene, keep, stagePresence } from \"../../kit/grey\";\n+import type { Joins, LitRect, SectionId } from \"../../bible\";\n+import type { BoundaryId } from \"../../handoff\";\n+import {\n+  boundaryIn,\n+  boundaryOut,\n+  type G,\n+  greyScene,\n+  keep,\n+  stagePresence,\n+} from \"../../kit/grey\";\n import type { Presence } from \"../../kit/motion\";\n-import { drawLayer, RESTS } from \"../../kit/rest\";\n+import { drawLayer, restLit, RESTS } from \"../../kit/rest\";\n import { DUR, EASE, MOTION, TICKET } from \"../../kit/style\";\n import { drawTicketFor, PRINT_TICKS } from \"../../kit/ticket\";\n import { progress } from \"../../math\";\n@@ -79,39 +86,47 @@ const litLayer = (id: BoundaryId): number =>\n /**\n  * A ticket's lit screen at beat `b` (kit/grey.ts sectionLit's ticket rule,\n  * on the tickets' own clear): the incoming bar line's window as it\n- * leaves, then the next one's as it arrives.\n+ * leaves, then the next one's as it arrives. The bar lines are the reel's\n+ * (kit/grey.ts boundaryIn, boundaryOut): a film's joins where it has them.\n  */\n-export function ticketLit(id: SectionId, b: number): LitRect | null {\n-  const hin = handoffIn(id);\n-  const hout = handoffOut(id);\n-  const at = (r: LitRect | null | undefined, k: number): LitRect | null =>\n+export function ticketLit(\n+  id: SectionId,\n+  b: number,\n+  joins?: Joins,\n+): LitRect | null {\n+  const hin = boundaryIn(id, joins);\n+  const hout = boundaryOut(id, joins);\n+  const at = (r: LitRect | null, k: number): LitRect | null =>\n     r && k > 0 ? { ...r, alpha: r.alpha * Math.min(1, k) } : null;\n-  if (hout?.lit) {\n-    const i = litLayer(hout.id);\n-    const k = i < 0 ? 0 : stagePresence(hout.id, \"enter\", b)[i].alpha;\n-    if (k > 0) return at(hout.lit, k);\n+  const z = hout ? restLit(hout) : null;\n+  if (hout && z) {\n+    const i = litLayer(hout);\n+    const k = i < 0 ? 0 : stagePresence(hout, \"enter\", b)[i].alpha;\n+    if (k > 0) return at(z, k);\n   }\n-  if (!hin?.lit) return null;\n-  const i = litLayer(hin.id);\n-  return at(hin.lit, i < 0 ? 0 : clearPresence(hin.id, b)[i].alpha);\n+  const a = hin ? restLit(hin) : null;\n+  if (!hin || !a) return null;\n+  const i = litLayer(hin);\n+  return at(a, i < 0 ? 0 : clearPresence(hin, b)[i].alpha);\n }\n \n /**\n  * A ticket section's scene: the stage it inherits taken down under the\n  * printing paper (clearPresence), the next section's brought in under the\n  * lifting ticket (the kit's entrance), and the act's ticket on its rail\n- * (kit/ticket.ts drawTicketFor), lit as ticketLit says.\n+ * (kit/ticket.ts drawTicketFor), lit as ticketLit says. The stage it\n+ * inherits is the bar line it starts from in the reel being drawn (G.prev):\n+ * the source's, or the section a film plays before it (film.ts).\n  */\n export function ticketScene(id: SectionId) {\n   return greyScene(\n     id,\n     (g) => {\n-      const prev = handoffIn(id);\n-      const next = handoffOut(id);\n-      if (prev) drawStageAs(g, prev.id, clearPresence(prev.id, g.b));\n-      if (next) drawStageAs(g, next.id, stagePresence(next.id, \"enter\", g.b));\n-      drawTicketFor(g.ctx, id, g.b);\n+      const { prev, next } = g;\n+      if (prev) drawStageAs(g, prev, clearPresence(prev, g.b));\n+      if (next) drawStageAs(g, next, stagePresence(next, \"enter\", g.b));\n+      drawTicketFor(g.ctx, id, g.b, { meta: g.join?.meta });\n     },\n-    { lit: (b) => ticketLit(id, b) },\n+    { lit: (b, _d, joins) => ticketLit(id, b, joins) },\n   );\n }"
    },

      "sha": "d2f2ab7aee19892748b1f7444c448200d9faaa8b",
      "filename": "docs/.vitepress/theme/showreel/score/BED.md",

      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2Fshowreel%2Fscore%2FBED.md",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2Fshowreel%2Fscore%2FBED.md?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -121,14 +121,24 @@ ffmpeg -i 'mise screenreel.wav' \\\n   docs/.vitepress/theme/showreel/score/music.opus\n ```\n \n-The tour plays source bars 12–44, 48–80, 96–110, 112–156, then 160 through\n-the complete ending. This removes the long breakdown and drum breaks without\n-cutting music at picture edits. Its last chorus begins at 4:04; the wind-down\n-and outro continue naturally through the closing card. The overview plays\n-bars 16–42, then 168 through the complete ending. Joins crossfade preceding\n-tails over 150 ms, reaching full level on the incoming downbeat. Both editions\n-leave just 0.385 s after the original ring-out. Arrangement and pin changes\n-belong in `film-music.ts`; the renderer checks the pin before decoding.\n+Each film is cut on the song's own bar lines (`film-music.ts` `musicCuts`),\n+never at a picture edit, and ends on the song's complete wind-down, outro and\n+ring-out, which dies out 0.385 s before the film does. The tour (4:58) plays\n+bars 11–79 straight through (the intro's tail under the open, the first groove\n+dropping at 0:10 as the open's card leaves and the switch begins, the break\n+under `mise use jq`'s install, the second groove, the riser ending in\n+depends), then 96–151 (the third and fourth grooves, the build under\n+bootstrap, the fifth groove to its last phrase), skipping only the drumless\n+breakdown and that phrase, then 156 to the end: the break under the clone\n+command typing, the loudest groove dropping at 4:18 on its install rows and\n+carrying the climax, the wind-down under the morph's lift, the outro's soft\n+kick on the toque's drop at 4:42, and the ring-out under the end card. The\n+overview (1:22) plays bars 11–39, then 168 to the end: the wind-down under\n+depends' lanes, the outro under the end card. Joins crossfade the outgoing\n+tail over 50 ms under the incoming downbeat. Arrangement and pin changes\n+belong in `film-music.ts`; the renderer checks the pin before decoding, and\n+`test/film-music.test.ts` holds each arrangement to the film's length, the\n+first groove's drop and the outro's place under the end card.\n \n `film-audio.ts` renders unmastered source effects without any music, cuts those\n with the picture, remaps their duck cues, and mixes them with the independently"
    },

      "patch": "@@ -62,18 +62,24 @@ export const part: Part = {\n   cues(m, s, facts) {\n     const c = listen(\"clone\", s, facts);\n     for (const t of c.all(\"slam\"))\n-      slam(m, t, chordAt(CHANGES, beatIn(s, t)).mid, 1.1, PAN.term);\n+      slam(m, t, chordAt(CHANGES, beatIn(s, t), t).mid, 1.1, PAN.term);\n     // Each header lights on its table's voice and its own tone of the\n     // chord (the one its piece rises to when it lands home), quieter than\n     // the landing.\n     TABLES.forEach((table, i) => {\n       for (const t of c.all(table))\n-        VOICES[table](m, t, chordAt(CHANGES, beatIn(s, t) + 1e-3).high[i], 0.7);\n+        VOICES[table](\n+          m,\n+          t,\n+          chordAt(CHANGES, beatIn(s, t) + 1e-3, t).high[i],\n+          0.7,\n+        );\n     });\n     for (const lane of LANES)\n       for (const t of c.all(lane))\n-        laneIn(m, t, lane, chordAt(CHANGES, beatIn(s, t) + 1e-3), 0.9);\n-    for (const t of c.all(\"ci\")) ciIn(m, t, chordAt(CHANGES, beatIn(s, t)), 0);\n+        laneIn(m, t, lane, chordAt(CHANGES, beatIn(s, t) + 1e-3, t), 0.9);\n+    for (const t of c.all(\"ci\"))\n+      ciIn(m, t, chordAt(CHANGES, beatIn(s, t), t), 0);\n     for (const t of c.all(\"ready\")) {\n       serviceBell(m, t, 0.06, 0);\n       serviceBell(m, t + 0.1, 0.04, 0.1, hz(96));\n@@ -85,7 +91,7 @@ export const part: Part = {\n     }\n     c.all(\"home\").forEach((t, i) => {\n       seat(m, t, 0.8);\n-      const tones = chordAt(CHANGES, beatIn(s, t) + 1e-3).high;\n+      const tones = chordAt(CHANGES, beatIn(s, t) + 1e-3, t).high;\n       VOICES[TABLES[i % 3]](m, t, tones[i % 3] + 12, 1);\n     });\n   },"

      "patch": "@@ -48,8 +48,30 @@ export const CH = {\n /** A section's changes: from each section beat on, a chord, until the next. */\n export type Changes = readonly (readonly [beat: number, chord: Chord])[];\n \n-/** The chord sounding at section beat `b`. */\n-export function chordAt(changes: Changes, b: number): Chord {\n+/**\n+ * A film's chart: the chord under reel second `t` where a film's music is\n+ * not the bed (film-audio.ts sets it round its render of the effects from\n+ * film-music.ts's song chart), or null where the film has no music under\n+ * that moment. Null in the source reel, whose parts follow their own\n+ * changes, set against the bed.\n+ */\n+type FilmChart = (t: number) => Chord | null;\n+let film: FilmChart | null = null;\n+\n+/** Use `chart` for the chords under the reel's moments (null: the parts' own changes). */\n+export function setFilmChart(chart: FilmChart | null): void {\n+  film = chart;\n+}\n+\n+/**\n+ * The chord sounding at section beat `b`: the film's, when a film chart is\n+ * set and the moment's reel second `t` is given, else the changes'.\n+ */\n+export function chordAt(changes: Changes, b: number, t?: number): Chord {\n+  if (film && t !== undefined) {\n+    const c = film(t);\n+    if (c) return c;\n+  }\n   let c = changes[0][1];\n   for (const [at, ch] of changes) if (at <= b + 1e-6) c = ch;\n   return c;"

      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2Fshowreel%2Ftest%2Fedit.test.ts",

      "patch": "@@ -1,21 +1,60 @@\n+// The landing-page films' edits (edit.ts) and joins (film.ts): every cut\n+// plays a span of one section at its source pace, the partial shots end\n+// where the storyboard's events say the source hands over to a neighbour\n+// the film does not keep, a ticket that follows a section the source did\n+// not put before it takes that section's stage down, every other cut the\n+// source did not make leaves and enters on the kit's curves, and the\n+// tickets are numbered in the film's order.\n+\n import assert from \"node:assert/strict\";\n+import { readFileSync } from \"node:fs\";\n+import { join } from \"node:path\";\n import { test } from \"node:test\";\n+import { BEAT } from \"../bible\";\n import {\n-  cuts,\n+  continuous,\n   cutAt,\n+  cuts,\n+  type Edition,\n+  END_TO,\n   filmChapters,\n   filmChaptersVtt,\n   filmDuration,\n-  type Edition,\n+  OPEN_TO,\n+  SWITCH_TO,\n } from \"../edit\";\n-import { commandCallout } from \"../film\";\n+import { createFilm, filmJoins, framePresence } from \"../film\";\n+import { handoffOut } from \"../handoff\";\n+import { setInkSink } from \"../kit/ink\";\n+import { END_CUES } from \"../kit/namecard\";\n+import { GLINT } from \"../kit/chef\";\n+import { DUR } from \"../kit/style\";\n import { loadFacts } from \"../load\";\n+import type { Reel } from \"../compose\";\n+import { createReel } from \"../reel\";\n import { sec } from \"../timeline\";\n-import { REPO } from \"./repo\";\n+import { recordingContext } from \"./record\";\n+import { REPO, SHOWREEL } from \"./repo\";\n import type { ReelFacts } from \"../bible\";\n \n-for (const edition of [\"tour\", \"overview\"] satisfies Edition[]) {\n-  test(`${edition}: picture and sound use contiguous cuts at the original reading pace`, () => {\n+const EDITIONS = [\"tour\", \"overview\"] satisfies Edition[];\n+\n+interface PlanEvent {\n+  at: number;\n+  end: number;\n+  name: string;\n+}\n+const board: { id: string; plan: PlanEvent[] }[] = JSON.parse(\n+  readFileSync(join(SHOWREEL, \"sections.json\"), \"utf8\"),\n+);\n+const event = (id: string, name: string): PlanEvent => {\n+  const e = board.find((s) => s.id === id)?.plan.find((p) => p.name === name);\n+  if (!e) throw new Error(`${id} has no event ${name}`);\n+  return e;\n+};\n+\n+for (const edition of EDITIONS) {\n+  test(`${edition}: contiguous cuts, each a span of one section at its source pace`, () => {\n     const list = cuts(edition);\n     let end = 0;\n     for (const cut of list) {\n@@ -24,20 +63,27 @@ for (const edition of [\"tour\", \"overview\"] satisfies Edition[]) {\n       const source = sec(cut.id);\n       assert.ok(cut.from >= source.start && cut.to <= source.end);\n       assert.ok(cut.to > cut.from);\n-      if (!cut.brand) {\n-        assert.equal(cut.from, source.start);\n-        assert.equal(\n-          cut.to,\n-          source.end,\n-          \"retained scenes keep their complete reading holds\",\n-        );\n-      }\n       assert.equal(cutAt(edition, cut.start).id, cut.id);\n       assert.equal(cutAt(edition, cut.end - 1 / 120).id, cut.id);\n       end = cut.end;\n     }\n     assert.equal(end, filmDuration(edition));\n-    assert.equal(cutAt(edition, end).brand, \"outro\");\n+    assert.equal(cutAt(edition, end).id, \"end\");\n+    // Whole sections but for the three the films cut inside.\n+    for (const cut of list) {\n+      const s = sec(cut.id);\n+      if (cut.id === \"open\" || cut.id === \"switch\")\n+        assert.equal(cut.from, s.start);\n+      else if (cut.id === \"end\") assert.equal(cut.from, s.start);\n+      else {\n+        assert.equal(cut.from, s.start);\n+        assert.equal(\n+          cut.to,\n+          s.end,\n+          `${cut.id} keeps its complete reading hold`,\n+        );\n+      }\n+    }\n   });\n   test(`${edition}: chapters cover the delivered film, including reordered scenes`, () => {\n     let end = 0;\n@@ -53,36 +99,140 @@ for (const edition of [\"tour\", \"overview\"] satisfies Edition[]) {\n       chapters.length + 1,\n     );\n     assert.equal(new Set(chapters.map((c) => c.id)).size, chapters.length);\n+    assert.equal(chapters[0].label, \"mise\");\n+    assert.equal(chapters.at(-1)!.label, \"Install mise\");\n+  });\n+  test(`${edition}: a ticket after a cut inherits the stage before it; other cuts enter and leave`, () => {\n+    const list = cuts(edition);\n+    const joins = filmJoins(edition);\n+    let acts = 0;\n+    list.forEach((cut, k) => {\n+      const prev = list[k - 1];\n+      const s = sec(cut.id);\n+      if (s.ticket) {\n+        assert.equal(\n+          joins[cut.id]?.meta,\n+          `ACT ${[\"I\", \"II\", \"III\", \"IV\", \"V\", \"VI\", \"VII\"][acts++]}`,\n+        );\n+      } else assert.equal(joins[cut.id], undefined);\n+      const jump = prev && !continuous(prev, cut);\n+      if (s.ticket && jump) {\n+        // The bar line the film's predecessor ends on, which its last frame holds.\n+        assert.equal(joins[cut.id]?.in, handoffOut(prev.id)?.id);\n+        assert.equal(prev.to, sec(prev.id).end);\n+      } else assert.equal(joins[cut.id]?.in, undefined);\n+      const first = framePresence(list, k, cut.start, joins);\n+      const mid = framePresence(list, k, (cut.start + cut.end) / 2, joins);\n+      const last = framePresence(list, k, cut.end - 1 / 120, joins);\n+      assert.deepEqual(\n+        mid,\n+        { alpha: 1, dy: 0 },\n+        `${cut.id} stands on its mark`,\n+      );\n+      if (jump && !joins[cut.id]?.in) {\n+        assert.equal(first.alpha, 0, `${cut.id} enters`);\n+        assert.ok(first.dy > 0);\n+        const landed = framePresence(\n+          list,\n+          k,\n+          cut.start + DUR.enter * BEAT,\n+          joins,\n+        );\n+        assert.deepEqual(landed, { alpha: 1, dy: 0 });\n+      } else\n+        assert.deepEqual(\n+          first,\n+          { alpha: 1, dy: 0 },\n+          `${cut.id} arrives at rest`,\n+        );\n+      const next = list[k + 1];\n+      if (next && !continuous(cut, next) && !joins[next.id]?.in) {\n+        assert.ok(last.alpha < 0.1, `${cut.id} leaves`);\n+        assert.ok(last.dy > 0);\n+        const still = framePresence(list, k, cut.end - DUR.exit * BEAT, joins);\n+        assert.deepEqual(still, { alpha: 1, dy: 0 });\n+      } else\n+        assert.deepEqual(last, { alpha: 1, dy: 0 }, `${cut.id} ends at rest`);\n+    });\n   });\n }\n \n-test(\"the tour is under five minutes and the overview is 60–90 seconds\", () => {\n+test(\"the partial shots end where the storyboard hands over to a scene the films cut\", () => {\n+  // The open: once its name card and chef have left, before the pitch's terminal rises.\n+  assert.ok(OPEN_TO >= event(\"open\", \"leave\").end);\n+  assert.ok(OPEN_TO <= event(\"open\", \"pitchTerm\").at);\n+  // The switch: on the fold into packslip's card, after its threads have drawn.\n+  assert.equal(SWITCH_TO, event(\"switch\", \"fold\").at);\n+  assert.ok(SWITCH_TO >= event(\"switch\", \"threads\").end + DUR.half);\n+  // The end card: its last move over (the glint and its sparkle), and a hold.\n+  assert.ok(END_TO >= END_CUES.glint + GLINT.dur + GLINT.sparkle + 1);\n+  assert.ok(END_TO <= sec(\"end\").len);\n+});\n+\n+test(\"the tour is under five minutes and the overview is 60–90 seconds, project switching first\", () => {\n   assert.ok(filmDuration(\"tour\") < 300);\n   assert.ok(filmDuration(\"overview\") >= 60 && filmDuration(\"overview\") <= 90);\n+  assert.equal(cuts(\"tour\")[0].id, \"open\");\n   assert.equal(\n     cuts(\"tour\")[1].id,\n     \"switch\",\n     \"project switching is the first demonstration\",\n   );\n+  assert.deepEqual(\n+    cuts(\"tour\")\n+      .slice(-2)\n+      .map((c) => c.id),\n+    [\"morph\", \"end\"],\n+  );\n+  assert.equal(cuts(\"overview\").at(-1)!.id, \"end\");\n });\n \n-test(\"callouts use the current recording's output, including both project versions\", () => {\n-  const { facts: data } = loadFacts(REPO);\n-  assert.ok(data);\n-  const facts = data as unknown as ReelFacts;\n-  const callouts = new Set<string>();\n-  for (let t = 0; t < sec(\"switch\").len; t += 1 / 30) {\n-    const value = commandCallout(\"switch\", t, facts);\n-    if (value) callouts.add(value);\n+/** Every string a reel draws on one frame, through the kit's ink tap. */\n+function inked(reel: Reel, t: number): string {\n+  const g = globalThis as { document?: unknown };\n+  const had = g.document;\n+  g.document = {\n+    createElement: () => ({\n+      width: 0,\n+      height: 0,\n+      getContext: () => recordingContext(() => {}),\n+    }),\n+  };\n+  const out: string[] = [];\n+  const prev = setInkSink((text) => out.push(text));\n+  try {\n+    reel.render(\n+      recordingContext((text) => out.push(text)),\n+      t,\n+      1920,\n+      1080,\n+    );\n+  } finally {\n+    setInkSink(prev);\n+    g.document = had;\n   }\n-  assert.ok(\n-    [...callouts].some((s) =>\n-      s.endsWith(`v${data.versions.node.other_version}`),\n-    ),\n-  );\n-  assert.ok(\n-    [...callouts].some((s) => s.endsWith(`v${data.versions.node.lts_version}`)),\n+  return out.join(\"\\n\");\n+}\n+\n+test(\"the Environments ticket takes down the registry's card, not packslip's stage\", (t) => {\n+  const { facts } = loadFacts(REPO);\n+  if (!facts) return t.skip(\"needs the capture set\");\n+  const f = facts as unknown as ReelFacts;\n+  const env = cuts(\"tour\").find((c) => c.id === \"env\")!;\n+  const registry = cuts(\"tour\").find((c) => c.id === \"registry\")!;\n+  assert.equal(registry.end, env.start);\n+  // Just into the ticket: the inherited stage is still up, leaving.\n+  const film = inked(createFilm(f, \"tour\"), env.start + 0.1);\n+  assert.match(film, /jq = \"latest\"/, \"the registry's card is on the frame\");\n+  assert.doesNotMatch(film, /hk = \"/, \"packslip's card is not\");\n+  assert.doesNotMatch(\n+    film,\n+    /linked ~\\/work\\/api/,\n+    \"packslip's terminal is not\",\n   );\n-  assert.equal(commandCallout(\"switch\", 0, facts), null);\n-  assert.equal(commandCallout(\"switch\", 10, null), null);\n+  assert.match(film, /ACT II/, \"the ticket is numbered in film order\");\n+  // The source reel's own env ticket still takes packslip's stage down.\n+  const source = inked(createReel(f), sec(\"env\").start + 0.1);\n+  assert.match(source, /hk = \"/);\n+  assert.match(source, /ACT III/);\n });"
    },

      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2F.vitepress%2Ftheme%2Fshowreel%2Ftest%2Ffilm-music.test.ts",

      "patch": "@@ -1,3 +1,8 @@\n+// The films' music (film-music.ts): the song cut on its own bar lines,\n+// covering the film from its first frame, ending with its complete\n+// ring-out a few frames before the film does, its outro under the end\n+// card, and never restarted by a picture cut.\n+\n import assert from \"node:assert/strict\";\n import { createHash } from \"node:crypto\";\n import { readFileSync } from \"node:fs\";\n@@ -6,59 +11,129 @@ import { playFilmMix } from \"../audio\";\n import { MockContext } from \"./mock-audio\";\n import { REPO } from \"./repo\";\n import { test } from \"node:test\";\n-import { filmDuration, type Edition } from \"../edit\";\n-import { MUSIC, musicChannel, musicCuts } from \"../film-music\";\n+import { cuts, filmDuration, type Edition } from \"../edit\";\n+import {\n+  BAR,\n+  filmChordAt,\n+  MUSIC,\n+  musicChannel,\n+  musicCuts,\n+  musicEnd,\n+  SONG_CHART,\n+  songChordAt,\n+  SONG_END,\n+} from \"../film-music\";\n+import { reelChord } from \"../film-audio\";\n+import { CH } from \"../score/harmony\";\n+import { sec } from \"../timeline\";\n+\n+/** The song's form (score/BED.md), bars. */\n+const FORM = { groove: 16, windDown: 168, outro: 172 } as const;\n \n for (const edition of [\"tour\", \"overview\"] as const) {\n-  test(`${edition} music has continuous coverage and retains the song's entire ending`, () => {\n-    const cuts = musicCuts(edition);\n+  test(`${edition} music covers the film from its first frame and keeps the song's whole ending`, () => {\n+    const list = musicCuts(edition);\n     let end = 0;\n-    for (const cut of cuts) {\n+    for (const cut of list) {\n       assert.equal(cut.start, end);\n-      assert.equal(cut.from % 2, 0, \"join must be on a downbeat\");\n+      assert.equal(cut.from % BAR, 0, \"a join lands on a downbeat\");\n       assert.ok(cut.from >= 0 && cut.to <= MUSIC.duration);\n+      assert.ok(cut.to > cut.from);\n       end = cut.end;\n     }\n-    assert.equal(cuts.at(-1)!.to, MUSIC.duration);\n-    assert.ok(Math.abs(filmDuration(edition) - end - 0.385) < 1e-8);\n+    assert.equal(list.at(-1)!.to, MUSIC.duration);\n+    assert.equal(musicEnd(edition), end);\n+    // The ring-out dies out before the film ends, within a second of it.\n+    const film = filmDuration(edition);\n+    assert.ok(\n+      end < film && end > film - 1,\n+      `${edition}: music ends ${end} of ${film}`,\n+    );\n+    // The first groove drops once the open's card has left and the first\n+    // demonstration begins (edit.ts OPEN_TO), not under the open itself.\n+    const first = list[0];\n+    const drop = first.start + FORM.groove * BAR - first.from;\n+    const open = cuts(edition)[0];\n+    assert.ok(\n+      drop >= open.end - 1 && drop <= open.end + 2,\n+      `the groove drops at ${drop}`,\n+    );\n+    // The song's outro starts under the end card, or just before it.\n+    const last = list.at(-1)!;\n+    const outro = last.start + FORM.outro * BAR - last.from;\n+    const endCard = cuts(edition).at(-1)!;\n+    assert.ok(\n+      outro <= endCard.start && outro >= endCard.start - 8,\n+      `the outro starts at ${outro}`,\n+    );\n+    assert.ok(last.from <= FORM.windDown * BAR, \"the wind-down plays whole\");\n   });\n-}\n \n-test(\"picture cuts and the old breath never stop or restart tour music\", () => {\n-  const rate = 1000;\n-  const source = Float32Array.from(\n-    { length: Math.round(MUSIC.duration * rate) },\n-    (_, i) => i / rate,\n-  );\n-  const tour = musicChannel(source, \"tour\", rate);\n-  for (const time of [6, 27, 28, 90, 112, 150, 210, 214, 238, 240, 276]) {\n-    const frame = time * rate;\n-    // A monotonic source identifies a continuous playhead, independent of\n-    // the visual edit and original source reel's music fades.\n-    assert.ok(\n-      Math.abs(tour[frame + 1] - tour[frame - 1] - 2 / rate) < 0.00004,\n-      `${time}s interrupts music`,\n+  test(`${edition}: picture cuts never stop or restart the music`, () => {\n+    const rate = 1000;\n+    const source = Float32Array.from(\n+      { length: Math.round(MUSIC.duration * rate) },\n+      (_, i) => i / rate,\n     );\n-  }\n-  assert.ok(\n-    tour[244 * rate] > tour[244 * rate - 151],\n-    \"4:04 starts the final chorus instead of a quiet breath\",\n-  );\n-});\n+    const track = musicChannel(source, edition, rate);\n+    const joins = new Set(\n+      musicCuts(edition).map((c) => Math.round(c.start * rate)),\n+    );\n+    for (const cut of cuts(edition).slice(1)) {\n+      const frame = Math.round(cut.start * rate);\n+      if (joins.has(frame)) continue;\n+      // A monotonic source identifies a continuous playhead across the picture's cut.\n+      assert.ok(\n+        Math.abs(track[frame + 1] - track[frame - 1] - 2 / rate) < 0.00004,\n+        `${cut.id} at ${cut.start}s interrupts the music`,\n+      );\n+    }\n+  });\n+}\n \n-test(\"both music arrangements preserve every last sample of the natural ring-out\", () => {\n+test(\"both arrangements preserve every last sample of the natural ring-out\", () => {\n   const rate = 1000;\n   const source = new Float32Array(Math.round(MUSIC.duration * rate)).fill(0.25);\n   source[source.length - 1] = 0.125;\n   for (const edition of [\"tour\", \"overview\"] as Edition[]) {\n     const track = musicChannel(source, edition, rate);\n-    const end = Math.round(musicCuts(edition).at(-1)!.end * rate);\n+    const end = Math.round(musicEnd(edition) * rate);\n     assert.equal(track[end - 1], 0.125);\n     assert.equal(track[end], 0);\n     assert.equal(track.at(-1), 0);\n   }\n });\n \n+test(\"the song's length is a whole number of its bars plus the ring-out\", () => {\n+  assert.ok(SONG_END > 179 && SONG_END < 180);\n+});\n+\n+test(\"the song chart is in order and the films' pitched effects read the chord under them\", () => {\n+  for (let i = 1; i < SONG_CHART.length; i++)\n+    assert.ok(SONG_CHART[i][0] > SONG_CHART[i - 1][0]);\n+  assert.equal(SONG_CHART.at(-1)![0], 176);\n+  // The first groove's vamp: F minor, C minor, B♭, F minor.\n+  assert.equal(songChordAt(16 * BAR), CH.Fm);\n+  assert.equal(songChordAt(21 * BAR), CH.Cm);\n+  assert.equal(songChordAt(25 * BAR), CH.Bb);\n+  assert.equal(songChordAt(30 * BAR), CH.Fm);\n+  // Through 112–135 the F leaves its third out, as the source's parts do.\n+  assert.equal(songChordAt(113 * BAR), CH.F5);\n+  // The film clock: the tour opens in the intro's tail and plays the song straight through its first cut.\n+  const first = musicCuts(\"tour\")[0];\n+  assert.equal(filmChordAt(\"tour\", 0), songChordAt(first.from));\n+  assert.equal(filmChordAt(\"tour\", 12), songChordAt(first.from + 12));\n+  assert.equal(filmChordAt(\"tour\", musicEnd(\"tour\") + 0.1), null);\n+  // A reel moment maps through the picture's cut to the film's: the switch\n+  // two seconds in plays at film 12.5 s in both editions.\n+  const t = sec(\"switch\").start + 2;\n+  for (const edition of [\"tour\", \"overview\"] as const)\n+    assert.equal(reelChord(edition, t), filmChordAt(edition, 12.5));\n+  // A section the film does not play has no chord of the film's.\n+  assert.equal(reelChord(\"tour\", sec(\"packslip\").start + 1), null);\n+  assert.equal(reelChord(\"overview\", sec(\"track\").start + 1), null);\n+});\n+\n test(\"the renderer's original-song pin matches the committed bytes\", () => {\n   const bytes = readFileSync(join(REPO, MUSIC.file));\n   assert.equal(createHash(\"sha256\").update(bytes).digest(\"hex\"), MUSIC.sha256);"
    },
