      "sha": "52b5de2e8a348ae0eaf008caf8629a19123d512b",
      "filename": "docs/contributing.md",
      "status": "modified",
      "additions": 5,
      "deletions": 3,
      "changes": 8,
      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2Fcontributing.md",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2Fcontributing.md",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/docs%2Fcontributing.md?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -178,9 +178,11 @@ eval \"$(@mise activate zsh)\"\n \n ### hk Configuration\n \n-[`hk.pkl`](https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/main/hk.pkl) defines `check` and `fix` workflows.\n-It currently has no Git `pre-commit` hook, so `hk install --mise` may report that there is\n-nothing to install. Run the checks explicitly before committing.\n+[`hk.pkl`](https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/main/hk.pkl) defines `check` and `fix` workflows\n+and a Git `pre-commit` hook. `hk install --mise` installs the hook, which runs the same linters\n+as `fix` and applies fixes before each commit (unstaged changes are stashed while it runs).\n+You can also run the checks explicitly with `mise run lint` or `mise run lint-fix`. On Windows,\n+`lint-fix` runs only Clippy, Prettier, and `cargo fmt`, not the other hk steps.\n \n ### Available Linters in hk\n "
    },
    {
      "sha": "f4a5660d0705aef1cca4d4fab79ac6164712ba6e",
      "filename": "docs/dev-tools/backends/index.md",
      "status": "modified",
      "additions": 10,
      "deletions": 10,
      "changes": 20,
      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2Fdev-tools%2Fbackends%2Findex.md",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2Fdev-tools%2Fbackends%2Findex.md",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/docs%2Fdev-tools%2Fbackends%2Findex.md?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -23,16 +23,16 @@ mise exec -- rg --version\n Add `-g` for your global configuration. You can use an explicit backend even\n when a tool has no registry shorthand; a registry submission is not required.\n \n-| Source                      | Backends                                                                                                                                                      | What to check                                                                            |\n-| --------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n-| Signed publisher manifests  | [Packslip](./packslip.html)                                                                                                                                   | The publisher must provide Packslip releases and a verifiable signer.                    |\n-| Curated binary recipes      | [Aqua](./aqua.html)                                                                                                                                           | The aqua registry must have an entry for the tool and platform. No aqua CLI is required. |\n-| Release assets              | [GitHub](./github.html), [GitLab](./gitlab.html), [Forgejo](./forgejo.html)                                                                                   | Releases must include an installable asset for your platform.                            |\n-| Direct downloads            | [HTTP](./http.html), [S3](./s3.html)                                                                                                                          | Supply download URLs and, for version discovery, a version source.                       |\n-| Language packages           | [Cargo](./cargo.html), [Go](./go.html), [npm](./npm.html), [pipx](./pipx.html), [gem](./gem.html), [.NET](./dotnet.html), [Swift Package Manager](./spm.html) | Read the backend's runtime and build prerequisites.                                      |\n-| Binary package ecosystems   | [Conda](./conda.html)                                                                                                                                         | Packages and their runtime dependencies must support your platform.                      |\n-| Plugin-defined installation | [vfox](./vfox.html), [asdf](./asdf.html) (legacy)                                                                                                             | Review the plugin and its dependencies before installation.                              |\n-| Legacy release installer    | [ubi](./ubi.html) (deprecated)                                                                                                                                | Migrate existing configurations to the appropriate release backend.                      |\n+| Source                      | Backends                                                                                                                                                                               | What to check                                                                            |\n+| --------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n+| Signed publisher manifests  | [Packslip](./packslip.html)                                                                                                                                                            | The publisher must provide Packslip releases and a verifiable signer.                    |\n+| Curated binary recipes      | [Aqua](./aqua.html)                                                                                                                                                                    | The aqua registry must have an entry for the tool and platform. No aqua CLI is required. |\n+| Release assets              | [GitHub](./github.html), [GitLab](./gitlab.html), [Forgejo](./forgejo.html)                                                                                                            | Releases must include an installable asset for your platform.                            |\n+| Direct downloads            | [HTTP](./http.html), [S3](./s3.html)                                                                                                                                                   | Supply download URLs and, for version discovery, a version source.                       |\n+| Language packages           | [Cargo](./cargo.html), [Go](./go.html), [npm](./npm.html), [pipx](./pipx.html), [gem](./gem.html), [.NET](./dotnet.html), [Swift Package Manager](./spm.html), [Spinel](./spinel.html) | Read the backend's runtime and build prerequisites.                                      |\n+| Binary package ecosystems   | [Conda](./conda.html)                                                                                                                                                                  | Packages and their runtime dependencies must support your platform.                      |\n+| Plugin-defined installation | [vfox](./vfox.html), [asdf](./asdf.html) (legacy)                                                                                                                                      | Review the plugin and its dependencies before installation.                              |\n+| Legacy release installer    | [ubi](./ubi.html) (deprecated)                                                                                                                                                         | Migrate existing configurations to the appropriate release backend.                      |\n \n Built-in language support is documented in the [language guides](/lang/node.html).\n Plugin authors can also create [custom backends](/backend-plugin-development.html)"
    },
    {
      "sha": "24cc62d835465b31b5129dee4185908e207ff790",
      "filename": "docs/dev-tools/backends/spinel.md",
      "status": "added",
      "additions": 76,
      "deletions": 0,
      "changes": 76,
      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2Fdev-tools%2Fbackends%2Fspinel.md",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2Fdev-tools%2Fbackends%2Fspinel.md",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/docs%2Fdev-tools%2Fbackends%2Fspinel.md?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -0,0 +1,76 @@\n+---\n+description: \"Compile Ruby command-line tools from GitHub source with the experimental Spinel backend.\"\n+---\n+\n+# Spinel Backend <Badge type=\"warning\" text=\"experimental\" />\n+\n+The `spinel:` backend compiles a Ruby command-line tool from a GitHub repository into a native\n+executable with [Spinel](https://github.com/matz/spinel), Matz's Ruby AOT compiler. The result needs\n+neither Ruby nor Spinel to run.\n+\n+::: warning\n+This backend is experimental, and it may not stay. Spinel is young, and the backend is only worth\n+keeping while Spinel takes off and the backend stays cheap to maintain. If either stops being true,\n+it will be removed. Enable it with `mise settings experimental=true`.\n+:::\n+\n+## Requirements\n+\n+- macOS or Linux (Windows is not supported).\n+- `git`.\n+- The `spinel` compiler on `PATH`, or its path in the `spinel` tool option. Spinel ships as source:\n+  see its [Quick Start](https://github.com/matz/spinel#quick-start) to build it.\n+- A C compiler, which Spinel invokes to produce the executable.\n+\n+Spinel supports only part of the Ruby language, so many Ruby tools will not compile. The backend\n+compiles a single entrypoint file. It does not install gems, copy data files, or fetch submodules.\n+\n+## Usage\n+\n+```sh\n+mise settings experimental=true\n+```\n+\n+Most tools need options, such as the entrypoint, so declare them in `mise.toml` and then install:\n+\n+```toml\n+[tools.\"spinel:tobi/try\"]\n+version = \"1.10.1\"\n+entrypoint = \"try.rb\"\n+bin = \"try\"\n+tag_prefix = \"v\"\n+```\n+\n+```sh\n+mise install\n+```\n+\n+`mise ls-remote spinel:tobi/try` lists the repository's git tags (via `git ls-remote`, not the GitHub\n+API). With `tag_prefix = \"v\"`, the tag `v1.10.1` is the version `1.10.1`.\n+\n+## Tool Options\n+\n+The following [tool-options](/dev-tools/#tool-options) are available for the `spinel` backend—these\n+go in `[tools]` in `mise.toml`.\n+\n+### `entrypoint`\n+\n+The Ruby file to compile, relative to the repository root. Defaults to `main.rb`.\n+\n+### `bin`\n+\n+The name of the executable written to `bin/`. Defaults to the repository name.\n+\n+### `tag_prefix`\n+\n+Text before the version in each git tag, such as `v`. Only tags with the prefix are listed.\n+\n+### `source_ref`\n+\n+A full 40-character commit SHA to build instead of the tag. The version is then only a label.\n+\n+### `spinel`\n+\n+The compiler to run. Defaults to `spinel` on `PATH`.\n+\n+Implementation: [`src/backend/spinel.rs`](https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/main/src/backend/spinel.rs)."
    },
    {
      "sha": "f00a3a08998d8a7cc5ea412383756957ce955a9e",
      "filename": "docs/public/llms.txt",
      "status": "modified",
      "additions": 1,
      "deletions": 0,
      "changes": 1,
      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2Fpublic%2Fllms.txt",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/docs%2Fpublic%2Fllms.txt",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/docs%2Fpublic%2Fllms.txt?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -63,6 +63,7 @@\n - [npm](https://mise.jdx.dev/dev-tools/backends/npm.html): The npm backend installs command-line packages from npm registries into separate tool directories. Keep your application's dependencies in package.json and use its package manager or mise deps to…\n - [packslip](https://mise.jdx.dev/dev-tools/backends/packslip.html): The Packslip backend installs tools using signed release manifests published by their maintainers. mise verifies the release, selects the build for your platform, and installs its executables.\n - [pypi](https://mise.jdx.dev/dev-tools/backends/pypi.html): The pypi backend installs Python command-line applications in isolated virtual environments. Each tool gets its own dependencies.\n+- [spinel](https://mise.jdx.dev/dev-tools/backends/spinel.html): The spinel: backend compiles a Ruby command-line tool from a GitHub repository into a native executable with Spinel, Matz's Ruby AOT compiler. The result needs neither Ruby nor Spinel to run.\n - [spm](https://mise.jdx.dev/dev-tools/backends/spm.html): You may install executables managed by Swift Package Manager directly from GitHub or GitLab releases.\n - [ubi](https://mise.jdx.dev/dev-tools/backends/ubi.html): The ubi backend is deprecated. Use the GitHub backend instead.\n - [vfox](https://mise.jdx.dev/dev-tools/backends/vfox.html): vfox is the recommended plugin system for mise. It provides cross-platform support, built-in modules, and a modern hook-based architecture."
    },

      "patch": "@@ -1,37 +1,37 @@\n WEBVTT\n \n mise\n-00:00:00.000 --> 00:00:06.000\n-Overview\n+00:00:00.000 --> 00:00:10.750\n+mise\n \n versions\n-00:00:06.000 --> 00:00:56.000\n+00:00:10.750 --> 00:00:58.250\n Project switching\n \n dev-tools\n-00:00:56.000 --> 00:01:30.000\n+00:00:58.250 --> 00:01:32.250\n Dev tools\n \n environments\n-00:01:30.000 --> 00:01:52.000\n+00:01:32.250 --> 00:01:54.250\n Environments\n \n tasks\n-00:01:52.000 --> 00:02:30.000\n+00:01:54.250 --> 00:02:32.250\n Tasks\n \n dotfiles\n-00:02:30.000 --> 00:03:10.000\n+00:02:32.250 --> 00:03:12.250\n Dotfiles\n \n everywhere\n-00:03:10.000 --> 00:03:34.000\n-In CI\n+00:03:12.250 --> 00:03:36.250\n+Everywhere\n \n new-machine\n-00:03:34.000 --> 00:04:36.000\n+00:03:36.250 --> 00:04:38.250\n A new machine\n \n install\n-00:04:36.000 --> 00:04:44.000\n+00:04:38.250 --> 00:04:58.250\n Install mise"

    {
      "sha": "9ae877dd6b932a026b10fe88badf40d1fc3c7f9f",
      "filename": "e2e-win/exec_inner_quotes.Tests.ps1",
      "status": "modified",
      "additions": 25,
      "deletions": 0,
      "changes": 25,
      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/e2e-win%2Fexec_inner_quotes.Tests.ps1",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/e2e-win%2Fexec_inner_quotes.Tests.ps1",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/e2e-win%2Fexec_inner_quotes.Tests.ps1?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -28,6 +28,31 @@ Describe 'exec / exec() inner-quote preservation (cmd /c)' {\n         $output | Should -Be '\"hello world\"'\n     }\n \n+    It 'preserves inner double quotes in a positional mise exec -- cmd /c command' {\n+        # pitchfork runs a `mise = true` daemon as `mise x -- cmd /C <run>`, with the\n+        # whole command line as the one argument after /C.\n+        $output = mise exec -- cmd /c 'echo \"hello world\"' | Select-Object -Last 1\n+        $output | Should -Not -Match '\\\\'\n+        $output | Should -Be '\"hello world\"'\n+    }\n+\n+    It 'still runs a positional cmd /c command without quotes' {\n+        $output = mise exec -- cmd /c 'echo hello world' | Select-Object -Last 1\n+        $output | Should -Be 'hello world'\n+    }\n+\n+    It 'launches a quoted program path with a space through a positional cmd /c' {\n+        # The case pitchfork hits: a `run` that quotes a program path and an argument.\n+        $dir = Join-Path $TestDrive 'quoted program'\n+        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dir | Out-Null\n+        $program = Join-Path $dir 'probe.cmd'\n+        '@echo launched [%~1]' | Set-Content -Path $program -Encoding ascii\n+\n+        $output = mise exec -- cmd /c \"`\"$program`\" `\"a b`\"\" | Select-Object -Last 1\n+        $LASTEXITCODE | Should -Be 0\n+        $output | Should -Be 'launched [a b]'\n+    }\n+\n     It 'passes an inner-quoted -c argument through to a program (mise exec -c)' {\n         # Without the fix the quoted script is split at the first space and node\n         # fails with a syntax error instead of printing the result."
    },
    {
      "sha": "6b992b64a2342e78b7bc9b34157a6dc4fa0d7411",
      "filename": "e2e/cli/test_hook_env_untrusted_keeps_global",
      "status": "added",
      "additions": 37,
      "deletions": 0,
      "changes": 37,
      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/e2e%2Fcli%2Ftest_hook_env_untrusted_keeps_global",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/e2e%2Fcli%2Ftest_hook_env_untrusted_keeps_global",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/e2e%2Fcli%2Ftest_hook_env_untrusted_keeps_global?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -0,0 +1,37 @@\n+#!/usr/bin/env bash\n+set -euo pipefail\n+\n+# An untrusted project config must not stop hook-env from applying the\n+# trusted global config (https://github.com/jdx/mise/issues/13918).\n+export MISE_TRUSTED_CONFIG_PATHS=\"$HOME/.config/mise\"\n+unset CI GITHUB_ACTIONS GITHUB_ACTION\n+\n+mkdir -p \"$HOME/.config/mise\"\n+cat >\"$HOME/.config/mise/config.toml\" <<'TOML'\n+[env]\n+GLOBAL_FOO = \"global\"\n+TOML\n+\n+mkdir -p project\n+cat >project/mise.toml <<'TOML'\n+[env]\n+LOCAL_FOO = \"local\"\n+TOML\n+cd project\n+\n+stdout=\"$(mise hook-env -s bash 2>stderr.txt)\"\n+assert_contains \"cat stderr.txt\" \"not trusted\"\n+assert_contains \"echo '$stdout'\" \"GLOBAL_FOO\"\n+assert_not_contains \"echo '$stdout'\" \"LOCAL_FOO\"\n+eval \"$stdout\"\n+assert \"printenv GLOBAL_FOO\" \"global\"\n+\n+# A second untrusted config in the same directory must warn again even though\n+# the first one was already reported and is unchanged.\n+cat >mise.local.toml <<'TOML'\n+[env]\n+LOCAL_BAR = \"local\"\n+TOML\n+eval \"$stdout\"\n+mise hook-env -s bash >/dev/null 2>stderr2.txt\n+assert_contains \"cat stderr2.txt\" \"mise.local.toml is not trusted\""
    },
    {
      "sha": "aea0b84371af2d7a23c6d0e5f22baf1e8bbd7fce",
      "filename": "e2e/config/test_local_settings_trust_controls",
      "status": "modified",
      "additions": 4,
      "deletions": 2,
      "changes": 6,
      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/e2e%2Fconfig%2Ftest_local_settings_trust_controls",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/e2e%2Fconfig%2Ftest_local_settings_trust_controls",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/e2e%2Fconfig%2Ftest_local_settings_trust_controls?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -24,8 +24,10 @@ output=$(MISE_YES=0 MISE_PARANOID=1 mise hook-env -s bash --force 2>&1)\n status=$?\n set -e\n \n-if [[ $status -eq 0 ]]; then\n-  echo \"FAIL: expected hook-env to reject untrusted local config\"\n+# hook-env skips the untrusted config with a warning instead of failing, so the\n+# trusted configs still apply.\n+if [[ $status -ne 0 ]]; then\n+  echo \"FAIL: expected hook-env to skip untrusted local config, got status $status\"\n   echo \"Output: $output\"\n   exit 1\n fi"
    },
    {
      "sha": "9ac566d1c6d4b9e4ceab9fc6b72f19e07774bc48",
      "filename": "e2e/config/test_no_config_skips_miserc",
      "status": "added",
      "additions": 27,
      "deletions": 0,
      "changes": 27,
      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/e2e%2Fconfig%2Ftest_no_config_skips_miserc",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/e2e%2Fconfig%2Ftest_no_config_skips_miserc",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/e2e%2Fconfig%2Ftest_no_config_skips_miserc?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -0,0 +1,27 @@\n+#!/usr/bin/env bash\n+\n+# --no-config must skip miserc discovery entirely: a malformed .miserc.toml\n+# anywhere in the chain must not fail config-free invocations.\n+\n+printf '[invalid' >.miserc.toml\n+printf '[invalid' >\"$MISE_CONFIG_DIR/miserc.toml\"\n+export MISE_SYSTEM_CONFIG_DIR=\"$HOME/system-mise\"\n+mkdir -p \"$MISE_SYSTEM_CONFIG_DIR\"\n+printf '[invalid' >\"$MISE_SYSTEM_CONFIG_DIR/miserc.toml\"\n+\n+# Without --no-config, the malformed miserc chain still fails (control).\n+assert_fail \"mise version\" \"Invalid TOML in config file\"\n+\n+# Env var and flag both succeed, from the project root ...\n+MISE_NO_CONFIG=1 assert_succeed \"mise version\"\n+assert_succeed \"mise --no-config version\"\n+MISE_NO_CONFIG=1 assert \"mise exec -- echo hello\" \"hello\"\n+assert \"mise --no-config exec -- echo hello\" \"hello\"\n+\n+# ... and from a nested directory (project miserc is discovered upward).\n+mkdir -p nested\n+cd nested || exit 1\n+MISE_NO_CONFIG=1 assert_succeed \"mise version\"\n+assert_succeed \"mise --no-config version\"\n+MISE_NO_CONFIG=1 assert \"mise exec -- echo hello\" \"hello\"\n+assert \"mise --no-config exec -- echo hello\" \"hello\""
    },
    {
      "sha": "e8b6e2d3c5010dbddbd008feda127a10d1e67e39",
      "filename": "e2e/config/test_schema_ajv",
      "status": "modified",
      "additions": 58,
      "deletions": 0,
      "changes": 58,
      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/e2e%2Fconfig%2Ftest_schema_ajv",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/e2e%2Fconfig%2Ftest_schema_ajv",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/e2e%2Fconfig%2Ftest_schema_ajv?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -146,3 +146,61 @@ printf '{\"bootstrap\":{\"services\":{\"worker\":{\"builtin\":\"history-watch\",\"command\":\n assert_fail \"$ajv_validate services-invalid.json\"\n printf '{\"bootstrap\":{\"services\":{\"worker\":{\"builtin\":\"history-watch\"}}}}\\n' >services-valid.json\n assert_succeed \"$ajv_validate services-valid.json\"\n+\n+# --- backend-specific tool options (typed only for tools of that backend)\n+for tools in \\\n+  '\"spinel:tobi/try\":\"1.10.1\"' \\\n+  '\"spinel:tobi/try\":{\"version\":\"1.10.1\",\"entrypoint\":\"try.rb\",\"bin\":\"try\",\"tag_prefix\":\"v\",\"spinel\":\"/opt/spinel\"}' \\\n+  '\"spinel:tobi/try\":[{\"version\":\"1.10.1\",\"entrypoint\":\"try.rb\",\"source_ref\":\"be566829856ca6bd22963e38a28ffb5efa24c7de\"}]' \\\n+  '\"cargo:foo\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"entrypoint\":5}' \\\n+  '\"github:cli/cli\":{\"version\":\"2\",\"asset_pattern\":\"gh_*\",\"no_app\":true,\"strip_components\":1,\"rename_exe\":{\"gh-*\":\"gh\"}}' \\\n+  '\"github:cli/cli\":{\"version\":\"2\",\"no_app\":\"true\",\"strip_components\":\"1\"}' \\\n+  '\"github:cli/cli\":{\"version\":\"2\",\"platforms\":{\"linux-x64\":{\"asset_pattern\":\"a\",\"url\":\"https://x/y\"}}}' \\\n+  '\"gitlab:o/r\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"api_url\":\"https://git.example.com/api/v4\"}' \\\n+  '\"http:tool\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"format\":\"tar.gz\",\"platforms\":{\"macos-arm64\":{\"url\":\"https://x\",\"checksum\":\"sha256:ab\"}}}' \\\n+  '\"aqua:o/r\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"libc\":\"musl\",\"vars\":{\"a\":\"b\"}}' \\\n+  '\"cargo:ripgrep\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"features\":[\"a\"],\"locked\":false,\"platforms\":{\"linux-x64\":{\"bin\":\"rg\"}}}' \\\n+  '\"npm:x\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"allow_builds\":[\"a\"],\"allow_exotic_deps\":true}' \\\n+  '\"npm:x\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"allow_low_downloads\":\"TRUE\"}' \\\n+  '\"pipx:x\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"extras\":[\"a\"],\"uvx\":false,\"dependency_prereleases\":\"allow\"}' \\\n+  '\"python\":{\"version\":\"3\",\"patch_sysconfig\":\"false\"}' \\\n+  '\"java\":{\"version\":\"21\",\"release_type\":\"ea\"}' \\\n+  '\"rust\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"profile\":\"minimal\",\"components\":\"a,b\",\"mr_boxington\":true}' \\\n+  '\"dotnet\":{\"version\":\"8\",\"runtime\":\"aspnetcore\"}' \\\n+  '\"dotnet:Some.Tool\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"runtime\":\"anything\"}' \\\n+  '\"node\":{\"version\":\"22\",\"prerelease\":true,\"lazy_bins\":\"node\"}'; do\n+  printf '{\"tools\":{%s}}\\n' \"$tools\" >tool-options-valid.json\n+  assert_succeed \"$ajv_validate tool-options-valid.json\"\n+done\n+for tools in \\\n+  '\"spinel:tobi/try\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"entrypoint\":5}' \\\n+  '\"spinel:tobi/try\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"source_ref\":\"abc\"}' \\\n+  '\"spinel:tobi/try\":[{\"version\":\"1\",\"bin\":false}]' \\\n+  '\"github:cli/cli\":{\"version\":\"2\",\"asset_pattern\":5}' \\\n+  '\"github:cli/cli\":{\"version\":\"2\",\"no_app\":\"maybe\"}' \\\n+  '\"github:cli/cli\":{\"version\":\"2\",\"platforms\":{\"linux-x64\":{\"asset_pattern\":5}}}' \\\n+  '\"github:cli/cli\":{\"version\":\"2\",\"platforms\":{\"linux\":{\"asset_pattern\":\"a\"}}}' \\\n+  '\"http:tool\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"format\":\"rar5\"}' \\\n+  '\"aqua:o/r\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"libc\":\"uclibc\"}' \\\n+  '\"cargo:ripgrep\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"features\":5}' \\\n+  '\"npm:x\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"allow_low_downloads\":\"sure\"}' \\\n+  '\"npm:x\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"allow_low_downloads\":\"1\"}' \\\n+  '\"npm:x\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"allow_low_downloads\":\" true \"}' \\\n+  '\"npm:x\":{\"version\":\"1\",\"allow_low_downloads\":1}' \\\n+  '\"python\":{\"version\":\"3\",\"patch_sysconfig\":\"nah\"}' \\\n+  '\"java\":{\"version\":\"21\",\"release_type\":\"stable\"}' \\\n+  '\"dotnet-core\":{\"version\":\"8\",\"runtime\":\"sdk\"}' \\\n+  '\"node\":{\"version\":\"22\",\"lazy_bins\":[\"a/b\"]}'; do\n+  printf '{\"tools\":{%s}}\\n' \"$tools\" >tool-options-invalid.json\n+  assert_fail \"$ajv_validate tool-options-invalid.json\"\n+done\n+\n+# Task tool tables use the same typed options.\n+printf '{\"tasks\":{\"build\":{\"run\":\"make\",\"tools\":{\"github:cli/cli\":{\"version\":\"2\",\"asset_pattern\":\"a\"}}}}}\\n' >task-tools-valid.json\n+assert_succeed \"$ajv_validate task-tools-valid.json\"\n+printf '{\"tasks\":{\"build\":{\"run\":\"make\",\"tools\":{\"github:cli/cli\":{\"version\":\"2\",\"asset_pattern\":5}}}}}\\n' >task-tools-invalid.json\n+assert_fail \"$ajv_validate task-tools-invalid.json\"\n+\n+# Deprecated root key that mise still accepts with a warning.\n+printf '{\"experimental_monorepo_root\":true}\\n' >legacy-monorepo-root.json\n+assert_succeed \"$ajv_validate legacy-monorepo-root.json\""

      "patch": "@@ -29,6 +29,7 @@ pub enum BackendType {\n     Packslip,\n     #[strum(to_string = \"pypi\", serialize = \"pipx\")]\n     Pipx,\n+    Spinel,\n     Spm,\n     Http,\n     S3,\n@@ -75,6 +76,7 @@ impl BackendType {\n             \"npm\" => BackendType::Npm,\n             \"packslip\" => BackendType::Packslip,\n             \"pypi\" | \"pipx\" => BackendType::Pipx,\n+            \"spinel\" => BackendType::Spinel,\n             \"spm\" => BackendType::Spm,\n             \"http\" => BackendType::Http,\n             \"s3\" => BackendType::S3,\n@@ -86,11 +88,12 @@ impl BackendType {\n \n     /// Returns true if this backend is still gated behind experimental mode.\n     pub fn is_experimental(&self) -> bool {\n-        use super::{dotnet, packslip, s3, spm};\n+        use super::{dotnet, packslip, s3, spinel, spm};\n         match self {\n             BackendType::Dotnet => dotnet::EXPERIMENTAL,\n             BackendType::Packslip => packslip::EXPERIMENTAL,\n             BackendType::S3 => s3::EXPERIMENTAL,\n+            BackendType::Spinel => spinel::EXPERIMENTAL,\n             BackendType::Spm => spm::EXPERIMENTAL,\n             _ => false,\n         }"

      "patch": "@@ -78,6 +78,7 @@ pub mod pipx;\n pub mod platform_target;\n mod platform_tokens;\n pub(crate) mod s3;\n+pub(crate) mod spinel;\n pub(crate) mod spm;\n pub mod static_helpers;\n pub(crate) mod ubi;\n@@ -663,6 +664,7 @@ pub fn arg_to_backend(ba: BackendArg) -> Option<ABackend> {\n         BackendType::Npm => Some(Arc::new(npm::NPMBackend::from_arg(ba))),\n         BackendType::Packslip => Some(Arc::new(packslip::PackslipBackend::from_arg(ba))),\n         BackendType::Pipx => Some(Arc::new(pipx::PIPXBackend::from_arg(ba))),\n+        BackendType::Spinel => Some(Arc::new(spinel::SpinelBackend::from_arg(ba))),\n         BackendType::Spm => Some(Arc::new(spm::SPMBackend::from_arg(ba))),\n         BackendType::Http => Some(Arc::new(http::HttpBackend::from_arg(ba))),\n         BackendType::S3 => Some(Arc::new(s3::S3Backend::from_arg(ba))),\n@@ -695,6 +697,7 @@ pub(crate) fn install_time_option_keys_for_type(backend_type: &BackendType) -> V\n         BackendType::Packslip => packslip::install_time_option_keys(),\n         BackendType::Pipx => pipx::install_time_option_keys(),\n         BackendType::Aqua => aqua::install_time_option_keys(),\n+        BackendType::Spinel => spinel::install_time_option_keys(),\n         BackendType::Spm => spm::install_time_option_keys(),\n         _ => vec![],\n     }"

    {
      "sha": "3150f1905fe98b9d4ca237175e36544455cebea1",
      "filename": "src/backend/spinel.rs",
      "status": "added",
      "additions": 318,
      "deletions": 0,
      "changes": 318,
      "blob_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/blob/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/src%2Fbackend%2Fspinel.rs",
      "raw_url": "https://github.com/jdx/mise/raw/dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6/src%2Fbackend%2Fspinel.rs",
      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/contents/src%2Fbackend%2Fspinel.rs?ref=dfe74a90b41603625ee6aabecb42f14a1f5eb0f6",
      "patch": "@@ -0,0 +1,318 @@\n+use std::path::Path;\n+use std::sync::Arc;\n+\n+use crate::args::BackendArg;\n+use crate::backend::VersionInfo;\n+use crate::backend::backend_type::BackendType;\n+use crate::backend::options::BackendOptions;\n+use crate::cmd::CmdLineRunner;\n+use crate::config::{Settings, SettingsExt};\n+use crate::toolset::ToolVersionOptions;\n+use crate::{backend::Backend, config::Config, file, timeout};\n+use async_trait::async_trait;\n+use eyre::{Result, bail, eyre};\n+use itertools::Itertools;\n+\n+pub(crate) const EXPERIMENTAL: bool = true;\n+\n+/// Compiles Ruby command-line tools from a GitHub repository with\n+/// [Spinel](https://github.com/matz/spinel), matz's Ruby AOT compiler.\n+#[derive(Debug)]\n+pub(crate) struct SpinelBackend {\n+    ba: Arc<BackendArg>,\n+}\n+\n+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\n+struct SpinelOptions<'a> {\n+    values: BackendOptions<'a>,\n+}\n+\n+impl<'a> SpinelOptions<'a> {\n+    fn new(raw: &'a ToolVersionOptions) -> Self {\n+        Self {\n+            values: BackendOptions::new(raw),\n+        }\n+    }\n+\n+    fn tag_prefix(&self) -> &'a str {\n+        self.values.str(\"tag_prefix\").unwrap_or(\"\")\n+    }\n+\n+    fn entrypoint(&self) -> &'a str {\n+        self.values.str(\"entrypoint\").unwrap_or(\"main.rb\")\n+    }\n+\n+    fn bin(&self) -> Option<&'a str> {\n+        self.values.str(\"bin\")\n+    }\n+\n+    fn source_ref(&self) -> Option<&'a str> {\n+        self.values.str(\"source_ref\")\n+    }\n+\n+    fn compiler(&self) -> &'a str {\n+        self.values.str(\"spinel\").unwrap_or(\"spinel\")\n+    }\n+}\n+\n+#[async_trait]\n+impl Backend for SpinelBackend {\n+    fn get_type(&self) -> BackendType {\n+        BackendType::Spinel\n+    }\n+\n+    fn ba(&self) -> &Arc<BackendArg> {\n+        &self.ba\n+    }\n+\n+    fn supports_lockfile_url(&self) -> bool {\n+        false\n+    }\n+\n+    fn remote_version_listing_tool_option_keys(&self) -> &'static [&'static str] {\n+        &[\"tag_prefix\"]\n+    }\n+\n+    fn get_dependencies(&self) -> Result<Vec<&str>> {\n+        Ok(vec![\"spinel\"])\n+    }\n+\n+    async fn _list_remote_versions(&self, config: &Arc<Config>) -> Result<Vec<VersionInfo>> {\n+        let repo = validate_repo(&self.tool_name())?;\n+        let opts = config.get_tool_opts_with_overrides(self.ba()).await?;\n+        let prefix = SpinelOptions::new(&opts).tag_prefix().to_string();\n+        let remote = repo_url(&repo);\n+        timeout::run_with_timeout_async(\n+            async || {\n+                // git ls-remote rather than the GitHub API: no token, no rate limit.\n+                let output = crate::cmd::cmd_read_async_inherited_env(\n+                    \"git\",\n+                    &[\"ls-remote\", \"--tags\", \"--refs\", &remote],\n+                    std::iter::empty::<(&str, &std::ffi::OsStr)>(),\n+                )\n+                .await?;\n+                Ok(versions_from_ls_remote(&output, &prefix))\n+            },\n+            Settings::get().fetch_remote_versions_timeout(),\n+        )\n+        .await\n+    }\n+\n+    async fn install_version_(\n+        &self,\n+        ctx: &crate::install_context::InstallContext,\n+        tv: crate::toolset::ToolVersion,\n+    ) -> Result<crate::toolset::ToolVersion> {\n+        if cfg!(windows) {\n+            bail!(\"the spinel backend does not support Windows\");\n+        }\n+        let request_opts = tv.request.options();\n+        if SpinelOptions::new(&request_opts)\n+            .values\n+            .str(\"spinel\")\n+            .is_none()\n+        {\n+            self.warn_if_dependency_missing(\n+                &ctx.config,\n+                \"spinel\",\n+                &[\"spinel\"],\n+                \"The spinel backend needs the Spinel compiler on PATH.\\n\\\n+Build it from https://github.com/matz/spinel (`make deps && make`),\\n\\\n+or point the `spinel` tool option at a compiler binary.\",\n+            )\n+            .await;\n+        }\n+\n+        let repo = validate_repo(&self.tool_name())?;\n+        let opts = SpinelOptions::new(&request_opts);\n+        let entrypoint = validate_entrypoint(opts.entrypoint())?;\n+        let bin = match opts.bin() {\n+            Some(bin) => bin.to_string(),\n+            None => repo\n+                .rsplit('/')\n+                .next()\n+                .expect(\"repo has an owner/name form\")\n+                .to_string(),\n+        };\n+        validate_bin(&bin)?;\n+        let git_ref = match opts.source_ref() {\n+            Some(sha) => validate_sha(sha)?.to_string(),\n+            None => {\n+                let tag = format!(\"{}{}\", opts.tag_prefix(), tv.version);\n+                validate_tag(&tag)?;\n+                format!(\"refs/tags/{tag}\")\n+            }\n+        };\n+\n+        let source = tv.download_path().join(\"source\");\n+        // A kept download directory must not leak files from an earlier build.\n+        file::remove_all(&source)?;\n+        let bin_dir = tv.install_path().join(\"bin\");\n+        file::create_dir_all(&source)?;\n+        file::create_dir_all(&bin_dir)?;\n+\n+        let git = |args: &[&str]| -> Result<()> {\n+            CmdLineRunner::new(\"git\")\n+                .args(args)\n+                .current_dir(&source)\n+                .with_pr(ctx.pr.as_ref())\n+                .execute()\n+        };\n+        git(&[\"init\", \"-q\"])?;\n+        git(&[\"fetch\", \"-q\", \"--depth\", \"1\", &repo_url(&repo), &git_ref])?;\n+        git(&[\"checkout\", \"-q\", \"--detach\", \"FETCH_HEAD\"])?;\n+\n+        let compiler = self\n+            .spawn_program(&ctx.config, Some(&ctx.ts), opts.compiler())\n+            .await;\n+        CmdLineRunner::new(compiler)\n+            .arg(entrypoint)\n+            .arg(\"-o\")\n+            .arg(bin_dir.join(&bin))\n+            .current_dir(&source)\n+            .with_pr(ctx.pr.as_ref())\n+            .envs(self.dependency_env(&ctx.config).await?)\n+            .env_values(tv.install_env())\n+            .execute()?;\n+        if !is_executable(&bin_dir.join(&bin)) {\n+            bail!(\"spinel did not produce an executable at bin/{bin}\");\n+        }\n+        Ok(tv)\n+    }\n+}\n+\n+impl SpinelBackend {\n+    pub(crate) fn from_arg(ba: BackendArg) -> Self {\n+        Self { ba: Arc::new(ba) }\n+    }\n+}\n+\n+/// Options that only matter while building, so a cached lockfile entry never\n+/// pins them.\n+pub(crate) fn install_time_option_keys() -> Vec<String> {\n+    vec![\"spinel\".into()]\n+}\n+\n+fn repo_url(repo: &str) -> String {\n+    format!(\"https://github.com/{repo}.git\")\n+}\n+\n+fn validate_repo(tool: &str) -> Result<String> {\n+    let valid = regex::Regex::new(r\"^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+/[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+$\")\n+        .expect(\"static regex\")\n+        .is_match(tool)\n+        && !tool.contains(\"..\");\n+    if !valid {\n+        bail!(\"expected spinel:owner/repo, got spinel:{tool}\");\n+    }\n+    Ok(tool.to_string())\n+}\n+\n+fn validate_bin(bin: &str) -> Result<()> {\n+    let valid = !bin.is_empty()\n+        && bin != \".\"\n+        && bin != \"..\"\n+        && bin\n+            .chars()\n+            .all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(c, '_' | '.' | '-'));\n+    if !valid {\n+        bail!(\"`bin` must be a plain file name, got {bin:?}\");\n+    }\n+    Ok(())\n+}\n+\n+/// The entrypoint is handed to the compiler inside the checkout, so keep it a\n+/// relative path that stays there.\n+fn validate_entrypoint(path: &str) -> Result<&str> {\n+    let escapes = path.is_empty()\n+        || path.starts_with('/')\n+        || path.starts_with('-')\n+        || path.contains('\\\\')\n+        || path.split('/').any(|part| part == \"..\" || part.is_empty());\n+    if escapes {\n+        bail!(\"`entrypoint` must be a relative path inside the repository, got {path:?}\");\n+    }\n+    Ok(path)\n+}\n+\n+fn validate_sha(sha: &str) -> Result<&str> {\n+    if sha.len() != 40 || !sha.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {\n+        bail!(\"`source_ref` must be a full 40-character commit SHA, got {sha:?}\");\n+    }\n+    Ok(sha)\n+}\n+\n+fn validate_tag(tag: &str) -> Result<()> {\n+    if tag.starts_with('-') || tag.contains(\"..\") || tag.chars().any(|c| c.is_whitespace()) {\n+        return Err(eyre!(\"invalid git tag {tag:?}\"));\n+    }\n+    Ok(())\n+}\n+\n+#[cfg(unix)]\n+fn is_executable(path: &Path) -> bool {\n+    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;\n+    path.metadata()\n+        .is_ok_and(|m| m.is_file() && m.permissions().mode() & 0o111 != 0)\n+}\n+\n+#[cfg(not(unix))]\n+fn is_executable(path: &Path) -> bool {\n+    path.is_file()\n+}\n+\n+/// Parses `git ls-remote --tags --refs` output into opaque versions, keeping\n+/// only tags that start with `prefix` and removing it.\n+fn versions_from_ls_remote(output: &str, prefix: &str) -> Vec<VersionInfo> {\n+    output\n+        .lines()\n+        .filter_map(|line| line.split_once('\\t'))\n+        .filter_map(|(_, git_ref)| git_ref.strip_prefix(\"refs/tags/\"))\n+        .filter_map(|tag| tag.strip_prefix(prefix))\n+        .filter(|version| !version.is_empty())\n+        .unique()\n+        .map(|version| VersionInfo {\n+            version: version.to_string(),\n+            ..Default::default()\n+        })\n+        .collect()\n+}\n+\n+#[cfg(test)]\n+mod tests {\n+    use super::*;\n+\n+    #[test]\n+    fn lists_prefixed_tags_without_the_prefix() {\n+        let output = \"aaa\\trefs/tags/v1.0.0\\nbbb\\trefs/tags/v1.1.0\\nccc\\trefs/tags/nightly\\n\";\n+        let versions: Vec<_> = versions_from_ls_remote(output, \"v\")\n+            .into_iter()\n+            .map(|v| v.version)\n+            .collect();\n+        assert_eq!(versions, [\"1.0.0\", \"1.1.0\"]);\n+        let all: Vec<_> = versions_from_ls_remote(output, \"\")\n+            .into_iter()\n+            .map(|v| v.version)\n+            .collect();\n+        assert_eq!(all, [\"v1.0.0\", \"v1.1.0\", \"nightly\"]);\n+    }\n+\n+    #[test]\n+    fn validates_repo_entrypoint_bin_and_sha() {\n+        assert!(validate_repo(\"tobi/try\").is_ok());\n+        assert!(validate_repo(\"tobi\").is_err());\n+        assert!(validate_repo(\"a/../b\").is_err());\n+        assert!(validate_entrypoint(\"try.rb\").is_ok());\n+        assert!(validate_entrypoint(\"bin/try.rb\").is_ok());\n+        for bad in [\"\", \"/etc/x.rb\", \"../x.rb\", \"a//b.rb\", \"-x.rb\", \"a\\\\b.rb\"] {\n+            assert!(validate_entrypoint(bad).is_err(), \"{bad}\");\n+        }\n+        assert!(validate_bin(\"try\").is_ok());\n+        assert!(validate_bin(\"a/b\").is_err());\n+        assert!(validate_bin(\"..\").is_err());\n+        assert!(validate_sha(&\"a\".repeat(40)).is_ok());\n+        assert!(validate_sha(\"abc\").is_err());\n+        assert!(validate_tag(\"v1.0.0\").is_ok());\n+        assert!(validate_tag(\"-x\").is_err());\n+    }\n+}"
    },

      "patch": "@@ -903,6 +903,22 @@ where\n             }\n         }\n     }\n+    // A positional `cmd /c <command>`, which is how pitchfork runs a `mise = true`\n+    // daemon, also needs its command verbatim once it contains a `\"`.\n+    if !shell_body_mode\n+        && let Some(prog) = program.to_str()\n+        && let Some(args) = args\n+            .iter()\n+            .map(|a| a.to_str().map(str::to_string))\n+            .collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()\n+        && let Some((switches, command)) = crate::path::positional_cmd_command(&args)\n+        && let Some(mut c) = crate::path::cmd_verbatim_command(prog, switches, command)\n+    {\n+        match c.status()?.code() {\n+            Some(code) => return Err(crate::request_exit(code)),\n+            None => return Err(eyre!(\"command failed: terminated by signal\")),\n+        }\n+    }\n \n     // libuv adds a CRT table for every Node child. Only an IPC child has both the Node channel\n     // marker and a matching pipe descriptor, so keep the normal duct path for all other commands"
    },

      "patch": "@@ -53,34 +53,11 @@ impl HookEnv {\n             self.shell,\n             &format!(\"Name the shell: `mise hook-env --shell {EXAMPLE_SHELL}`.\"),\n         )?;\n-        let config = match Config::get().await {\n-            Ok(config) => config,\n-            Err(err) => {\n-                let Some(config_path) = hook_env::untrusted_config_error_path(&err) else {\n-                    return Err(err);\n-                };\n-                if hook_env::should_show_untrusted_config_warning(&config_path) {\n-                    if let Err(mark_err) =\n- Explicit commands still raise the full UntrustedConfig error.\n-                    // Written directly to stderr because the untrusted config's own\n-                    // [settings] (e.g. quiet, log_level) are applied before the trust\n-                    // check and must not be able to silence this notice.\n-                    safe_eprintln!(\n-                        \"{} {} {} is not explicit action, so an untrusted config is\n+        // skipped (with a warning) rather than blocking the configs that are trusted.\n+        crate::config::skip_untrusted_configs();\n+        let config = Config::get().await?;\n+        let untrusted_configs = crate::config::skipped_untrusted_configs();\n         // Shell activation must stay fast and non-networked; missing tools are\n }\n-        hook_env::clear_untrusted_config_warning(&mut patches);\n+        if untrusted_configs.is_empty() {\n+            hook_env::clear_untrusted_config_warning(&mut patches);\n+        }\n \n         let output = hook_env::build_env_commands(&*shell, &patches);\n         miseprint!(\"{output}\")?;\n+        if !untrusted_configs.is_empty()\n+            && hook_env::should_show_untrusted_config_warning(&untrusted_configs)\n+        {\n+            if let Err(mark_err) =\n+                hook_env::mark_untrusted_config_warning_seen(&*shell, &untrusted_configs)\n+            {\n+                trace!(\"failed to mark untrusted config warning seen: {mark_err}\");\n+            }\n+            // Written directly to stderr because the untrusted config's own\n+            // [settings] (e.g. quiet, log_level) are applied before the trust\n+            // check and must not be able to silence this notice.\n+            for config_path in &untrusted_configs {\n+                safe_eprintln!(\n+                    \"{} {} {} is not trusted, run `mise trust` to enable it\",\n+                    style::eyellow(\"mise\"),\n+                    style::eyellow(\"WARN\"),\n+                    display_path(config_path)\n+                );\n+            }\n+        }\n       "patch": "@@ -88,11 +88,16 @@ pub(crate) struct LsRemote {\n \n impl LsRemote {\n     pub(crate) async fn run(self) -> Result<()> {\n-        if self.prerelease {\n-            Settings::override_with(|s| s.prereleases = Some(true));\n-        }\n-        if self.no_versions_host {\n-            Settings::override_with(|s| s.use_versions_host = Some(false));\n+        if self.prerelease || self.no_versions_host {\n+            // One override, one rebuild: each `override_with` loads settings again.\n+            Settings::override_with(|s| {\n+                if self.prerelease {\n+                    s.prereleases = Some(true);\n+                }\n+                if self.no_versions_host {\n+                    s.use_versions_host = Some(false);\n+                }\n+            })?;\n         }\n         backend::set_strict_metadata(self.strict_metadata);\n         let config = Config::get().await?;"

      "patch": "@@ -35,6 +35,9 @@ pub(crate) fn init_global_only() {\n /// Merge the system and global miserc files, skipping project files found\n /// from the working directory.\n fn load_global_miserc_settings() -> MisercSettings {\n+    if super::Settings::no_config() {\n+        return MisercSettings::default();\n+    }\n     let mut settings = MisercSettings::default();\n     // A broken file must not block credentials or discard another valid layer.\n     for path in [\n@@ -80,7 +83,7 @@ pub(crate) fn invocation_cwd() -> Option<&'static Path> {\n \n /// Get the loaded miserc settings, or default if not initialized.\n pub(crate) fn get() -> &'static MisercSettings {\n-    if super::Settings::is_package_query() {\n+    if super::Settings::is_package_query() || super::Settings::no_config() {\n         static QUERY_MISERC: std::sync::LazyLock<MisercSettings> =\n             std::sync::LazyLock::new(MisercSettings::default);\n         return &QUERY_MISERC;\n@@ -122,6 +125,9 @@ pub(crate) fn get_ignored_config_paths() -> Option<&'static BTreeSet<PathBuf>> {\n /// directory mise runs from, so machine-wide operations such as `mise prune`\n /// reach the same verdict everywhere.\n pub(crate) fn get_global_ignored_config_paths() -> Option<&'static BTreeSet<PathBuf>> {\n+    if super::Settings::no_config() {\n+        return None;\n+    }\n     static GLOBAL: std::sync::LazyLock<Option<BTreeSet<PathBuf>>> =\n         std::sync::LazyLock::new(|| {\n             let settings = load_global_miserc_settings();\n@@ -192,6 +198,9 @@ fn render_miserc_template(\n /// 2. Global ~/.config/mise/miserc.local.toml, then miserc.toml\n /// 3. System /etc/mise/miserc.toml\n fn load_miserc_settings() -> Result<MisercSettings> {\n+    if super::Settings::no_config() {\n+        return Ok(MisercSettings::default());\n+    }\n     load_miserc_files(find_miserc_files())\n }\n "
    },

      "patch": "@@ -9,6 +9,7 @@ use std::env::join_paths;\n use std::fmt::{Debug, Formatter};\n use std::path::{Component, Path, PathBuf};\n use std::sync::LazyLock as Lazy;\n+use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};\n use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex, RwLock};\n use std::time::{Duration, SystemTime};\n use tokio::{sync::OnceCell, task::JoinSet};\n@@ -265,7 +266,7 @@ impl Config {\n             Duration::from_secs(5),\n         )\n         .await?;\n-        Settings::reload();\n+        Settings::reload()?;\n         Config::load().await\n     }\n \n@@ -3542,6 +3543,21 @@ fn declined_trust_prompt(path: &Path, err: &eyre::Report) -> bool {\n     ) && (config_file::is_ignored(&config_trust_root(path)) || config_file::is_ignored(path))\n }\n \n+static SKIP_UNTRUSTED_CONFIGS: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);\n+static SKIPPED_UNTRUSTED_CONFIGS: Mutex<Vec<PathBuf>> = Mutex::new(Vec::new());\n+\n+/// Makes config loading skip untrusted config files instead of failing, so the\n+/// remaining (e.g. global) configs still apply. Used by `hook-env`, where entering\n+/// a directory is not an explicit request to run its config.\n+pub fn skip_untrusted_configs() {\n+    SKIP_UNTRUSTED_CONFIGS.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);\n+}\n+\n+/// Untrusted config files skipped so far when [`skip_untrusted_configs`] is active.\n+pub fn skipped_untrusted_configs() -> Vec<PathBuf> {\n+    SKIPPED_UNTRUSTED_CONFIGS.lock().unwrap().clone()\n+}\n+\n async fn load_all_config_files(\n     config_filenames: &[PathBuf],\n     idiomatic_filenames: &BTreeMap<String, Vec<String>>,\n@@ -3583,6 +3599,20 @@ async fn load_all_config_files(\n                     );\n                     continue;\n                 }\n+                Err(err)\n+                    if SKIP_UNTRUSTED_CONFIGS.load(Ordering::Relaxed)\n+                        && matches!(\n+                            err.downcast_ref::<crate::errors::Error>(),\n+                            Some(crate::errors::Error::UntrustedConfig(_))\n+                        ) =>\n+                {\n+                    debug!(\"skipping untrusted config file: {}\", display_path(f));\n+                    let mut skipped = SKIPPED_UNTRUSTED_CONFIGS.lock().unwrap();\n+                    if !skipped.contains(f) {\n+                        skipped.push(f.clone());\n+                    }\n+                    continue;\n+                }\n                 Err(err) => {\n                     return Err(err.wrap_err(format!(\n                         \"error parsing config file: {}\","

the exit has to cope with an empty cache.\n+fn rebuild() -> Result<()> {\n+    let settings = load().wrap_err(\"failed to reload settings\")?;\n+    mise_settings::store(settings);\n+    Ok(())\n+}\n+\n /// Build settings from every source. Registered with [`mise_settings::set_loader`], so\n /// [`Settings::try_get`] runs this, and caches the result, when nothing is cached.\n fn load() -> Result<Arc<Settings>> {\n@@ -2694,10 +2716,35 @@ mod tests {\n         Settings::reset(Some(partial));\n         assert!(Settings::get().offline());\n \n-        Settings::reload();\n+        Settings::reload().unwrap();\n         assert!(Settings::get().offline());\n     }\n \n+    /// A reload that cannot build settings reports the error and keeps the previous settings,\n+    /// rather than emptying the cache for the next `Settings::get()` to unwrap (#13923).\n+    #[test]\n+    fn test_failed_reload_keeps_previous_settings() {\n+        let _settings = crate::test::SettingsGuard::lock();\n+        Settings::reset(None);\n+        let before = Settings::get();\n+\n+        let mut env = crate::test::EnvVarGuard::new();\n+        env.set(\"MISE_YES\", \"maybe\");\n+        let err = Settings::reload().unwrap_err();\n+        assert!(format!(\"{err:#}\").contains(\"MISE_YES\"), \"{err:#}\");\n+        let err = Settings::override_with(|s| s.offline = Some(true)).unwrap_err();\n+        assert!(format!(\"{err:#}\").contains(\"MISE_YES\"), \"{err:#}\");\n+\n+        let after = Settings::get();\n+        assert!(Arc::ptr_eq(&before, &after));\n+\n+        // The override that could not be applied is not waiting in the CLI layer for the next\n+        // reload to pick it up.\n+        drop(env);\n+        Settings::reload().unwrap();\n+        assert!(!Settings::get().offline);\n+    }\n+\n     #[test]\n     fn test_offline_implies_prefer_offline() {\n         let _settings = crate::test::SettingsGuard::lock();"
